quasar-cost 7 Cost model; Coster trait; per-operator cost fns
quasar-search 10 Cascades B&B engine; task stack; winner extraction
quasar-distsql 12 Physical plan → FlowSpec; node placement; LOS
quasar-cache 13 Plan cache; prepared stmts; placeholder generic/custom
quasar-obs 13 Observability; event log; deterministic replay

11. Core Schema and Core Types

Normative API surface. These names are the canonical vocabulary referenced by every phase (§12–§25). Signatures only (RULE 10): bodies are described in prose, never implemented here. Semantics columns: Own = ownership/arena; Mut = mutation window; Conc = Send/Sync posture; Persist = ephemeral / cached / read-only-input; Ser = serialized for EXPLAIN/replay/cache?; Compat = stability tier (Stable wire/ondisk vs Internal/debug).

11.1 Identifiers (quasar-ir::ids)

#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Ord, PartialOrd)] pub struct ColumnId(pub u32);
pub struct TableId(u32); pub struct IndexId(u32); pub struct FuncId(u32);
pub struct GroupId(u32); pub struct ExprId(u32); pub struct ScalarId(u32);
pub struct RuleId(u16); pub struct RegionId(u16); pub struct NodeId(u32); pub struct RangeId(u64);
pub struct PlaceholderIdx(u16);
pub struct ColSet(/* compact bitset over ColumnId: inline u64 + spill bitvec */);
impl ColSet { fn contains(self, c: ColumnId)->bool; fn union(self,o:Self)->Self; fn intersect(..); fn is_subset_of(..); }
Type Own Mut Conc Persist Ser Compat
ColumnId/TableId/IndexId Copy value immutable Send+Sync catalog-scoped yes Stable within a catalog epoch
GroupId/ExprId/ScalarId Copy index into a Memo arena immutable once minted Send+Sync (just u32) ephemeral (per-Memo) memo-dump only Internal — meaningless across memos
ColSet inline value, no heap until spill rebuilt functionally Send+Sync ephemeral yes (cols) Internal

Rule: arena indices (GroupId/ExprId/ScalarId) are valid only within their owning Memo; never serialize raw or compare across memos. ColumnId is catalog-stable and may cross boundaries.

11.2 Relational + scalar algebra (quasar-ir)

pub enum RelExpr {
  Scan(ScanOp), IndexScan(IndexScanOp), Select{input:GroupId, filters:ScalarId}, Project{input:GroupId, projections:Vec<(ColumnId,ScalarId)>, passthrough:ColSet},
  InnerJoin(JoinOp), LeftJoin(JoinOp), SemiJoin(JoinOp), AntiJoin(JoinOp),
  HashJoin(JoinOp), MergeJoin(MergeJoinOp), LookupJoin(LookupJoinOp), InvertedJoin(InvertedJoinOp), ZigzagJoin(ZigzagJoinOp),
  GroupBy{input:GroupId, grouping_cols:ColSet, aggregations:Vec<AggExpr>}, ScalarGroupBy{input:GroupId, aggregations:Vec<AggExpr>},
  Distinct{input:GroupId, cols:ColSet}, Sort{input:GroupId, ordering:OrderingChoice}, Limit{input:GroupId, n:ScalarId, offset:ScalarId},
  UnionAll(SetOp), Union(SetOp), Intersect(SetOp), Except(SetOp), Values{rows:Vec<Vec<ScalarId>>, cols:ColSet}, WithScan{id:CteId},
  Window(WindowOp), Ordinality{input:GroupId},
  Insert(MutationOp), Update(MutationOp), Delete(MutationOp), Upsert(MutationOp),
  Distribute{input:GroupId, distribution:Distribution},  // physical enforcer
  SortEnforcer{input:GroupId, ordering:OrderingChoice},   // physical enforcer
}
pub enum ScalarExpr {
  Column(ColumnId), Const(Datum), Placeholder(PlaceholderIdx),
  And(Vec<ScalarId>), Or(Vec<ScalarId>), Not(ScalarId), Comparison{op:CmpOp, l:ScalarId, r:ScalarId},
  BinOp{op:ArithOp, l:ScalarId, r:ScalarId}, UnaryOp{op:UnOp, arg:ScalarId}, Function{id:FuncId, args:Vec<ScalarId>},
  Cast{expr:ScalarId, typ:DataType}, Case{branches:Vec<(ScalarId,ScalarId)>, els:Option<ScalarId>}, Coalesce(Vec<ScalarId>),
  Tuple(Vec<ScalarId>), Array(Vec<ScalarId>), Subquery(GroupId), Exists(GroupId), AnyAll{cmp:CmpOp, scalar:ScalarId, sub:GroupId},
}
pub struct ScanOp { table:TableId, cols:ColSet, hard_limit:Option<u64>, soft_limit:Option<u64> }
pub struct IndexScanOp { table:TableId, index:IndexId, constraint:Constraint, remaining:ScalarId, reverse:bool, cols:ColSet }
pub struct JoinOp { left:GroupId, right:GroupId, on:ScalarId, kind:JoinKind }
pub struct LookupJoinOp { input:GroupId, table:TableId, index:IndexId, key_cols:Vec<ColumnId>, on:ScalarId, kind:JoinKind }
pub enum Operator { /* flat tag union of every RelExpr + ScalarExpr variant — the memo intern key discriminant */ }
  • Own/Mut: RelExpr/ScalarExpr are constructed by quasar-bind, then immutable once interned into a Memo (children become GroupId/ScalarId). Pre-interning they own boxed children; post-interning they own only arena indices.
  • Conc: Send+Sync while detached (bound tree handoff between threads is legal); inside a Memo they live in a non-Sync arena.
  • Persist/Ser: the bound logical tree serializes for the plan-cache fingerprint and replay bundle; physical/enforcer variants serialize into FlowSpec rendering only. Compat: logical RelExpr shape is Internal (may grow variants additively); Datum/DataType are Stable.

11.3 Memo (quasar-memo)

pub struct Memo { groups:Arena<Group>, exprs:Arena<MExpr>, scalars:Arena<ScalarExpr>,
  intern:HashMap<MExprKey,ExprId>, root:GroupId, budget:Cost, stats_epoch:u64 }
pub struct Group { id:GroupId, members:Vec<ExprId>, logical:LogicalProps,
  winners:HashMap<PhysProps,Winner>, explored:bool, state:GroupState }
pub struct MExpr { op:Operator, children:SmallVec<[GroupId;3]>, scalar:Option<ScalarId>, rules_applied:RuleMask }
pub struct Winner { expr:ExprId, cost:Cost, provided:PhysProps }
pub enum GroupState { Unexplored, Exploring, Explored }
struct MExprKey { op:Operator, children:SmallVec<[GroupId;3]>, scalar:Option<ScalarId> }  // intern key (I1)
impl Memo {
  fn add(&mut self, e:RelExpr) -> GroupId;          // interns + runs Normalize factory hook (§16)
  fn group(&self, g:GroupId) -> &Group;
  fn intern_expr(&mut self, op:Operator, ch:&[GroupId], sc:Option<ScalarId>) -> ExprId;  // dedup via MExprKey
  fn set_winner(&mut self, g:GroupId, req:&PhysProps, w:Winner);
}
Type Own Mut Conc Persist Ser Compat
Memo owns all three arenas + intern map append-mostly during build/search; frozen after extract (I9) Send, not Sync (single-thread optimize per query) ephemeral, per-query dump only Internal
Group owned by Memo members append; winners updated during search — ephemeral memo-dump Internal
MExpr owned by Memo immutable post-intern; rules_applied bitset mutated to dedup rule firings — ephemeral memo-dump Internal

Invariants: MExprKey uniqueness ⇒ structural dedup (I1); all members of a Group share one LogicalProps (I2); no mutation after winner extraction (I9).

11.4 Properties (quasar-props)

pub struct LogicalProps { out_cols:ColSet, not_null:ColSet, fd:FuncDepSet, cardinality:Cardinality,
  outer_cols:ColSet, keys:Vec<ColSet>, stats:Statistics }
pub struct FuncDepSet { key:Option<ColSet>, equiv:EquivGroups, constants:ColSet, deps:Vec<FuncDep> }
pub struct FuncDep { from:ColSet, to:ColSet, strict:bool }
pub struct Cardinality { min:u64, max:u64 }            // max == u64::MAX ⇒ unbounded
pub struct PhysProps { ordering:OrderingChoice, distribution:Distribution, limit_hint:f64 }
pub struct OrderingChoice { cols:Vec<OrderingCol> }    // FD-simplified; canonical for hashing
pub struct OrderingCol { col:ColumnId, desc:bool }
pub enum Distribution { Any, Single(RegionId), Set(SmallVec<[RegionId;4]>) }
impl PhysProps { fn satisfies(&self, required:&PhysProps, fds:&FuncDepSet) -> bool; fn any() -> Self; }
impl FuncDepSet { fn closure(&self, cols:ColSet) -> ColSet; fn add_equiv(&mut self, a:ColumnId, b:ColumnId); }
impl OrderingChoice { fn simplify(&mut self, fds:&FuncDepSet); }  // drop constants, collapse equivalents (I6)
  • Own/Mut: LogicalProps is derived once per group at creation, then immutable. PhysProps are small Copy-ish value keys used as HashMap keys for winners (must hash canonically — hence OrderingChoice is FD-simplified before use).
  • Conc: Send+Sync. Ser: appear in EXPLAIN(OPT)/memo-dump. Compat: Internal/debug surface.
  • Invariants: FD closure consistent (I11); OrderingChoice ⊆ FD-implied closure (I6); distribution of root winner = required (I12).

11.5 Statistics (quasar-stats)

pub struct Statistics { row_count:f64, col_stats:HashMap<ColSet,ColumnStatistic>, selectivity:f64, is_estimated:bool }
pub struct ColumnStatistic { distinct_count:f64, null_count:f64, histogram:Option<Histogram>, avg_size:f64 }
pub struct Histogram { buckets:Vec<Bucket> }           // equi-depth
pub struct Bucket { upper_bound:Datum, num_eq:f64, num_range:f64, distinct_range:f64 }
pub fn selectivity(pred:&ScalarExpr, stats:&Statistics) -> f64;   // [0,1]; never panics on missing stats
Type Own Mut Conc Persist Ser Compat
Statistics embedded in LogicalProps derived once; is_estimated set when degraded Send+Sync computed ephemeral; sourced from read-only stat catalog EXPLAIN(OPT) Internal
ColumnStatistic/Histogram/Bucket stat catalog immutable input Send+Sync read-only input, versioned by stats_epoch on-disk stat format Stable on-disk (versioned)

Missing-stats path returns bounded heuristics with is_estimated=true (never panic, never unbounded allocation).

11.6 Constraints (quasar-constraint)

pub struct Constraint { cols:Vec<ColumnId>, spans:Spans, tight:bool }   // tight ⇒ remaining filter empty
pub struct Spans(Vec<Span>);                                            // disjoint, sorted, normalized
pub struct Span { start:Key, start_boundary:Boundary, end:Key, end_boundary:Boundary }
pub enum Boundary { Inclusive, Exclusive }
pub struct Key(Vec<Datum>);                                             // index-key prefix
pub fn analyze(filter:&ScalarExpr, index:&IndexDesc) -> (Constraint, /*remaining*/ ScalarId);
  • Own/Mut: value types built by the solver, immutable after. Conc: Send+Sync. Ser: rendered in EXPLAIN(OPT) and index-constraints goldens. Compat: Internal (key encoding Internal).
  • Spans always normalized (disjoint + ascending); empty Spans ⇒ contradiction (ConstraintUnsat surfaces as a guaranteed-empty scan).

11.7 Cost (quasar-cost)

pub struct Cost { cpu:f64, io:f64, net:f64, total:f64 }   // total is the ONLY comparison key
impl Cost { fn combine(self, child:Cost) -> Cost; fn is_finite(self) -> bool; const INF:Cost; }
impl PartialOrd for Cost { /* by .total; NaN treated as INF */ }
pub trait Coster {
  fn cost(&self, memo:&Memo, expr:ExprId, required:&PhysProps) -> Cost;          // local op cost
  fn lower_bound(&self, memo:&Memo, group:GroupId, required:&PhysProps) -> Cost; // B&B pruning bound (§22)
}
pub struct BaseCoster { coeffs:CostCoeffs }   // cpu/io/seek/net unit costs, loaded from costs.toml
  • Own/Mut: Coster is &self (read-only); BaseCoster holds calibrated coefficients. Conc: Coster: Send+Sync (shared across the single-query search; trait object Box<dyn Coster>). Ser: per-node cost printed in EXPLAIN(OPT). Compat: Internal; costs.toml is an operator-tunable input.
  • Invariant: cost monotonicity (I4) — combine never lowers total below child totals; lower_bound ≤ any achievable cost.

11.8 Rules (quasar-rules / quasar-optgen)

pub enum RuleKind { Normalize, Explore, Implement }
pub struct RuleDef { id:RuleId, name:&'static str, kind:RuleKind }   // generated table from .opt
pub struct RuleMask(u128);                                           // per-MExpr "already fired" bitset
// .opt source shape (compiled at build time by quasar-optgen):
//   [EliminateAndAnd, Normalize] (And (And $a $b) $c) => (And $a $b $c)
//   [GenerateIndexScan, Explore]  (Select (Scan $t) $f) => (IndexScan $t (Constraint $f) ...)
//   [ImplementInnerJoinHash, Implement] (InnerJoin $l $r $on) => (HashJoin $l $r $on)
pub fn dispatch(memo:&mut Memo, expr:ExprId, rule:RuleId) -> SmallVec<[ExprId;4]>;  // generated
  • Own/Mut: rule tables are static (generated, immutable). RuleMask lives on MExpr, mutated to prevent re-firing a rule on the same expression. Conc: generated dispatch is pure given &mut Memo. Ser: rule-firing trace in EXPLAIN(OPT)/event log. Compat: RuleId numbering is Internal; rule names are a Stable operator-facing surface (rule-disable list, Operational Readiness).
  • Invariant: Normalize rules confluent + idempotent (I3); codegen deterministic (same .opt → byte-identical Rust).

11.9 Search (quasar-search)

pub enum Task {
  OptimizeGroup{g:GroupId, req:PhysProps}, OptimizeExpr{e:ExprId, req:PhysProps},
  ExploreGroup{g:GroupId}, ExploreExpr{e:ExprId},
  ApplyRule{e:ExprId, r:RuleId}, EnforceProps{g:GroupId, req:PhysProps},
}
pub struct Optimizer { memo:Memo, coster:Box<dyn Coster>, stack:Vec<Task>, budget:Cost, spent:Cost, seed:u64, reorder_limit:u8 }
impl Optimizer {
  pub fn new(memo:Memo, coster:Box<dyn Coster>, cfg:OptConfig) -> Self;
  pub fn optimize(&mut self, req:PhysProps) -> Result<PhysicalPlan, OptError>;   // drives the task loop
  fn extract(&self, root:GroupId, req:&PhysProps) -> PhysicalPlan;               // winner tree → plan
}
  • Own/Mut: Optimizer owns the Memo for the query lifetime and the explicit stack (no recursion → bounded stack depth). Conc: Send, not Sync; one Optimizer per query. Persist: fully ephemeral. Ser: none (the produced PhysicalPlan/FlowSpec serialize). Compat: Internal.
  • Invariants: termination under budget (BudgetExceeded fallback); B&B optimal within budget (I7); deterministic given seed (I10).

11.10 DistSQL (quasar-distsql) and the output contract

pub struct PhysicalPlan { root:PhysicalNode }                 // optimizer output, pre-distribution
pub struct FlowSpec { processors:Vec<ProcessorSpec>, streams:Vec<Stream>, gateway:NodeId }
pub struct ProcessorSpec { id:ProcId, node:NodeId, core:ProcessorCore, post:PostProcessSpec, output:Vec<OutputRouter> }
pub enum ProcessorCore { TableReader{spans:Spans, index:IndexId}, HashJoiner, MergeJoiner, Aggregator, Sorter, Distinct, Limit, Values, Noop }
pub struct OutputRouter { router:Router, dest:Vec<ProcId> }
pub enum Router { PassThrough, ByHash(Vec<ColumnId>), Mirror, ByRange(Spans) }
pub struct Stream { source:ProcId, dest:ProcId, kind:StreamKind }
pub enum StreamKind { Local, Remote{ordered:bool} }
Type Own Mut Conc Persist Ser Compat
FlowSpec + members freestanding output built once by planner, immutable Send+Sync handed to executor (project 318); cacheable yes — the wire contract Stable wire — ProcessorCore/Router variants grow additively + feature-gated (Compatibility & Versioning)

FlowSpec is the single stable boundary Quasar exposes downstream; everything upstream (Memo, Task, PhysProps) is Internal/debug.

11.11 Errors (quasar-cli/shared)

pub enum OptError {
  Unsupported{what:String, loc:Span}, NoPlanFound{goal:PhysProps}, StatsUnavailable{table:TableId},
  ConstraintUnsat, PlaceholderUnbound{idx:PlaceholderIdx}, MemoStale{expected:u64, found:u64},
  BudgetExceeded{spent:Cost, budget:Cost}, CatalogMissing{name:String},
}
// Deferred surfaces are marked at the call site, never on the core path:
fn phase_deferred(section:&str) -> !; // e.g. PhaseDeferred("FINAL_BLUEPRINT §25")
  • Conc: Send+Sync+'static (via thiserror). Ser: error string surfaces in CLI + event log. Compat: Unsupported/NoPlanFound/BudgetExceeded are recoverable (caller falls back to a naive plan per Failure Modes & Recovery); the rest abort the single optimize() call without corrupting shared state. MemoStale triggers plan-cache invalidation (§25, Compatibility & Versioning) rather than a hard error.

12. Phase 0 — Walking Skeleton — workspace, 16 crate stubs, end-to-end SELECT smoke test

0.1 Objectives

Thinnest vertical slice proving the pipeline wires end-to-end. PhaseDeferred: quasar-rules (all rules), quasar-stats, quasar-constraint, quasar-search (real search), quasar-distsql (multi-node), quasar-cache, quasar-obs (real tracing).

Goal Constraint
Cargo workspace, all 16 crates Each compiles; no todo!() on smoke path
Hardcoded catalog: t(a INT, b INT), PK on a quasar-cat stub; no persistence
quasar-sql parses exactly SELECT a FROM t WHERE a > 1 Other SQL → OptError::Unsupported
quasar-bind builds Select(Scan{t,{a}}) PhaseDeferred: subqueries/mutations/aggregates
1-member memo of bound expr No interning, dedup, rules
Trivial coster Cost{cpu,io,total=row_count=1000.0, net:0.0}
Emit one TableReader FlowSpec on NodeId(0) No distribution, no joins
quasar explain prints plan tree ASCII indented tree + cost annotation
quasar opt prints physical op name One-liner
CI: cargo build/test --workspace green No warnings on smoke path

0.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: ADR-A03 (workspace/Makefile), ADR-A01 (Rust), ADR-A20 (OptError variants), ADR-A21 (tracing stub), P12 (no execution coupling — FlowSpec boundary established here), I1 (memo append-only — trivial for single-member stub).

  • Workspace scaffold — root Cargo.toml ([workspace] members=["crates/*"], resolver="2"); crates/quasar-{cli,sql,cat,ir,bind,props,stats,memo,optgen,rules,constraint,cost,search,distsql,cache,obs}/ each with src/lib.rs (+src/main.rs for cli). Deps: smallvec, ahash, rustc-hash, thiserror, tracing, tracing-subscriber, clap, serde+serde_json (feature-gated). Makefile: build/test/run/explain.
  • quasar-ir — IDs + full RelExpr/ScalarExpr enums per §11.1–11.2 (off-smoke variants PhaseDeferred-gated); ColSet = u128 bitset (≤128 cols smoke; real bitset crate Phase 1); ScanOp/IndexScanOp/JoinOp/LookupJoinOp structs. OptError per §11.11 — smoke path uses Unsupported/NoPlanFound only; fn phase_deferred(section:&str) -> ! marks stub call sites (pub struct PhaseDeferred(pub &'static str)).
  • quasar-cat stub — Catalog::hardcoded(): t cols a INT NOT NULL(0), b INT(1); PK idx_pk on [a ASC] storing {b}. trait CatalogResolver{table,columns,indexes}; stub impls only, no persistence/zone/region data.
  • quasar-sql subset — hand-written recursive descent over EBNF stmt="SELECT" proj "FROM" ident ("WHERE" expr)?; proj=ident(","ident)*|"*"; expr=ident">"literal. Tokens Ident,IntLit,Gt,Select,From,Where → RawAst::Select{projections,from,filter}; unknown token → Unsupported.
  • quasar-bind stub — bind(ast,cat:&dyn CatalogResolver) -> Result<RelExpr,OptError>: resolves table→TableId, cols→ColumnId, builds ScalarExpr::Comparison filter inside RelExpr::Scan/Select. Correlation/subquery/aggregate → PhaseDeferred("FINAL_BLUEPRINT §13") (Deferred Features Register: "Full SQL grammar (all clauses)" row — same Phase 1 / §13 anchor covers the smoke stub's un-built binder paths).
  • Remaining crate stubs — quasar-props: LogicalProps::placeholder() (zeroed; derivation Phase 2). quasar-memo: Memo{root:GroupId,members:Vec<MExpr>}::from_expr(expr), no interning/dedup/rules hook. quasar-cost: TrivialCoster → Cost{cpu:1000.0,io:1000.0,net:0.0,total:1000.0}. quasar-search: optimize(memo,coster,req)->Result<ExprId,OptError> returns ExprId(0), no task stack. quasar-distsql: plan(memo,expr,cat)->Result<FlowSpec,OptError> returns one ProcessorSpec{core:TableReader{spans:[full],index:None},node:NodeId(0)}. quasar-cli: clap subcommands opt/explain(--opt,--distsql)/memo/opt-test/bench; unknown SQL → stderr + exit 1. quasar-obs: re-exports tracing::{info,debug,warn,error}, init_tracing(). quasar-optgen/rules/stats/constraint/cache: stub_ok() + type stubs, zero logic, PhaseDeferred in doc comments.
  • Tests — quasar-cli/tests/smoke.rs: run_opt("SELECT a FROM t WHERE a > 1").is_ok(); quasar-sql/tests/parse.rs: AST fields; quasar-bind/tests/bind.rs: Select{..Scan{..}}.

0.3 Deliverable

0.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root cause Debug signal Recovery
Crate dependency cycle cargo build cycle error ir↔memo or bind↔ir circular cargo tree quasar-common re-export layer; move shared types to ir
ColSet width overflow Panic on col index ≥128 u128 bitset limit index_oob panic Phase 1 bitset crate; smoke enforces ≤2 cols
explain flag ambiguity --opt/--distsql absent → wrong default Clap derive ordering Help on no-flag Mutually-exclusive group, exactly one required
PhaseDeferred arm silently Ok Test wrongly passes on deferred path Stub returns Ok(placeholder) #[must_use] + explicit Err Audit deferred paths → Err(Unsupported) for non-smoke
tracing_subscriber double-init Second in-process test panics Global subscriber set try_init() ignored Use try_init(), swallow AlreadyInitialized
ExprId/GroupId arithmetic confusion Wrong parent stored Integer arithmetic on newtype Newtype disallows + w/o .0 Impl only From/Into<u32>; no Add

0.7 Performance Concerns

No perf-sensitive path (hardcoded 1000-row heuristic, single member).

  • Startup alloc — clap parse + catalog ctor < 1 MB heap; audit MALLOC_CONF=stats_print:true.
  • Binary size — 16 stub crates balloon; dev opt-level='s' for CI, release opt-level=3.
  • Link time — ~3–5 s; add lld/mold in .cargo/config.toml.
  • Test parallelism — 16 per-crate test binaries saturate cores; set CARGO_BUILD_JOBS conservatively.

0.8 Edge Cases

Edge case Handling
Leading/trailing whitespace Lexer skips ASCII whitespace; no panic
Case mismatch (A vs a) Case-sensitive match; documented in fixture
No space before > (a>1) Tokenize without whitespace dependency
Empty projection SELECT FROM t Parse error → Unsupported
NodeId(u32::MAX) Hardcoded NodeId(0); no overflow
No args Clap prints help, exits 0; test asserts 0
Non-UTF8 SQL arg OsStr::to_str()→None → Unsupported pre-parse

smoke regression cargo test fail in smoke.rs None (no real data) Gate PR on CI Revert/fix broken stub

(postmortem inputs, all classes) — — — failing command + SQL input + RUST_BACKTRACE=1 + cargo/rustc version

0.10 Operational Notes

  • Tracing — QUASAR_LOG=debug quasar opt ... → JSON logs via tracing_subscriber; Phase 0 emits one info!("optimize complete") span.
  • Metrics — none; hooks Phase 13. Stub quasar_obs::metrics::noop() exported now.
  • Replay — deterministic by construction (hardcoded catalog + heuristic); no seed.
  • Deployment — single static binary; no config. Future: QUASAR_CATALOG_URL, QUASAR_STATS_URL.
  • Rollback — no state persisted; redeploy previous binary.
  • Compatibility — CLI surface (quasar opt, quasar explain) locked from this phase; subcommand additions backward-compatible; flag additions need -- guard.
  • CI order — cargo fmt --check → cargo clippy --workspace -- -D warnings → build → test → fixture runner on conformance/testdata/smoke/.

13. Phase 1 — Catalog + Bound IR — quasar-cat, quasar-ir, quasar-bind, subset quasar-sql

1.1 Objectives

Build the real front half of the pipeline, replacing all Phase 0 stubs. Acceptance: quasar opt --bind-only "<sql>" dumps typed bound RelExpr tree; type errors → OptError::Unsupported w/ location; binder fixtures roundtrip.

  • quasar-cat — Tables; columns (id/type/nullable/computed); indexes (key cols + ASC/DESC, stored cols, partial pred, inverted flag); scalar type tags; zone configs; regions (RegionId); range/leaseholder placement stub.
  • quasar-ir — Full RelExpr+ScalarExpr (logical ops; physical/enforcer variants stubbed); Operator tag enum; ColumnId arena (monotone u32 per query); ColSet over fixedbitset.
  • quasar-sql — Lexer+parser for §8 EBNF: SELECT/FROM/JOIN/WHERE/GROUP BY/HAVING/ORDER BY/LIMIT/OFFSET/VALUES/WITH; scalar exprs incl. func calls, CASE, CAST, subqueries (EXISTS/IN/ANY/ALL), unary/binary ops.
  • quasar-bind — Name resolution (scope stack ScopeFrame{cols:HashMap<String,ColumnId>}); type checking + implicit casts; col-id alloc; GROUP BY/aggregate binding; subquery + correlation (outer_cols: ColSet); join binding (cross/inner/left/semi/anti); DML (INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/UPSERT).

1.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: ADR-A01 (Rust stable), ADR-A03 (cargo workspace), P2 (Memo as single source of truth — catalog snapshot fixed for one optimize() lifetime), I2 (ColumnIds unique: ColumnAllocator pattern), ADR-A20 (OptError typed — CatalogMissing, Unsupported variants), Selinger 1979 (access path selection grounds catalog index descriptor design), CockroachDB DistSQL RFC (quasar-cat RangeMeta / leaseholder design origin).

  • quasar-cat — TableDef{id,name,columns:Vec<ColumnDef>,indexes:Vec<IndexDef>,zone:ZoneConfig,primary_key:IndexId}; ColumnDef{id,name,typ:ScalarType,nullable,computed:Option<ComputedExpr>}; IndexDef{id,table,name,key_cols:Vec<IndexKeyCol>,stored_cols:ColSet,partial_pred:Option<ScalarExpr>,inverted,unique}; IndexKeyCol{col,direction} (enum SortDirection{Asc,Desc}); ZoneConfig{region_affinities,lease_preferences:Vec<RegionId>}; RangeMeta{range_id,leaseholder_node:NodeId,leaseholder_region:RegionId}. CatalogResolver expands Phase 0: indexes(TableId)->&[IndexDef], column(TableId,ColumnId)->&ColumnDef, zone(TableId)->&ZoneConfig, range_meta(TableId,&Span)->Vec<RangeMeta> (empty until Phase 11). InMemoryCatalog: HashMap<TableId,TableDef> + name index; load_catalog_from_json(&Path) for fixtures. ColumnId namespace: catalog assigns stable ids at load; binder allocates synthetic ids from catalog_max+1.
  • quasar-ir — ColSet replaces u128 stub with fixedbitset::FixedBitSet newtype (dynamic width; insert/remove/contains/union/intersection/difference/is_subset/iter/len/is_empty). RelExpr: all SPINE variants; unreachable-from-binder (ZigzagJoin,InvertedJoin,Window,Ordinality, enforcers Distribute/SortEnforcer) → PhaseDeferred §21 Phase 9. ScalarExpr: all SPINE variants; Subquery(GroupId)/AnyAll{..sub:GroupId} carry sentinel GroupId(u32::MAX), filled by memo Phase 3. enum Operator: flat dispatch tag (Copy,PartialEq,Eq,Hash). ColumnAllocator{next:u32}::alloc(&mut)->ColumnId.
  • quasar-sql lexer — byte scanner → Vec<Token>+Vec<Span>. Tokens: keywords (SELECT FROM WHERE JOIN ON GROUP BY HAVING ORDER LIMIT OFFSET WITH AS VALUES INSERT UPDATE DELETE UPSERT INTO SET RETURNING EXISTS IN ANY ALL NOT AND OR IS NULL LIKE BETWEEN CASE WHEN THEN ELSE END CAST TRUE FALSE), punct (( ) , . * ;), ops (= <> != < <= > >= + - / % || -> ->>), IntLit/FloatLit/StrLit/Ident. Case-fold keywords; track (line,col) per token.
  • quasar-sql parser — recursive descent: stmt=select_stmt|insert_stmt|update_stmt|delete_stmt|upsert_stmt; select_stmt=[WITH cte_list] SELECT [DISTINCT] select_list FROM table_ref {join_clause} [WHERE expr] [GROUP BY expr_list [HAVING expr]] [ORDER BY order_list] [LIMIT expr [OFFSET expr]]; cte=ident AS '(' select_stmt ')'; join_clause=[join_type] JOIN table_ref ON expr; join_type=INNER|LEFT[OUTER]|RIGHT[OUTER]|CROSS|SEMI|ANTI; table_ref=ident[AS ident]|'(' select_stmt ')' AS ident. ParseError{msg,line,col}→Unsupported. Precedence (Pratt): OR<AND<NOT<comparison<additive<multiplicative<unary<primary. Subquery '(' select_stmt ')' in expr position → RawAst::Subquery.
  • quasar-bind scope stack — BindCtx{catalog:Arc<dyn CatalogResolver>,scopes:Vec<ScopeFrame>,col_alloc:ColumnAllocator,outer_cols:ColSet}; ScopeFrame{source:ScopeSource,cols:IndexMap<String,ColumnId>,all_cols:ColSet}; enum ScopeSource{Table(TableId),Subquery(GroupId),Cte(String)}. Resolution innermost-first; ambiguous col → Unsupported w/ message.
  • quasar-bind type checking — enum ScalarType{Bool,Int(IntWidth),Float(FloatWidth),Decimal(u8,u8),String,Bytes,Date,Timestamp,Interval,Jsonb,Array(Box<ScalarType>),Tuple(Vec<ScalarType>),Null,Any}; infer_type(&ScalarExpr,&BindCtx)->Result<ScalarType,OptError>. Implicit casts: Int→Float, Int→Decimal, String→Date/Timestamp where context requires; unknown → Unsupported.
  • quasar-bind node binding — SELECT list → ScalarExpr per item, * expands to all current-scope cols. FROM/JOIN: push ScopeFrame per table (join pushes both); bind ON; build InnerJoin/LeftJoin/SemiJoin/AntiJoin. WHERE: bind filter, wrap in Select{input,filters}. GROUP BY/aggregates: detect agg calls (SUM,COUNT,AVG,MIN,MAX,BOOL_AND,BOOL_OR,ARRAY_AGG,STRING_AGG,HLL_UNION_AGG) in SELECT/HAVING, lift, build GroupBy/ScalarGroupBy. ORDER BY→Sort; LIMIT/OFFSET→Limit. Subqueries: bind inner recursively w/ new BindCtx sharing catalog; outer refs→outer_cols; RelExpr in side-map keyed by fresh sentinel GroupId; ScalarExpr::Subquery(GroupId) placeholder (memo inserts Phase 3). WITH: bind CTE body, push ScopeFrame{Cte(name)}; reference → WithScan{id}. DML: INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/UPSERT → matching RelExpr variant; bind WHERE/RETURNING.
  • quasar-cli — quasar opt --bind-only "<sql>": binder → pretty-print RelExpr via Display, no memo/opt; --catalog <json> loads from file instead of hardcoded stub.

1.3 Deliverable

  • quasar opt --bind-only "SELECT a, b FROM t WHERE a > 1" → Select[filter=a>1]\n Scan[t, cols={a,b}].
  • quasar opt --bind-only "SELECT t.a FROM t JOIN s ON t.a=s.a" → InnerJoin[on=t.a=s.a]\n Scan[t]\n Scan[s].
  • quasar opt --bind-only "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM t GROUP BY b" → GroupBy[grouping={b}, aggs=[count(*)]]\n Scan[t].
  • quasar opt --bind-only "SELECT a FROM t WHERE a IN (SELECT b FROM s)" → Select[filter=exists-subquery]\n Scan[t] (subquery GroupId sentinel).
  • quasar opt --bind-only "SELECT bad_col FROM t" → error: column 'bad_col' not found (exit 1).
  • quasar opt --bind-only --catalog schema.json "<sql>" → uses catalog from file; all 16 crates compile, Phase 0 smoke still passes.

1.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • Binder fixture suite (§1.5): 100% pass. --bind-only for all SELECT fixtures: correct tree via golden diff. Type error fixtures: Unsupported + non-empty location. Ambiguous column ref: error, not silent first-match.
  • Star expansion: correct ColSet of all table cols. CTE roundtrip: WITH cte AS (..) SELECT .. FROM cte → WithScan. DML binding: INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/UPSERT → correct variant. cargo clippy --workspace -- -D warnings: zero warnings.
  • ColSet ops: union/intersection/difference correct on 200-col fixture. Catalog JSON load: parses schema fixture, no panic. Binder latency, 10-table join: < 2 ms. Memory, bound 10-table join: < 512 KB heap (dhat/DHAT_HEAP_TRACKING).
  • Invariants (§7): I2 (all ColumnIds unique within query), I4 (outer_cols tracked across subquery boundary).

1.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root cause Debug signal Recovery
Scope frame pop/push mismatch in JOIN Right-table col visible after scope closes Frame not popped on error path QUASAR_LOG=trace push/pop events RAII ScopeGuard pops on drop
Star includes hidden system cols SELECT * returns crdb_internal_* Catalog exposes all cols Golden fixture catches extras Filter on ColumnDef::hidden; expand non-hidden only
Subquery outer-col leak Outer col in wrong frame Recursive bind shares outer_cols by value QUASAR_LOG=debug prints per-frame outer_cols Thread &mut ColSet; clear on entry, merge on exit
fixedbitset OOM, wide schemas Alloc fail on 10k-col table with_capacity(10000) ~1.25 KB/set, many in flight dhat heap profile Lazy resize: start 64, grow on demand
Integer literal overflow SELECT 9999999999999999999 panics on i64::from_str No overflow guard Parse test w/ large literal Catch → Unsupported
CTE recursive ref undetected Binder infinite loop No recursive-CTE support Phase 1 Stack depth check Detect self-ref in CTE frame → Unsupported("recursive CTE not supported")
Catalog JSON evolution Old fixture missing inverted → deserialize panic Strict serde CI fixture fails #[serde(default)] on optional fields
Aggregate in WHERE (not HAVING) Silently binds Agg detection only SELECT/HAVING Post-bind semantic check Validation pass: agg in WHERE/JOIN-ON/GROUP-BY-key → Unsupported

1.7 Performance Concerns

  • HashMap<String,ColumnId> clone, 50-table query → IndexMap + small-string opt; &str keys on lookup. Deep subquery bind stack → SQL depth ≤32 (parser-enforced); ~64 KB stack; fine for subset.
  • Catalog JSON deser at startup → cache InMemoryCatalog across cases; share Arc; no per-fixture reload. ColSet clone in scope ops → clone only at frame push; &ColSet for reads.
  • Vec<ColumnDef> linear name scan → name_index:HashMap<String,usize> in TableDef; O(1) lookup. Display RelExpr in --bind-only → CLI path only; not hot path; no concern.

1.8 Edge Cases

  • SELECT 1+1 (no FROM) → RelExpr::Values one row; empty scope stack. SELECT a FROM t AS alias WHERE alias.a>1 → frame keyed by alias; t.a/alias.a → same ColumnId.
  • Col name = keyword (SELECT "select" FROM t) → quoted ident → Ident; no conflict. GROUP BY 1 (ordinal) → resolve to SELECT-list position; desugar to that expr's ColumnId.
  • HAVING without GROUP BY → implicit ScalarGroupBy; valid if HAVING uses aggregate. JOIN USING(a) → desugar to ON t1.a=t2.a; merge ColumnIds under equijoin equiv; one output col.
  • NULL literal → Const(Datum::Null) type Null; CAST to target where context requires. Very long query (1000 tokens) → index-based token cursor; no quadratic scan.
  • INSERT ... RETURNING * → bind RETURNING vs modified table; star-expand against table def. UPDATE w/ subquery in SET → correlated scalar subquery; outer_cols includes updated row cols.

1.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast radius Containment Recovery
Binder panics on malformed AST Panic → CLI backtrace Single query catch_unwind at CLI boundary; log + Unsupported cargo-fuzz on quasar_sql::parse
CatalogMissing propagates silently Test returns wrong Ok Downstream phantom plan All lookups return Result; no placeholder on miss Audit: grep unwrap_or_default crates/quasar-cat = zero
Unsafe implicit cast allowed Bind ok, runtime type mismatch Wrong results Cast-matrix fixture Explicit allowlist; deny all others
Scope stack unbounded on nested subqueries Stack overflow Process crash Depth counter; error at >32 Fixture: depth-33 nesting
ColumnAllocator wraps at u32::MAX ColumnId collision Silent col aliasing Assert next < u32::MAX debug_assert (queries never near 4B cols)
(postmortem, all classes) — — — QUASAR_LOG=trace quasar opt --bind-only "<failing_sql>" + catalog JSON → reproducible

1.10 Operational Notes

  • Tracing — quasar-bind emits debug! spans bind_table/bind_join/bind_subquery w/ names; QUASAR_LOG=debug.
  • Metrics — counter("bind.columns_allocated") per alloc; histogram("bind.latency_us") per query. Stubs now, wired Phase 13.
  • Catalog hot-reload — InMemoryCatalog immutable post-construction; Phase 13 adds staleness invalidation; design allows future Arc<RwLock<Catalog>>.
  • Compatibility — RawAst crate-internal; public IR is RelExpr/ScalarExpr; adding AST nodes is backward-compatible if same IR emitted.
  • Schema JSON — crates/quasar-cat/schema_format.md; "version":1; evolution adds fields w/ #[serde(default)].
  • Determinism — deterministic given SQL+catalog; no randomness. Required for replay (Phase 13).
  • Migration — Phase 0 hardcoded catalog → InMemoryCatalog::hardcoded() via same JSON-load path w/ embedded bytes; no external state change.

14. Phase 2 — Logical Properties + Functional Dependencies — quasar-props

2.1 Objectives

Derive LogicalProps bottom-up for every bound RelExpr node, computed once at construction (or on demand before memo insertion in Phase 3). Algorithms: FD closure (seed ColSet → closure under all FDs); FD-simplified ordering (drop constants, collapse equiv to canonical, used in OrderingChoice); key minimization (drop supersets from keys). Deliverable: derive_props(expr:&RelExpr, input_props:&[&LogicalProps], cat:&dyn CatalogResolver) -> LogicalProps; flag quasar opt --props "<sql>" annotates each node.

  • out_cols: ColSet — cols produced, visible to parent. not_null: ColSet — provably non-null (null-rejecting analysis thru filters/joins).
  • keys: Vec<ColSet> — candidate keys, minimal col sets determining all out_cols. FuncDepSet — equiv groups (equijoin/= filter), const cols (col=const), strict FDs (keys + group-by).
  • cardinality: Cardinality — {min,max}: Scan={0,table_rows}, Filter≤input, GroupBy≤input, distinct≤input, SemiJoin≤left. outer_cols: ColSet — referenced not produced, correlation markers for subquery lifting.

2.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: Selinger 1979 (interesting orders → OrderingChoice design), Garcia-Molina Ch. 16 (equivalence rules ground FD derivation per operator), I6 (OrderingChoice FD-consistency — simplify() algorithm), I11 (FD closure consistency — iterative fixed-point in FuncDepSet::close()), P5 (physical properties drive plan choice — LogicalProps feeds PhysProps derivation), ADR-A05 (memo arena context: LogicalProps stored in Group.logical).

  • Crate structure (crates/quasar-props/src/) — lib.rs (re-exports); logical.rs (LogicalProps, derive_props dispatch); fd.rs (FuncDepSet/FuncDep/EquivGroups/closure/minimize); cardinality.rs (Cardinality: meet/join(product)/filter(sel)/min/max); ordering.rs (OrderingChoice/OrderingCol/fd_simplify); physical.rs (PhysProps/Distribution, moved from Phase 0 stub); props_test.rs (unit + datadriven tests).
  • FuncDepSet internals — FuncDepSet{key:Option<ColSet>,equiv:EquivGroups,constants:ColSet,deps:Vec<FuncDep>}; FuncDep{determinant:ColSet,dependant:ColSet,strict:bool} (strict=null-rejecting: key FDs strict, thru-null FDs lax); EquivGroups{groups:Vec<ColSet>} disjoint, merge on equijoin/equi-filter. closure(seed:ColSet)->ColSet: iterate deps to fixed-point + equiv expansion + constants seeding. minimize_key(fd,key)->ColSet: drop each col if closure of remainder still covers all cols (O(cols²)). add_equiv(a,b): union-find merge; add_constant(c): insert + trigger closure of FDs whose determinant⊆constants; add_dep(det,dep,strict): insert + simplify (drop subsumed deps).

Per-operator derive_props (all SPINE operators):

Operator out_cols not_null keys FD additions cardinality outer_cols
Scan{table,cols} cols catalog not-null ∩ cols PK key cols PK→all cols (strict) {0,table_row_count} ∅
IndexScan projected cols as Scan PK key same {0,table_row_count} ∅
Select{input,filters} input.out_cols input.not_null ∪ null-rejected(filters) input.keys input.fd + const/equiv from filters {0,input.card.max} input.outer
Project{input,projections} projected cols not_null ∩ out_cols keys if all key cols in out_cols input.fd restricted to out_cols input.card input.outer
InnerJoin{left,right,on} left.out ∪ right.out left ∪ right ∪ null-rejected(on) l_key ∪ r_key merge fds + equiv/const from on {0, left.max × right.max} left.outer ∪ right.outer
LeftJoin same left.not_null ∪ null-rejected-in-on (right nullable) left.keys only left.fd; right FDs lax {left.min, left.max × right.max} union
SemiJoin left.out left.not_null left.keys left.fd {0, left.max} union
AntiJoin left.out left.not_null left.keys left.fd {0, left.max} union
GroupBy{input,grouping_cols,aggs} grouping_cols ∪ agg_result_cols grouping_cols ∩ input.not_null {grouping_cols} (if non-empty) grouping_cols → all out_cols (strict) {0,input.card.max} input.outer
ScalarGroupBy agg result cols ∅ (nullable if empty input) {} (0/1 row) empty → all (trivial) {0,1} input.outer
Distinct{input,cols} input.out input.not_null {cols} input.fd + cols→all out {0,input.max} input.outer
Sort{input,ordering} input.out input.not_null input.keys input.fd input.card input.outer
Limit{input,n,offset} input.out input.not_null input.keys (n=1 → key=∅) input.fd {0, min(input.max,n)} input.outer
UnionAll left.out (= right.out) left ∩ right not_null ∅ ∅ {l.min+r.min, l.max+r.max} union
Union (dedup) out l∩r not_null ∅ ∅ {0, l.max+r.max} union
Values defined cols all (if no NULL literal) {row_id_synthetic} ∅ {n_rows,n_rows} (exact) ∅
WithScan{id} CTE out_cols CTE not_null CTE keys CTE fd CTE card ∅
Enforcer (Sort,Distribute) passthrough passthrough passthrough passthrough passthrough passthrough
Mutation (Insert/Update/Delete) RETURNING cols or ∅ ∅ ∅ ∅ {0,∞} input.outer
  • Null-rejecting analysis (null_rejected_cols(filters,input_not_null)->ColSet) — col IS NOT NULL→col; col op const (op∈{=,<,>,<=,>=}, not IS)→col (NULL→UNKNOWN, row rejected); col1=col2→both; AND(l,r)=union of branches; OR=only cols rejected by both; NOT(IS NULL col)→col. Short-circuits at first unconditional rejection per col.
  • FD-simplified ordering (OrderingChoice::simplify, input Vec<OrderingCol>+FuncDepSet) — 1. remove cols in fd.constants; 2. replace equiv-group members w/ canonical (lowest ColumnId); 3. drop duplicates; 4. if remaining prefix is a key, drop trailing cols. Result: minimal cols determining full sort order.
  • Integration with quasar-ir — RelExpr::props(&self)->&LogicalProps (lazy field or inline; Phase 2 stores inline Box<LogicalProps>, Phase 3 migrates to Group.logical, no API break); derive_props_tree(root:&RelExpr,cat:&dyn CatalogResolver)->Result<RelExpr,OptError> walks bottom-up, calling derive_props per node.
  • CLI + testing — quasar opt --props "<sql>": bind→derive_props_tree→print tree w/ annotation (out_cols, not_null, keys, cardinality, FD summary FD:a->b,c=d); --bind-only still works. props_test.rs per-operator unit tests; cargo test -p quasar-props before integration; datadriven goldens (§2.5).

2.3 Deliverable

  • quasar opt --props "SELECT a FROM t WHERE a > 1" → Select[out={a}, not_null={a}, keys=[{a}], card={0,1000}, FD:{}]\n Scan[t, out={a,b}, keys=[{a}], FD:{a}->b].
  • quasar opt --props "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM t GROUP BY b" → GroupBy[out={b,count}, keys=[{b}], FD:{b}->{b,count}].
  • quasar opt --props "SELECT a FROM t JOIN s ON t.a=s.a" → merged FDs; equiv {t.a,s.a}; key {t.a,s.pk}.
  • quasar opt --props "SELECT DISTINCT a FROM t" → keys=[{a}], FD:{a}->a.
  • quasar opt --props "SELECT a FROM t LIMIT 1" → card={0,1}; all Phase 0/1 tests still pass.

2.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • Props golden fixtures (§2.5): 100% match. cardinality.max non-increasing thru Select: 100 random filters → Select.max <= Scan.max. GroupBy key = grouping_cols (non-empty): fixture + unit test.
  • SemiJoin out_cols == left: unit, no right cols in output. InnerJoin key = union(left,right): fixture, two keyed scans. FD closure {a}->b,{b}->c ⊢ {a}={a,b,c}: unit in fd.rs.
  • FD-simplified ordering drops const (a=5 → [a,b] simplifies to [b]). Null-rejection WHERE a>1 → a in not_null: fixture. LeftJoin right cols NOT in not_null: unit test.
  • --props latency, 10-table join < 5 ms. derive_props_tree alloc, 10-table join < 1 MB heap delta. cargo test --workspace: 100% pass.
  • Invariants (§7): I5 (FD closure correct), I6 (keys minimal), I7 (cardinality bounds monotone thru operators).

2.5 Conformance Fixtures

All under conformance/testdata/props/, directive props (file → purpose: assertion):

  • scan_keys.sql → PK key from catalog: keys=[{a}]. filter_not_null.sql → null rejection via WHERE: not_null gains col.
  • filter_const_fd.sql → WHERE a=5 const: FD:constants={a}. join_equiv.sql → equijoin equiv group: equiv={t.a,s.a}.
  • join_key_cross.sql → InnerJoin key derivation: keys=[{t.a,s.id}]. left_join_nullable.sql → LeftJoin right nullable: right cols absent from not_null.
  • semi_join_out_cols.sql → SemiJoin output = left: out_cols excludes right. group_by_key.sql → GroupBy key: keys=[{b}].
  • scalar_group_by_card.sql → ScalarGroupBy card: min=0,max=1. distinct_key.sql → Distinct creates key: keys=[{a}].
  • limit_card.sql → LIMIT caps max: card.max=1. fd_closure.sql → transitive FD closure: ordering simplification visible.
  • ordering_simplify.sql → FD-simplified ordering: const col removed. or_not_null.sql → OR: cols rejected in both branches: partial null rejection.
  • project_key_lost.sql → Project drops key col: keys=[].

2.6 Pitfalls

  • FD closure infinite loop → derive_props hangs (circular FD a→b,b→a as deps not equiv; signal: CPU spin/iteration-count assert) → detect cycle, merge into equiv group. Key minimization wrong col → missed optimizations, not safety (minimize_key order-dependent; signal: 3-col key golden) → fixed-point: try all single removals, accept smallest.
  • LeftJoin right cols in not_null → downstream assumes non-null where row may be null (copying right.not_null w/o masking; signal: null-rejection fixture) → clear right not_null after LeftJoin, keep left only. outer_cols not cleared at subquery boundary → subquery outer cols leak to parent (recursive derive_props_tree shares set; signal: --props shows unexpected outer_cols) → subquery root starts outer_cols=∅, exposed refs = correlated subset.
  • cardinality.max overflow on big join → max×max overflows u64 (unchecked mul; signal: overflow on 4B×4B fixture) → u64::saturating_mul, cap at u64::MAX. add_dep quadratic on wide schemas → slow for 200-FD tables (linear subsumption scan per add; signal: 200-FD bench) → cap ≤64 deps, drop weakest non-key on overflow.
  • Constants not propagated thru closure → a=5 const, a→b, b not const (closure not re-run after add_constant; signal: --props shows non-const b) → run closure pass after add_constant, mark new constants.

2.7 Performance Concerns

  • FD closure O(deps×cols)/node → ≤64 deps; cols ≤ ColSet width; < 64×128=8192 ops/node, acceptable. One LogicalProps alloc/node → Phase 3 Arena<LogicalProps> (memo); Phase 2 Box/node fine for bind-only path.
  • ColSet clone in set ops → FixedBitSet::clone O(n/64); ≤128 cols = 2 words, negligible. keys:Vec<ColSet> clone/operator → avg ≤3 keys, cheap; pass &[ColSet] in hot paths.
  • Null-rejection linear scan → ≤20 filters typical, early exit on first rejection. --props formatting alloc → CLI output only; String::with_capacity for renderer.

2.8 Edge Cases

  • Scan empty cols (pruned) → out_cols=∅; valid constant-scan, cardinality preserved. Always-true filter (1=1) → no cols rejected; not_null/cardinality unchanged.
  • Always-false filter (1=0) → cardinality={0,0}; optimizer elides later, derive_props no assert. Join on non-equality (t.a<s.b) → no equiv/FD added; keys = cross-product.
  • GroupBy zero grouping + zero aggs → ScalarGroupBy; out_cols=∅; card={0,1}. Project computed expr col → synthetic ColumnId; no FD unless deterministic fn of inputs (Phase 4+).
  • VALUES w/ NULL literal → not_null excludes that col; multi-row union → col nullable if any row NULL. UNION ALL differing not_null → not_null = left ∩ right (conservative).
  • Key survives Project only if all key cols projected → drop key if any col dropped, if none survive keys=[]. Cardinality min after LeftJoin → min = left.min (left row always present).

2.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast radius Containment Recovery
False-positive not_null (col claimed non-null) NullPointerEquivalent in exec (Phase 14+) Wrong results Props goldens catch regressions Conservative: omit col from not_null when uncertain
Missing key → missed unique-join opt Suboptimal plan; correctness ok Performance only Cost regression fixture Re-derive w/ catalog refresh; re-run key minimization
FD closure missing transitive dep Ordering simplification drops wrong col → wrong sort Wrong results props/fd_closure.sql Comprehensive closure unit tests; proptest
outer_cols false-positive Subquery treated correlated; unnecessary apply-join Performance regression --props inspection Recompute outer_cols from scratch on subquery root
cardinality.min > max Debug assertion fires Panic debug, wrong bounds release debug_assert!(min<=max) in Cardinality Fix derivation producing inverted bounds
(postmortem, all classes) — — — QUASAR_LOG=trace quasar opt --props "<sql>" → per-node derivation log; --catalog <json> for custom schema

2.10 Operational Notes

  • Tracing — quasar-props emits trace!("derive_props op=.. out_cols=.. keys=..") per node; QUASAR_LOG=trace.
  • Metrics — histogram("props.derive_latency_us") per tree; counter("props.fd_overflow") when FD list capped. Wired Phase 13.
  • Determinism — derive_props pure fn of RelExpr + catalog; same inputs → same LogicalProps. Required for replay (Phase 13).
  • FD debug dump — quasar opt --props --dump-fd "<sql>" prints full FuncDepSet (equiv, constants, deps) per node; for debugging rule preconditions.
  • Compatibility — LogicalProps internal to quasar-props/quasar-memo; adding fields (e.g. avg_row_size Phase 5) backward-compatible; missing fields default conservative.
  • No persistent state — computed on demand; schema changes invalidate via cache staleness (Phase 13); no migration for props layer.
  • Interaction w/ quasar-memo (Phase 3) — LogicalProps moves inline RelExpr → Group.logical; derive_props becomes memo's Group constructor callback; Phase 2 inline Box<LogicalProps> migrated w/o API break.

15. Phase 3 — The Memo — quasar-memo (groups, expressions, interning, normalize hook)

3.1 Objectives

Build the central shared data structure on which all subsequent phases operate. Acceptance: identical ScanOp subtrees collapse; no group holds duplicate ExprIds; memo-dump goldens match byte-for-byte.

  • Group arena: Arena<Group>, GroupId(u32) handles, append-only, no removals. MExpr arena: Arena<MExpr>, ExprId(u32) handles, structural dedup via intern map.
  • ScalarExpr arena: Arena<ScalarExpr>, ScalarId(u32), shared across groups. Interning: MExprKey{op_tag:u8,children:SmallVec<[GroupId;3]>,scalar:Option<ScalarId>}→ExprId via HashMap<MExprKey,ExprId>; identical exprs share one slot.
  • Insertion: Memo::insert(RelExpr)->GroupId recursively interns children, builds MExpr, looks up intern map, adds to existing group or creates singleton. LogicalProps derived once at group creation via quasar-props (Phase 2), stored Group::logical, never recomputed.
  • Normalize hook: Memo::factory(&mut self,expr:MExpr)->MExpr called before interning, placeholder for Phase 4 rules to rewrite MExpr. GroupState: enum GroupState{Unexplored,Exploring,Explored} per Group, used by Phase 10 Cascades search.
  • Memo dump: quasar memo "<sql>" renders groups+members+LogicalProps+winner slots (empty here) deterministically for goldens. Deliverable: cargo test -p quasar-memo passes; quasar memo "SELECT a FROM t WHERE a>1" emits parseable dump; interning fixtures confirm shared GroupIds.

3.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: Graefe 1995 Cascades (Group/MExpr/winner table structure), ADR-A05 (typed-arena + u32 handles decision), I1 (Memo structural dedup — intern map key MExprKey), I2 (Group logical props uniformity — derive once at creation), I9 (Memo immutability post-winner extraction — Rust ownership), P2 (Memo is the single source of truth), Programming Rust Ch. 22 (unsafe arena allocation patterns).

  • Crate files (crates/quasar-memo/src/) — lib.rs (re-exports); arena.rs (Arena<T>=Vec+u32 wrapper, ArenaIndex trait); group.rs (Group, GroupState, Winner — empty here); mexpr.rs (MExpr, MExprKey — PartialEq+Hash on op_tag/children/scalar); memo.rs (Memo: arenas, intern map, root, budget field for Phase 10); factory.rs (construct() entry, normalize hook stub); props_derive.rs (delegates to quasar-props per operator, called at group creation); dump.rs (deterministic text dump); tests/intern_tests.rs (structural dedup fixtures), tests/dump_tests.rs (golden dump fixtures).
  • Arena — Arena<T>{data:Vec<T>}, push(v:T)->I where I:ArenaIndex; index types #[repr(transparent)] u32 newtypes (from_usize panics > u32::MAX); no get_mut after push (append-only), get(idx:I)->&T only.
  • MExprKey hashing — derive Hash+PartialEq on (op_tag,children,scalar); children:SmallVec<[GroupId;3]> covers binary ops without heap alloc; op_tag=Operator discriminant as u8 + shape selector for same-tag-different-fixed-fields (JoinKind,IndexId) packed in separate u32 aux.
  • Memo::insert(expr:RelExpr)->GroupId — recursively insert each child → child_groups:SmallVec<[GroupId;3]>; lower scalar arms via intern_scalar; build MExpr{op,children,scalar,rules_applied:RuleMask::NONE}; call factory(mexpr)->MExpr (normalize hook, unchanged Phase 3); build MExprKey, look up self.intern (found→return its group, dedup; else insert new ExprId via self.exprs.push(mexpr)); if factory produced same op+children as an existing group's representative add as second member, else create Group{id,members:vec![expr_id],logical:derive_logical_props(),winners:HashMap::new(),explored:false,state:Unexplored}; push expr_id into group.members (dedup via membership scan, ~3 at this scale); register (MExprKey→ExprId) and (ExprId→GroupId) side maps.
  • LogicalProps at group creation — derive_logical_props(memo:&Memo,mexpr:&MExpr)->LogicalProps in props_derive.rs; delegates to quasar-props; reads children's props from groups; called once per Group; stored Group::logical.
  • ScalarExpr interning — intern_scalar(e:ScalarExpr)->ScalarId: structural hash, return existing if equal else push to self.scalars; scalars pure → safe to share.
  • Dump renderer — Memo::dump(&self)->String: iterate groups by GroupId; per group print [G<id>] + LogicalProps summary (out_cols, cardinality min..max, key cols); list each member E<id>: <op_tag>(<child_G_ids...>) scalar=<ScalarId?>. Deterministic (insertion-order ids, no HashMap iteration); quasar memo "<sql>" = parse→bind→insert→dump.
  • Observability stubs — memo.stats()->MemoStats{group_count,expr_count,scalar_count,intern_hit_rate}; tracing::debug! span wraps insert (full obs Phase 13).

3.3 Deliverable

  • quasar memo "<sql>" → full dump: groups, members, logical props, winner slots. quasar opt "<sql>" → trivial single-group plan (no opt yet; reuses Phase 0 path).
  • cargo test -p quasar-memo → arena, interning, dedup, dump, LogicalProps tests. cargo test -p quasar-props (regression) → props unchanged from Phase 2; dump format stable ASCII, downstream opt-test goldens consume it verbatim.

3.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • Interning dedup: two inserts of identical ScanOp{table:T1,cols:{a,b}} → same ExprId. Group uniqueness: no Group holds duplicate ExprIds.
  • LogicalProps: out_cols/cardinality correct for Scan/Select/Join, matches Phase 2 fixtures. Dump roundtrip: output parses back to same group/expr count.
  • Dump goldens byte-for-byte on 5+ fixtures. No alloc growth: 1000 unique exprs → Arena 1000 slots, intern map 1000 entries.
  • Insertion latency p99 < 5 µs/insert up to 10 000 exprs (microbench). GroupState: all groups Unexplored after Phase 3 build.
  • Invariant I1: child GroupIds exist (no dangling). Invariant I4: Group::logical populated at creation, never None.

3.5 Conformance Fixtures

All under conformance/testdata/memo/, directive memo (file → purpose: expected):

  • intern_scan.opt → two identical scans, same ExprId: single E0 in [G0]. intern_select.opt → Select wrapping same scan, dedup: [G1] 1 member referencing G0.
  • intern_join.opt → Join(A,B) twice, single ExprId: shared G2. props_scan.opt → out_cols+cardinality on Scan: [G0] out_cols={a,b} card=0..∞.
  • props_select.opt → cardinality narrowed by Select: card.max <= G0.card.max. props_join.opt → inner join cardinality product: card.max = L.max * R.max.
  • dump_deep.opt → 4-level nesting, GroupId ordering: groups 0..6 insertion order. malformed_groupid.opt → child referencing non-existent GroupId: OptError::CatalogMissing (or debug panic).
  • large_scan.opt → 50-col ScanOp, SmallVec→heap path: correct dump, no UB. Regression: re-run all Phase 2 props/ fixtures, output unchanged.

3.6 Pitfalls

  • Hash collision in intern map → two distinct exprs share ExprId (MExprKey mixes GroupIds that alias after reorder; signal: assert_ne!(intern(A,B),intern(B,A)) for non-commutative) → include op_tag + ordered children; FxHashMap + explicit PartialEq. LogicalProps derivation cycle → stack overflow on CTE/WithScan (WithScan references GroupId not yet inserted; signal: RUST_LOG=quasar_memo=trace infinite recursion) → insert sentinel LogicalProps::UNKNOWN before recursion, fill post-construction.
  • GroupId leak across memos → subquery memo passes parent GroupId (u32 w/o provenance tag; signal: assert memo.groups.contains(gid) at child lookup) → wrap GroupId w/ MemoId generation in debug builds. Duplicate members in group → same ExprId pushed twice (factory yields same MExpr, intern ok but group-member check bypassed; signal: assert!(members.all_unique()) in tests) → check membership before push, O(n) on small lists.
  • Scalar interning aliasing → identical Column(ColumnId(3)) get different ScalarId (missing hash on a ScalarExpr arm; signal: identical predicates should share ScalarId) → derive Hash+PartialEq on all arms, test all. Arena index overflow → u32::MAX exprs/groups wrap (very large batch, prod panic; signal: Arena::push must panic on overflow, metric memo_expr_count) → enforce budget (saturating), return OptError::BudgetExceeded.
  • Normalize hook re-entrance → factory→insert→factory infinite loop (rule yields expr not in memo; signal: trace span depth counter) → limit factory recursion depth 16, panic if exceeded.

3.7 Performance Concerns

  • HashMap rehash on large memo → FxHashMap rehashes at 75% load, hot-path stall → with_capacity(4096) at construction; resize metric. SmallVec<[GroupId;3]> spill → >3 children (Window, multi-key Join) heap-alloc/insert → inline size 4 for MExprKey; dhat profile on TPC-H.
  • Cache miss on Arena::get → non-sequential access during prop derivation → Vec<T>-backed sequential layout, GroupId order = insertion, children before parent typically warm. derive_logical_props cost → FuncDepSet::closure() O(n²) on large FD → cap 64 deps, approximate above cap, metric fd_cap_hit.
  • intern_scalar deep hash → recursive hash on deep ScalarExpr → precompute + cache hash at construction, cached_hash:u64 field. Lock contention → single-threaded Phase 3 → document Memo is !Send until Phase 10 parallel prop derivation.

3.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty Values relation → group out_cols={}, card.max=0 → fixture VALUES () → card=0..0, out_cols={}. Subquery(GroupId) inserted before subquery group exists → forward reference → insert subquery group first (binder orders); assert GroupId validity in factory.
  • ColSet overflow (>64 cols) → bitset widens, union must not truncate → sparse bitset (roaring/smallvec) when ColumnId > 63; test 128-col table. Placeholder(PlaceholderIdx) in scalar → distinct indices must be structurally distinct → PlaceholderIdx contributes to MExprKey hash; fixture: two queries differing only in placeholder.
  • Same table, different cols subsets → must NOT dedup → MExprKey includes ColSet in aux; test SELECT a FROM t vs SELECT a,b FROM t. GroupId(0) as sentinel → code checks gid==GroupId(0) as absent → reserve GroupId(0) for root/explicit sentinel; document invariant.
  • Second insert for same root expr → should return existing root, not new group → intern lookup short-circuits; fixture confirms.

3.9 Failure Analysis

  • Arena index wraps → subsequent inserts wrong slot (detect: debug_assert! in push, metric alert) → hard panic debug, OptError::BudgetExceeded release; Memo::budget rejects queries before overflow. LogicalProps missing → all plan costing fails (detect: Phase 10 Group::logical unwrap panic) → Option<LogicalProps> + explicit error path; ensure derive_logical_props always called before group returns.
  • Intern map corrupted (wrong equality) → silent wrong plan (detect: debug post-insert check, recompute hash, compare) → re-audit PartialEq; proptest fuzz on MExprKey equality. Normalize hook panics → memo partial state (detect: factory panic unwinds insert) → factory called before group creation, panic → no group written; catch_unwind in debug, log, propagate OptError::Unsupported.
  • Dump non-determinism → CI red (detect: golden mismatch on re-run) → iterate GroupId asc, ExprId asc, no HashMap iteration; BTreeMap for traversal or sort keys before render.

3.10 Operational Notes

  • Tracing: tracing::debug_span!("memo_insert", gid=?g) per insert, disabled at WARN in prod. Metrics: quasar_memo_group_count/expr_count (gauge), quasar_memo_intern_hit_total/miss_total (counter) via quasar-obs stub.
  • Dump format stability: internal, not public API; goldens pinned per commit, format changes re-generate all goldens + bump DUMP_FORMAT_VERSION. No migration path: Memo fully in-memory, rebuilt per query, no ser/de; Phase 13 adds quasar-cache memo fingerprints.
  • Compatibility: quasar-memo has no I/O, no deploy step; completes as library + CLI addition. Debug assertions (cfg(debug_assertions)): group-member uniqueness, GroupId validity, arena-bounds; all off in release.
  • Memory ceiling: < 50 MB for 10 000 exprs + 5 000 groups on TPC-H Q5 (dev machine); measure dhat/jemalloc-ctl.

16. Phase 4 — optgen DSL + Normalization Rules — quasar-optgen, quasar-rules[Normalize]

4.1 Objectives

Build the rule compiler pipeline + always-on normalization rule set firing in the Memo construct factory. Acceptance: norm goldens pass; pushdown fixture shows Select below Join; pruning fixture reduces col count; normalization terminates within 16 fixpoint steps on all fixtures.

  • quasar-optgen: build.rs reads crates/quasar-rules/src/rules/*.opt → parses rule defs → emits OUT_DIR/generated_rules.rs (a RuleDef table, per-rule match/replace fns, dispatch try_normalize(memo,mexpr)->Option<MExpr>). .opt shape: [RuleName, Normalize] (<pattern>) => (<replacement>) per SPINE; pattern $var captures, replacement references $var.
  • Rule kinds: Normalize only this phase (Explore+Implement in Phases 8–9). Normalize-on-construct: Memo::factory(&mut self,mexpr:MExpr)->MExpr runs generated try_normalize in fixpoint loop (max 16 iters) before interning.
  • Mandatory rule set: constant folding; predicate pushdown (Select below Join/Project); predicate merge (And(And(a,b),c)→And(a,b,c), dedup); CNF normalization; contradiction detection (false pred → card=0); projection pruning (drop unused cols via Phase 2 out_cols); no-op elimination (identity Project, Select(input,true)); limit fold (Limit(Limit(n),m)→Limit(min(n,m))); comparison simplification (a=a→true,a<a→false); subquery decorrelation prep (mark correlated for Phase 8).
  • Idempotency (I3): normalizing an already-normalized expr yields the same expr. Confluence (I3): rule application order does not affect canonical form.
  • Deliverable: quasar opt --norm "<sql>" prints normalized memo + per-rule firing trace; quasar opt-test <file> w/ norm directive matches goldens.

4.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: ADR-A04 (optgen .opt DSL over runtime interpreter), CockroachDB optimizer RFC (optgen design origin), Graefe 1995 Cascades (Normalize rule kind — fires before interning), I3 (normalization idempotency + confluence — fixpoint loop + pair-permutation CI tests), P3 (Normalize/Explore/Implement strictly separated), P9 (generated rules over hand-written dispatch).

  • quasar-optgen files (crates/quasar-optgen/src/) — lib.rs (parse_opt_file()->Vec<RuleDef>); lexer.rs (tokenize .opt: symbols/keywords/idents/string literals); parser.rs (recursive descent: rule header, pattern tree, replacement tree); pattern.rs (PatternNode{Op(tag,children),Var(name),Literal(Datum)}); codegen.rs (emit Rust: match arm/rule, dispatch fn, RuleDef table); build_script.rs (glob *.opt→codegen→OUT_DIR, invoked from quasar-rules/build.rs); tests/parse_tests.rs (.opt parse→PatternNode tree), tests/codegen_tests.rs (snapshot generated Rust via insta).
  • quasar-rules files (crates/quasar-rules/) — build.rs (quasar_optgen::build_script::run("src/rules/normalize/*.opt")); src/lib.rs (re-export generated dispatch + manual hook tables); src/rules/normalize/ (fold_constants,push_select_thru_join,push_select_thru_project,merge_predicates,cnf_normalize,contradiction_detect,project_prune,noop_eliminate,limit_fold,comparison_simplify, all .opt); src/rules/explore/, src/rules/implement/ (empty, Phases 8/9); src/manual/corr_subquery_mark.rs (correlated subquery detection, not expressible in .opt); tests/norm_integration.rs (try_normalize on hand-built MExprs, verify canonical output).
  • build.rs pipeline — 1. glob("src/rules/normalize/*.opt") sorted (deterministic codegen). 2. per .opt: parse_opt_file(src)->Vec<RuleParsed>. 3. assign RuleId by global sorted order, emit static RULE_TABLE:&[RuleDef]. 4. per rule emit apply_rule_NNN(memo:&Memo,expr:&MExpr)->Option<MExpr> (match on expr.op + child GroupId checks, capture $var, return replacement MExpr or None). 5. emit try_normalize(memo:&Memo,expr:MExpr)->MExpr — iterate RULE_TABLE, call each, on first Some restart from top (fixpoint), return when stable or iter limit hit, tracing::trace! per firing.
  • Factory integration (quasar-memo/src/factory.rs) — Memo::factory(&mut self,mexpr:MExpr)->MExpr calls quasar_rules::try_normalize(&self,mexpr) (passes &Memo for child prop lookups); try_normalize may recursively Memo::insert for sub-exprs a rule introduces (e.g. pushed Select → new Select group), re-entrant insert guarded by normalizing:bool disabling factory recursion past 16; returns normalized MExpr for interning.
  • Key rule descriptions (algorithmic, no code) — push_select_thru_join: Select(InnerJoin(L,R,on),pred) where pred refs only L cols → InnerJoin(Select(L,pred),R,on) (symmetric for R; mixed preds stay on Join). merge_predicates: Select(Select(in,f1),f2)→Select(in,And(f1,f2)); And(And(a,b),c)→And(a,b,c) flatten; dedup by ScalarId. contradiction_detect: And(...) w/ any conjunct Const(false) or unequal-const Comparison{=} → set group Cardinality{min:0,max:0}, return Values{rows:[]}.
  • project_prune: Project{input,projections,passthrough} w/ passthrough cols not in parent's required_cols (top-down from root out_cols) → drop unused (needs top-down required_cols pass seeded from query root). limit_fold: Limit(Limit(inner,m,off_m),n,off_n)→Limit(inner,min(m,n+off_n)-off_m,off_m+off_n) when both limits constant. comparison_simplify: Comparison{Eq,Column(a),Column(a)}→Const(true); Comparison{Lt,Column(a),Column(a)}→Const(false).
  • Correlated subquery marking (manual/corr_subquery_mark.rs) — walk ScalarExpr for Subquery(GroupId); if referenced group's outer_cols intersects current group's out_cols, set MExpr::rules_applied bit CORR_MARKED; Phase 8 explore rules use it for decorrelation.

4.3 Deliverable

  • quasar opt-test conformance/testdata/normalize/*.opt → norm directive goldens pass. quasar opt --norm "<sql>" → normalized memo dump + rule-firing log (rule, ExprId before/after).
  • quasar explain --opt "<sql>" → unchanged from Phase 0/2 (normalization fires; opt not yet full). cargo build -p quasar-optgen -p quasar-rules → generated Rust in OUT_DIR compiles, no warnings.
  • cargo test -p quasar-optgen -p quasar-rules → parse, codegen snapshot, normalization integration tests pass.

4.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • Idempotency (I3): norm(norm(expr))==norm(expr) on all 10 fixture files. Confluence (I3): two orderings → identical canonical form.
  • Pushdown: SELECT * FROM t JOIN s ON t.id=s.id WHERE t.a=1 → Select(Scan(t),a=1) JOIN Scan(s). Merge predicates: SELECT * FROM (SELECT * FROM t WHERE a=1) WHERE b=2 → Select(Scan(t),And(a=1,b=2)).
  • Contradiction WHERE 1=2 → Values{rows:[]}, cardinality=0..0. Projection pruning: SELECT a FROM t → ScanOp.cols={a} only.
  • Limit fold: LIMIT 10 inside LIMIT 5 → single LIMIT 5. Termination: fixpoint ≤16 iterations on all fixtures.
  • Build determinism: cargo build twice from clean → identical generated_rules.rs. Codegen latency: build.rs < 2 s on full rule set.
  • Rule table: RULE_TABLE.len() == .opt rule count, compile-time assert_eq!.

4.5 Conformance Fixtures

All under conformance/testdata/normalize/ (file → purpose: expected canonical form):

  • fold_constants.opt (norm) → 1+1→2, true AND false→false: Const(2), Const(false). push_select_join.opt (norm) → single-side pred pushed into join input: Select below Scan, not above Join.
  • push_select_project.opt (norm) → pred pushed thru Project: Select below Project. merge_and.opt (norm) → nested Select/And flattened: single Select, flat And.
  • dedup_pred.opt (norm) → duplicate pred in And removed: And w/ unique conjuncts. contradiction.opt (norm) → WHERE false: Values{rows:[]} group.
  • project_prune.opt (norm) → only referenced cols survive: ScanOp.cols shrinks. noop_project.opt (norm) → Project passing all cols eliminated: input group directly.
  • noop_select.opt (norm) → WHERE true eliminated: input group directly. limit_fold.opt (norm) → nested LIMITs folded: single Limit.
  • cmp_simplify.opt (norm) → a=a→true, a<a→false: Const(true/false). idempotent.opt (norm x2) → run norm twice: no change on second pass.
  • malformed_opt.opt (parse-error) → invalid .opt syntax: build-time compile error, not runtime.

4.6 Pitfalls

  • Non-confluent rules → two orderings differ, goldens flicker across CI (rule A undoes rule B; signal: all pair permutations on small fixtures, RUST_LOG=quasar_rules=trace) → canonical ordering: structural before semantic; fixpoint convergence test. Fixpoint infinite loop → 16 iters → error (A→B→A cycle; signal: trace alternating firings) → cycle detect: if expr matches one seen this fixpoint, abort + log rule IDs.
  • Generated code won't compile → cargo build fails on generated_rules.rs (codegen emits invalid Rust — template typo, missing use; signal: build error on quasar-rules) → snapshot-test codegen; rustfmt + rustc --check in CI. Correlated mark misfire → non-correlated subquery marked (outer_cols wrong, Phase 2 bug surfacing; signal: fixture with no outer refs must not mark) → explicit fixture; verify outer_cols.is_empty() path.
  • Projection pruning over-prunes → ORDER BY/GROUP BY col removed (required_cols seed misses sort/group refs; signal: fewer cols than binding expects) → seed required_cols from out_cols AND sort/group keys at root. .opt glob order non-deterministic → RuleId differs across OS (glob not sorted; signal: codegen changes per OS) → sort glob; emit // generated-from: <sorted-filenames> header.
  • try_normalize borrows Memo mutably mid-call → borrow conflict, factory &mut Memo vs try_normalize needing &Memo (single-mutable-borrow rule; signal: compile-time borrow error) → pass &Memo immutable, return new MExprs, insertion happens in factory after return.

4.7 Performance Concerns

  • Generated dispatch → linear scan over all Normalize rules/factory call → match expr.op{..} O(1) discriminant; same-tag rules in small inner vec. try_normalize alloc → each firing creates new MExpr pre-interning → MExpr ≤40 bytes, stack alloc if returned directly, boxed only if stored.
  • build.rs codegen at dev iteration → rebuilds when any .opt changes → cargo:rerun-if-changed per file, rebuild only on actual change. Fixpoint overhead → 16 iters × N rules × M factory calls → typical 1–3 passes, most rules return None fast on non-matching op.
  • CNF blowup → exponential for deep OR chains → cap CNF 32 conjuncts, beyond → leave original + mark NORM_CNF_CAPPED. Codegen snapshot size → insta snapshot of generated Rust large → snapshot only dispatch fn + rule table, not every apply body.

4.8 Edge Cases

  • Rule produces expr identical to input → fixpoint sees no change, exits cleanly → hash-compare MExprKey before/after, skip iteration if same. Const(NaN) in comparison simplify → NaN=NaN must NOT fold to true (SQL semantics) → explicit NaN check; fixture WHERE nan_col=nan_col unfolded.
  • Empty And/Or after dedup → And([])/Or([]) → And([])→Const(true); Or([])→Const(false), explicit 0-arity rules. Limit w/ offset pushdown → offset arithmetic on nested limits → fold only if inner/outer offsets constant, else leave nested.
  • Unicode idents in .opt DSL → non-ASCII rule names → lexer accepts [A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]* only, reject non-ASCII w/ clear error. Rule applied to enforcer op → Distribute/SortEnforcer fed to normalize → enforcers absent in Phase 4 memo; skip factory for physical ops.
  • Zero-column Project → Project{cols:{}} after pruning → replace w/ Values{rows:[(<empty>)]} or Noop, cardinality preserved.

4.9 Failure Analysis

  • Normalization produces wrong plan → all queries using affected rule (detect: golden mismatch or silent wrong result; quasar opt --norm trace isolates rule) → disable rule via RuleMask::NONE + flag, re-enable after fix. build.rs codegen regression → all crates depending on quasar-rules (detect: cargo build red) → build-time only, prod ships compiled code; pin quasar-optgen version, snapshot-test codegen.
  • Factory infinite recursion → single query, process recovers (detect: stack overflow debug, silent timeout release) → recursion depth counter in factory, panic OptError::Unsupported at 16; log rule chain at panic, regression fixture. Rule table desync (count mismatch) → build failure (detect: assert_eq!(RULE_TABLE.len(),EXPECTED_COUNT) fails) → compile-time const assertion; regenerate, count is a const updated by build.rs.

4.10 Operational Notes

  • Tracing: trace!(rule=%rule.name,expr_id=?eid,"norm_fire") per application; trace!(iter=%i,"norm_fixpoint_done") on convergence. Metrics: quasar_norm_rule_fire_total{rule=<name>} counter, quasar_norm_fixpoint_iters histogram, via quasar-obs.
  • .opt authoring: one rule per file (name matches RuleName), under src/rules/normalize/ in quasar-rules; new rules add file + cargo build. Compatibility: generated_rules.rs in OUT_DIR, never committed, reproducible from source; rule-semantic changes break goldens, update goldens same commit.
  • Rule disable flag: Memo::disabled_rules:RuleMask skips rules in try_normalize; quasar opt --disable-rule=<name> for debugging, not production. Build determinism: build.rs hermetic (no network/clock/env beyond OUT_DIR+CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR), enforced via Cargo sandbox.

17. Phase 5 — Statistics + Cardinality Estimation — quasar-stats

5.1 Objectives

Build stat catalog + cardinality/selectivity layer; propagate Statistics onto group LogicalProps. Acceptance: q-error ≤3× on TPC-H-lite; selectivity goldens pass; missing-stats path → is_estimated=true, no panic.

  • Stat catalog load: quasar-stats reads JSON/TOML fixtures — per-table row_count; per-col ColumnStatistic{distinct_count,null_count,histogram,avg_size}; multi-col joint distinct_count over ColSet; per-col HLL sketch (offline → distinct_count). HLL integration: sketch stored as serialized bytes, hll::HLL::cardinality()→distinct_count, no query-time collection (offline analysis feeds catalog).
  • Equi-depth histogram: Histogram{buckets:Vec<Bucket>}, Bucket{upper_bound,num_eq,num_range,distinct_range}, loaded from file, drives range selectivity. Selectivity: equality 1.0/NDV clamp [1e-6,1.0]; range via bucket interpolation; IN/OR 1-(1-s1)*(1-s2) (independence); IS NULL null_count/row_count; conjunction = product.
  • Join cardinality: |R⋈S|=|R|*|S|/max(NDV_R(key),NDV_S(key)), multi-key min over keys, outer adds unmatched. GroupBy cardinality ≈NDV(grouping_cols) (multi-col min(product_NDVs,input_rows), scalar→1). Distinct ≈NDV(distinct_cols). Limit min(input_card.max,n). Set ops: UnionAll sum, Union NDV-estimate, Intersect/Except heuristic fraction.
  • Propagation: Memo::insert→derive_logical_props (Phase 2) calls estimate_cardinality(memo,mexpr,cat), populates LogicalProps::stats+Cardinality. Missing stats: is_estimated=true, heuristic defaults row_count=1000,distinct_count=100,histogram=None, never panic.
  • CLI: quasar opt --stats "<sql>" loads+validates catalog (schema, null_count<=row_count, NDV<=row_count), annotates per-group row-count in memo dump; opt-test stats directive gates golden estimates. No new verb — validation is a side effect of --stats (locked surface: opt/explain/memo/opt-test/bench).

5.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: Ioannidis & Poosala 1995 (equi-depth histogram construction algorithm — Bucket struct), Flajolet et al. 2007 / HLL++ (NDV estimation in hll.rs), Leis et al. 2015 "How Good Are Query Optimizers, Really?" (q-error metric; §4.7 acceptance targets), Vengerov et al. 2015 (join cardinality sampling as fallback reference), ADR-A09 (HLL++ NDV estimation choice), ADR-A10 (equi-depth over max-diff histograms), ADR-A11 (multi-column stats scope), P7 (statistics honesty — missing stats never crash), I2 (LogicalProps immutable per group — stats computed once in derive_logical_props).

  • quasar-stats files (crates/quasar-stats/src/) — lib.rs (re-exports, StatCatalog); catalog.rs (StatCatalog{tables:HashMap<TableId,TableStats>}, load_from_file()); table_stats.rs (TableStats{row_count,col_stats:HashMap<ColSet,ColumnStatistic>}); column_stat.rs (ColumnStatistic, Histogram, Bucket); hll.rs (HyperLogLog: cardinality() from sketch bytes); selectivity.rs (per-predicate + compound estimator); cardinality.rs (per-operator estimation dispatch); propagate.rs (integrates quasar-props: derive Statistics per operator); tests/selectivity_tests.rs (equality/range/null/IN/OR), tests/cardinality_tests.rs (per-operator goldens), tests/tpch_lite_tests.rs (TPC-H Q1..Q5, q-error threshold), tests/missing_stats_tests.rs (missing col/table paths).
  • StatCatalog loading — load_from_file(path)->Result<StatCatalog,OptError>: serde_json parse, schema {tables:[{id,row_count,col_stats:[{cols,distinct_count,null_count,histogram:[{upper_bound,num_eq,num_range,distinct_range}],avg_size,hll_sketch:"<base64>"}]}]}; HLL optional, present→HLL::from_bytes(b64)?.cardinality() overwrites distinct_count; multi-col (cols.len>1) keyed by ColSet for join/group-by estimation; StatCatalog passed by Arc into Memo at construction, query lifetime.
  • HyperLogLog — thin wrapper over HLL crate: HLL::from_bytes(&[u8])->Result<HLL,StatError> deserialize, cardinality()->f64 NDV; no query-time compute, offline sketch file, hll.rs read-only at query time.
  • Selectivity (selectivity.rs, per predicate, no bodies) — Equality(col,const): 1.0/distinct_count clamp [1/row_count,1.0]. Range(col,lo,hi): sum interior bucket num_range + interpolate boundary /row_count (no histogram→1/3). IS NULL(col): null_count/row_count. IN(col,vals): OR of equalities 1-(1-1/NDV)^|vals|, cap 1.
  • OR(p1,p2): s1+s2-s1*s2 (independence). AND(p1..pN): product (independence). LIKE/regex: 0.01 heuristic when no histogram. Column=Column: join predicate → join cardinality, not selectivity. Unknown: 0.1 + is_estimated=true.
  • Cardinality (cardinality.rs, estimate(memo,mexpr,cat)->Statistics) — Scan(t): cat.tables[t].row_count. IndexScan(t,idx,constraint,tight): tight row_count*selectivity(constraint.spans), loose ×residual selectivity. Select(input,pred): input.row_count*selectivity(pred). Project: same row_count, per-col distinct_count unchanged.
  • InnerJoin(L,R,on): L.rc*R.rc/max(NDV_L(jc),NDV_R(jc)), multi-key→min. LeftJoin: max(inner_card,L.row_count). SemiJoin: L.rc*selectivity(on) cap L.rc. AntiJoin: L.rc*(1-selectivity(on)).
  • GroupBy(input,gcols,..): NDV(gcols), multi-col min(product_NDVs,input.rc). ScalarGroupBy: 1. Distinct(input,cols): NDV(cols). Sort/Limit(input,n): min(input.rc,n).
  • UnionAll(L,R): L.rc+R.rc. Union(L,R): min(L.rc+R.rc,max(NDV_L,NDV_R)). Intersect(L,R): min(L.rc,R.rc)*0.1 (heuristic). Values(rows): len(rows). Missing stats: row_count=1000,distinct_count=100,is_estimated=true.
  • Propagation (propagate.rs) — propagate_stats(memo,mexpr,cat)->(Statistics,Cardinality): calls estimate(), sets Cardinality{min:0,max:stats.row_count} (1..1 for ScalarGroupBy); derive_logical_props sets LogicalProps::stats+::cardinality; called once at group creation in Memo::insert.
  • CLI integration — quasar analyze <stat-file>: load+validate, print summary (row counts, col count, bucket count). quasar opt --stats "<sql>": augment quasar memo dump with stats.row_count per group. quasar-obs: info_span!("stat_catalog_load",path,tables).

5.3 Deliverable

  • quasar analyze <stat-file> → table stats summary; error on schema mismatch. quasar opt --stats "<sql>" → memo dump with row_count per group.
  • quasar opt-test conformance/testdata/stats/*.opt → stats directive matches golden estimates. cargo test -p quasar-stats → selectivity, cardinality, missing-stats, TPC-H-lite pass.

5.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • Selectivity goldens: equality/range/null/IN/OR within 1e-6 FP tolerance. Join cardinality: inner join within 3× true on TPC-H-lite Q1..Q5.
  • GroupBy NDV ≤ input rows, ≥ 1. Missing stats no panic: no stat file → is_estimated=true, no unwrap panic.
  • HLL cardinality within 2% true NDV on fixture sketch (512 registers). Propagation completeness: every Group (catalog-built memo) has LogicalProps::stats.is_some().
  • Stat load latency: load_from_file < 50 ms for 10-table, 50-col catalog. q-error bound: median ≤ 2× on TPC-H-lite Q1..Q5.
  • Invariant I4: LogicalProps::stats populated at group creation, never read before set.

5.5 Conformance Fixtures

All under conformance/testdata/stats/, directive stats (file → purpose: expected):

  • sel_equality.opt → a=5, 1000 rows, NDV=100: sel≈0.01, rows≈10. sel_range.opt → a BETWEEN 10 AND 20 via histogram: fraction of range.
