  • sel_null.opt → a IS NULL, null=50/1000: 0.05. sel_in.opt → a IN (1,2,3), NDV=50: ≈1-(49/50)^3.
  • sel_or.opt → a=1 OR b=2: s_a+s_b-s_a*s_b. card_join.opt → R(1000)⋈S(500) on k, NDV 200: ≈2500.
  • card_groupby.opt → GROUP BY a,b, NDV(a)=10,NDV(b)=5: ≤50. card_distinct.opt → DISTINCT a, NDV=30: 30.
  • card_limit.opt → LIMIT 10 on 1000 rows: 10. tpch_q1_lite.opt → TPC-H Q1 chain: within 3× ref.
  • tpch_q3_lite.opt → TPC-H Q3 join chain: within 3×. missing_col_stat.opt → column no stat entry: is_estimated=true, no panic.
  • missing_table_stat.opt → table absent: heuristic 1000, is_estimated=true. hll_sketch.opt → file includes HLL bytes: distinct_count from HLL.

5.6 Pitfalls

  • Selectivity clamp missing → 1/NDV→Inf/0.0 at NDV=0 (unguarded divide; signal: fixture distinct_count=0 clamped) → clamp distinct_count=max(.,1.0) before divide, debug-assert >0. Join cardinality explosion → estimate >> true, wrong join order (high NDV on non-join-key as denominator; signal: dump shows row_count=1e15) → join-key-specific NDV, multi-col stats narrow.
  • Histogram boundary error → off-by-one on upper_bound, wrong fraction (inclusive vs exclusive confusion; signal: range fixture exact answer) → test BETWEEN lo AND lo → num_eq only. Multi-col stat key mismatch → ColSet ordering differs, lookup miss (non-canonical ordering; signal: fixture GROUP BY (a,b) finds two-col stat) → canonicalize ColSet by sorted ColumnId, assert on load.
  • is_estimated not propagated → parent uses child row_count as authoritative (no OR-propagate; signal: parent is_estimated=false despite estimation) → parent.is_estimated = children.any(|c| c.stats.is_estimated). Float underflow deep conjunction → s1*..*sN→0.0 on 20+ AND (IEEE-754 underflow; signal: metric 0.0 for complex filter) → log space exp(sum(log(si))), clamp 1e-15.
  • HLL incompatible version → from_bytes Err, cardinality→0.0 (different HLL lib version; signal: warn + stat_hll_parse_fail) → use JSON distinct_count, continue is_estimated=true.

5.7 Performance Concerns

  • HashMap<ColSet,_> lookup per group → pre-index single-col ColumnId->&ColumnStatistic, multi-col only when needed. StatCatalog clone per query → Arc<StatCatalog> shared, invalidate on refresh.
  • Histogram binary search (≤256 buckets) → cache-friendly Vec, ~8 comparisons, acceptable. Float math (exp/log) → pre-compute log(selectivity) per col on load, combine in log space.
  • Cold-start JSON parse → parse once at startup, cache Arc, async load (Phase 13 quasar-cache). Redundant estimation → set once at group creation, read Group::logical.stats, never re-estimate.

5.8 Edge Cases

  • Row count = 0 → Cardinality.min=0,max=0 propagates, no divide-by-zero downstream. Row count = 1 → cap GroupBy min(NDV_estimate,input.rc).
  • null_count > row_count → validate on load → StatError::Invalid. Histogram 0 buckets → check is_empty() before binary search, fall back 1/NDV.
  • NDV > row_count → distinct_count = min(.,row_count) on load. Correlated a>5 AND a<10 → multi-range merge in histogram, not product; Phase 6 constraint solver handles.
  • Placeholder a=$1 → 1/NDV as avg-value equality, is_estimated=true. Stat file updated mid-query → per-query Arc<StatCatalog> immutable, refresh affects new queries (Phase 13 invalidation).

5.9 Failure Analysis

  • Bad stat file (parse error) → affected tables use heuristic estimates (detect: load_from_file Err, quasar analyze reports) → is_estimated=true path, stat_load_error metric; fix file, reload, quasar analyze pre-deploy. Cardinality wildly wrong → wrong join order, slow query (detect: quasar_stats_q_error histogram) → refresh stats, update histogram, add fixture.
  • distinct_count=0 divide crash → single query wrong plan (unguarded 1/0.0=Inf, Inf cost) → clamp guard, stat_ndv_zero_hit metric; fix clamp, release (library). HLL parse fails silently → minor inaccuracy (distinct_count stays JSON value; detect: stat_hll_parse_fail_total) → WARN log, surface in quasar analyze.
  • Stats not propagated → Phase 7/10 crash (LogicalProps::stats None, cost-model unwrap) → Option<Statistics> + explicit missing handling; propagate_stats per operator in derive_logical_props.

5.10 Operational Notes

  • Tracing: debug_span!("stats_estimate",op,group) per call; warn!(table,"stat_missing") on missing table. Metrics: quasar_stat_catalog_tables (gauge), quasar_stat_estimate_total{is_estimated} (counter), quasar_stat_q_error histogram (conformance harness post-run), quasar_stat_hll_parse_fail_total (counter).
  • Stat file format: JSON "version":1, future increments, v1 backward compat in Phase 13. Stat refresh: Phase 5 manual quasar analyze --load <file>, auto staleness → Phase 13 quasar-cache.
  • No network I/O: Phase 5 reads local files only, no live collection. Deterministic replay: fixture load deterministic, Phase 13 replay snapshots stat-file path with query.
  • Test fixtures: canonical conformance/testdata/stats/fixtures/*.json, checked in, not auto-generated.

18. Phase 6 — Index Constraint / Span Solver — quasar-constraint

6.1 Objectives

Turn filter predicates over index key columns into Constraint + Spans + remaining scalar filter. Build spans column-by-column over key prefix; AND = intersect spans + extend prefix; OR = union spans; handle =,<,<=,>,>=,IN,IS NULL,IS NOT NULL, tuple comparisons. Mark tight (constraint fully captures predicate, remaining empty) vs loose (residual scalar). Computed-col constraints (rewrite over stored expr). Partial-index applicability (partial predicate implied by query predicate). Inverted-index span surface (JSON/array containment, trigram). Deliverable: quasar explain --opt renders spans + remaining filter; opt-test index-constraints directive. Acceptance: a=5 AND b>10 ON INDEX(a,b) → [/5/11 - /5/∞]; unsatisfiable → empty Spans; tightness flagged correctly.

  • a = 5 → [/5 - /5], tight. a > 3 → (/3 - /∞), tight.
  • a IN (1,3,5) → union 3 point spans, tight. a = 5 AND b > 10 → [/5/10 - /5] (excl lower b), tight.
  • lower(a) = 'x' (computed) → rewrite to stored col span, tight. a LIKE 'abc%' → prefix [/abc - /abd), loose.
  • partial idx WHERE active → applicable if query implies active=true. inverted @> '{"k":1}' → InvertedSpan on key path, loose.

6.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: Silberschatz Ch. 15 (index access path cost — grounds the tight/loose constraint distinction), ADR-A13 (dedicated quasar-constraint crate design), P1 (cost-based: index constraint enables cost comparison between IndexScan and TableReader), I5 (required physical properties satisfied — constraint solver feeds IndexScan to satisfy ordering/distribution props), Neumann & Kemper 2015 (subquery decorrelation informs scope of remaining-filter design).

  • Crate quasar-constraint. Deps: quasar-ir (ScalarExpr/Datum), quasar-cat (IndexId, partial predicate, computed-col exprs), quasar-props (LogicalProps / not-null sets).
  • Files/modules — src/lib.rs (re-exports, public ConstraintSolver); src/columns.rs (ColumnConstraint per-column, SpanSet sorted Vec<Span> merge-on-insert); src/solver.rs (solve(pred:ScalarId,index:&IndexDef,memo:&Memo,cat:&dyn Catalog)->SolveResult); src/intersect.rs (AND: per-col intersection, short-circuit on empty); src/union.rs (OR: per-col union, sort+merge-overlapping); src/inverted.rs (InvertedSpan + inverted applicability); src/partial.rs (partial-index implication: syntactic subsumption + prop-implies); src/computed.rs (rewrite virtual-col predicate → stored expr).
  • Algorithm solve — 1. decompose ScalarExpr: AND→intersect column-by-column, OR→union, NOT→complement (flip boundaries, invert direction), leaf→single_column_span. 2. single_column_span: Comparison{op,l:Column(c),r:Const(d)}→Span, constant-fold Cast, IS NULL→[/NULL - /NULL]. 3. prefix extension: process key cols left-to-right, col constrainable only if all prior key cols equality-constrained, trailing col may be range. 4. tightness: tight iff every comparison captured by a span (no conjunct left), record remaining:ScalarId for residual post-scan filter.
    1. IN expansion: union of point spans, cap IN_EXPANSION_LIMIT=256, exceed → range span + loose. 6. tuple (a,b)<(x,y) → multi-col span (lexicographic upper bound). 7. computed cols: catalog ComputedColDef{col,expr}, substitute, solve normally. 8. partial applicability: implies(query_pred,index.partial_predicate,props) (syntactic exact-match + not-null entailment), false → skip index. 9. inverted: detect @>,?,&&, delegate inverted::solve→InvertedSpan, always loose. 10. obs: debug! span constraint_solve{index_id,span_count,tight}.
  • Interfaces — pub struct SolveResult{pub constraint:Constraint,pub remaining:Option<ScalarId>}; pub fn solve(pred:ScalarId,index:&IndexDef,memo:&Memo,cat:&dyn Catalog)->SolveResult; pub fn spans_are_empty(c:&Constraint)->bool; pub fn implies(outer:ScalarId,inner:ScalarId,props:&LogicalProps)->bool.
  • Integration (Phase 8 GenerateIndexScans) — quasar-rules calls solve when generating IndexScan alternatives for Scan+Select.

6.3 Deliverable

  • crates/quasar-constraint/ compiles; public APIs stable.
  • opt-test <file> directive index-constraints renders per-index Constraint{spans,tight} + remaining.
  • quasar explain --opt "SELECT * FROM t WHERE a=5 AND b>10" shows [/5/10 - /5] for INDEX(a,b).
  • quasar memo "<sql>" shows span annotations on IndexScan MExprs.
  • --index-constraints-debug prints per-column solve trace.

6.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • Goldens pass (opt-test conformance/testdata/index-constraints/*.opt-test): 100%. Unsatisfiable → spans_are_empty → no IndexScan emitted: 5+ fixtures.
  • Tight correct: tight → remaining=None; remaining!=None → tight=false (I5): 100%. IN ≤256 → tight point spans; >256 → range + loose: unit.
  • Partial-index negative: not applicable when predicate doesn't imply (fixture). solve ≤50 µs/predicate on 10-col index (p99, criterion).
  • No heap alloc for 1-col equality (dhat). Tuple span correct for 2-col (a,b) (golden).

6.5 Conformance Fixtures

Under conformance/testdata/index-constraints/ (file → purpose: expected):

  • basic_equality.opt-test → a=5 1-col: [/5 - /5] tight. range_compound.opt-test → a=5 AND b>10 2-col: [/5/10 - /5] tight.
  • or_union.opt-test → a=1 OR a=3: union 2 point spans. in_expansion.opt-test → a IN (1..300): range + loose beyond limit.
  • unsatisfiable.opt-test → a>10 AND a<5: empty spans. partial_index.opt-test → WHERE active=true on INDEX WHERE active: applicable.
  • partial_index_neg.opt-test → no active predicate: not applicable. computed_col.opt-test → lower(name)='x' computed col: span on stored col.
  • inverted_json.opt-test → data @> '{"k":1}': InvertedSpan, loose. null_handling.opt-test → a IS NULL: [/NULL - /NULL] tight.
  • tuple_compare.opt-test → (a,b) < (3,7): multi-col upper bound. regression_*.opt-test → bug regressions: vary.

6.6 Pitfalls

  • Span boundary direction → extra/missing rows (off-by-one Inclusive/Exclusive after NOT-complement; signal: boundary fixture golden diff) → explicit boundary enum, unit-test complement. False tight on LIKE prefix → post-scan filter skipped, wrong results (tight marked when suffix uncaptured; signal: remaining=None but rows differ) → assert LIKE → always loose, fixture.
  • IN cap not applied → span explosion, O(n) solve (missing cap guard; signal: span_count spike) → enforce IN_EXPANSION_LIMIT, in_expansion_truncated. Computed col circular rewrite → stack overflow/loop (computed col references another; signal: recursion depth counter) → depth limit 10, OptError::Unsupported.
  • Partial false positive → wrong index, corrupt results (implies syntactic-only, missing negation; signal: correctness mismatch) → propositional-implication, test NOT active. Multi-col prefix break → trailing col constrained without prior equality (prefix check bug; signal: span len > contiguous prefix) → strict prefix-walk, assert col[i-1] equality.
  • Null span semantics → IS NULL intersected wrong with NOT NULL (NULL sorts first in CRDB key space; signal: nullable-col fixture) → special null-span, consult not_null from LogicalProps.

6.7 Performance Concerns

  • Span merge: SpanSet insert keeps sorted; O(n log n) for large OR. IN n≤256 sort+scan cache-friendly (Vec<Span>).
  • Allocation: SolveResult/Constraint once per index per call. SmallVec<[Span;4]> avoids heap for ≤4 spans (common 1-col).
  • Recursion depth: deep OR-of-IN chains; iterate with explicit stack if depth >64.
  • Inverted solve: JSON-containment tokenization; cap token count 32/call; profile separately.
  • Catalog calls: ComputedColDef + partial_predicate cached in IndexDef (loaded in quasar-cat); not re-fetched.
  • implies cost: O(predicate size); fast-exit if partial predicate TRUE.

6.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty index key (degenerate) → single all-encompassing span, tight=false. NULL in IN list → include [/NULL - /NULL], mark nullable.
  • Predicate refs non-index column → that conjunct goes to remaining, other cols unaffected. Cast on Datum (CAST(a AS bigint)>5) → const-fold Datum side; col-side cast → loose.
  • NOT IN (...) → complement of union → n+1 spans. Overlapping OR spans → merge in SpanSet::insert → single span.
  • DESC key direction → flip span boundary direction in key encoding. Index dropped mid-solve → quasar-cat CatalogMissing → OptError::CatalogMissing.
  • Zero-length string literal → [/'' - /''] tight equality, not NULL.

6.9 Failure Analysis

  • solve panic on malformed ScalarExpr → single query, others unaffected (detect: #[should_panic], ERROR span) → catch at GenerateIndexScans → OptError::Unsupported; fix root cause, regression fixture. Empty span returned non-empty → wrong results in exec (detect: golden diff, 0 rows wrongly) → spans_are_empty before emit, WARN constraint_empty_but_nonflagged; empty → skip IndexScan.
  • Tight wrong (loose→tight) → missing rows (detect: correctness test fails) → post-scan filter always applied when loose; conservative default tight=false unless provable. implies false negative → performance only, wrong index skipped, suboptimal (detect: EXPLAIN plan diff) → widen implication, partial_index_skipped.
  • Solver hangs on cyclic predicate → single query blocked (detect: bench timeout) → depth-limit guard returns error <1 ms; cycle detection, fuzz target over ScalarExpr.

6.10 Operational Notes

  • Tracing: every solve → debug_span!("constraint_solve",index_id,tight,span_count). Metrics: quasar_constraint_spans_generated_total{index_type}, quasar_constraint_tight_ratio, quasar_constraint_in_expansion_truncated_total.
  • Event log: quasar-obs records ConstraintSolved{index_id,span_count,tight,remaining_is_some}, replayed (Phase 13). Deployment: pure library, no network I/O, no migration; activated when Phase 8 rules call solve.
  • Compatibility: SolveResult internal (not serialized); Constraint schema change → bump memo dump version field. Rollback: disable index type via feature flag constraint_inverted_enabled/constraint_partial_enabled → full scan.
  • Testing cadence: opt-test conformance/testdata/index-constraints/ per PR; fuzz_constraint_solve 60 s in CI.

19. Phase 7 — Cost Model — quasar-cost

7.1 Objectives

Assign Cost{cpu,io,net,total} to every physical MExpr; total = branch-and-bound comparison key. Equations consume Statistics (Phase 5) + PhysProps (Phase 9); Coster trait decouples cost from search. Constants in costs.toml (tunable): cpu_row,cpu_seek,io_page,io_seek,net_rpc,page_size,hash_table_mem_limit,sort_mem_limit, each documented as formula comment. Deliverable: quasar explain --opt prints per-node cost breakdown. Acceptance: cost regression goldens; total monotonicity (I4); MergeJoin beats HashJoin on sorted-input fixtures.

Per operator (cpu | io | net | notes):

  • TableReader: rows*cpu_row | pages*io_page | 0 (local) | pages=rows*avg_row_size/page_size. IndexScan: rows*cpu_row+seeks*cpu_seek | seeks*io_seek+pages*io_page | 0 | seeks=span_count, tight reduces rows.
  • HashJoin: (build+probe)*cpu_row | spill_pages*io_page if mem exceeded | net if input crosses node | build capped at hash_table_mem_limit. MergeJoin: (left+right)*cpu_row | 0 (streaming) | 0 if co-located | beats HashJoin when ordering provided.
  • LookupJoin: input_rows*cpu_lookup | input_rows*io_seek | input_rows*net_rpc if remote | net dominates distributed, prefer when input small. Sort: n*log2(n)*cpu_row | spill_io if rows>sort_mem_limit | 0 | spill_io=spill_pages*io_page.
  • HashAgg: rows*cpu_row | spill_io if mem exceeded | 0 | —. StreamAgg: rows*cpu_row*0.5 | 0 | 0 | half cpu vs HashAgg, ordered input.
  • Distinct: rows*cpu_row | 0 | 0 | hash-based, HashAgg spill model. Limit(n): caps parent via limit_hint in PhysProps | — | — | propagate limit_hint=n downward. UnionAll: sum of inputs | — | — | —.

7.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: Selinger 1979 (original access-path cost model — seek + page-scan cost forms), PostgreSQL optimizer (cost_seqscan / cost_index calibration reference), CockroachDB cost model design doc (per-operator constants; net-cost multiplier), ADR-A08 (Cost{cpu,io,net,total} struct design), Zhou et al. 2010 SCOPE (distribution-aware cost partition property), I4 (cost monotonicity — Coster::cost never returns negative delta), I7 (B&B soundness depends on lower_bound ≤ achievable cost).

  • Crate quasar-cost. Deps: quasar-ir, quasar-memo, quasar-stats, quasar-props, quasar-cat.
  • Files/modules — src/lib.rs (Coster trait, DefaultCoster, Cost arithmetic — Add, PartialOrd); src/constants.rs (CostConstants loaded from costs.toml via config crate); src/operator_cost.rs (one cost_* per family: scan, index_scan, hash_join, merge_join, lookup_join, sort, hash_agg, stream_agg, distinct, limit, set_ops); src/memory_model.rs (spill_pages(rows,row_size,mem_limit)->f64 for hash/sort operators); src/net_cost.rs (net_cost(rows,row_size,distribution:&Distribution,provided:&Distribution)->f64: 0 if same region, rows*avg_row_size*net_rpc_per_byte if crossing); src/regression.rs (cost_regression_check(expected,actual,tol)->bool).
  • Algorithm per operator — 1. extract Statistics from memo.group(child).logical.stats. 2. avg_row_size = sum ColumnStatistic.avg_size over output cols. 3. apply formula, floor at f64::EPSILON (avoid zero-cost cycles in B&B). 4. Limit: if PhysProps.limit_hint < f64::MAX, scale scan/sort by min(1.0,limit_hint/row_count).
    1. LookupJoin net: per input row one RPC to leaseholder, net=input_rows*net_rpc+input_rows*io_seek. 6. spill: if rows*avg_row_size > mem_limit {spill_pages=(rows*avg_row_size-mem_limit)/page_size} else 0. 7. total=cpu+io+net, net weighted NET_WEIGHT=10.0 (cross-region RPC = 10× cpu unit). 8. distribution-aware: provided.distribution==required.distribution→net=0, else add net_cost(...).
    1. Constants loaded once at startup into Arc<CostConstants>; each optimize() snapshots Arc at entry, threads immutable snapshot through every cost() (no RwLock, no per-call lock); hot-reload (SIGHUP, bench) swaps process-wide Arc for next optimize(), in-flight keep snapshot (no mid-query change — 7.6/7.8).
  • Interfaces — pub trait Coster: Send+Sync { fn cost(&self,memo:&Memo,expr:ExprId,required:&PhysProps)->Cost; }; pub struct DefaultCoster{constants:Arc<CostConstants>} (impls Coster); pub fn load_constants(path:&Path)->Result<CostConstants,OptError>.
  • Integration — quasar-search holds Box<dyn Coster>; calls coster.cost(memo,expr,required) during OptimizeExpr; stores in Winner.

7.3 Deliverable

  • crates/quasar-cost/ compiles; Coster + DefaultCoster stable.
  • quasar explain --opt "<sql>" → TableReader cost=(cpu=1.2, io=0.8, net=0.0, total=2.0).
  • quasar bench tpch cost-only benchmark (no execution) over TPC-H templates; prints plan + costs.
  • costs.toml in workspace root, all constants + formula comments.
  • quasar opt-test conformance/testdata/cost/ cost-regression directive.

7.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • Cost regression goldens: 100%. total monotonicity (I4): parent ≥ max child, all 16 operators, property test.
  • MergeJoin < HashJoin on pre-sorted input (sort_order provided), confirmed. LookupJoin < HashJoin for input_rows<1000 on remote (fixture).
  • StreamAgg < HashAgg when input ordered by grouping cols (fixture). Zero-cost floor: no Cost.total == 0.0 (unit).
  • cost() ≤2 µs p99 (no I/O, no lock contention; criterion). costs.toml missing → OptError::Unsupported, clear message (unit).
  • Net=0 when distribution matches (unit on co-located plan).

7.5 Conformance Fixtures

Under conformance/testdata/cost/ (file → purpose: expected):

  • scan_basic.opt-test → TableReader formula: cpu,io,total within 1% formula. index_scan_seeks.opt-test → IndexScan 3 spans: seek cost component present.
  • hash_vs_merge_join.opt-test → MergeJoin wins when order provided: MergeJoin selected, lower cost. lookup_join_net.opt-test → LookupJoin net RPC: net>0 for remote leaseholder.
  • sort_spill.opt-test → Sort rows>sort_mem_limit: spill io >0. limit_hint.opt-test → Limit(10) on large scan: scan cost scaled down.
  • stream_agg_wins.opt-test → StreamAgg over HashAgg when ordered: StreamAgg in plan. net_weight.opt-test → cross-region join: net component largest.
  • regression_*.opt-test → bug regressions: vary.

7.6 Pitfalls

  • Zero-cost operator → B&B loops/wrong plan (missing floor; signal: total==0.0 in Winner) → floor EPSILON, unit test. Stats unavailable → NaN → NaN propagates, comparisons false (row_count=0.0 denominator; signal: NaN check in cost()) → default row_count=1.0, StatsUnavailable.
  • net underweighted → HashJoin over LookupJoin remote small (NET_WEIGHT too low; signal: EXPLAIN unexpected cross-region HashJoin) → tune NET_WEIGHT, bench with real RPC latency. limit_hint not propagated → full sort despite top-N (not threaded into child; signal: Sort cost = full n*log(n)) → enforce propagation in search task.
  • spill_pages underestimated → uncosted spills, wrong choice (avg_row_size too small, nullable cols; signal: actual exec spills) → include null bitmap bytes in avg_row_size. Hot-reload race → stale constants mid-optimize (Arc<RwLock> write during cost(); signal: inconsistency within one optimize()) → snapshot at optimize() entry, no mid-call reload.
  • MergeJoin always cheaper (bug) → MergeJoin without provided ordering (missing sort-enforcer cost; signal: EXPLAIN Sort+MergeJoin < HashJoin) → include Sort enforcer cost when ordering not provided.

7.7 Performance Concerns

  • cost() called N×M (N groups × M required-props) during search; <2 µs: no I/O, no per-call alloc.
  • CostConstants arc: Arc::clone at construction; single Arc::load in cost(); never lock per call (snapshot, not RwLock).
  • f64 arithmetic: simple mul/add; auto-vectorizes in tight loops; avoid pow/log except Sort.
  • log2(n) Sort: pre-compute (row_count).log2() once; cache if Sort repeats in same group.
  • Statistics lookup: memo.group(g).logical.stats O(1) field access (Phase 5 writes once); no recompute.
  • Memory: Cost = 4×f64 = 32 bytes; register pair; no heap alloc.

7.8 Edge Cases

  • row_count=0.0 (empty table) → floor 1.0 for cost, cost >0. row_count=f64::INFINITY → cap MAX_ROWS=1e12.
  • limit_hint=0 → cost = EPSILON floor, valid. avg_row_size=0 (no output cols) → min MIN_ROW_BYTES=8.
  • Cross-region dist with single-region required → net = full transfer, never zero. HashJoin build > memory, spill exceeds budget → OptError::BudgetExceeded, try LookupJoin.
  • NaN in Statistics (corrupt) → pre-check in cost() → OptError::StatsUnavailable. Constants file updated between optimize() calls → both use snapshot, no mid-query inconsistency.

7.9 Failure Analysis

  • Wrong operator (cost bug) → all queries using operator (detect: plan regression on TPC-H golden) → per-operator cost-override flag for A/B; fix formula, update goldens, regression test. NaN/Inf escapes to Winner → B&B picks arbitrary plan (detect: Winner.cost.total.is_nan() assert) → assert in OptimizeExpr on Winner insert; sanitize, WARN cost_nan_detected.
  • costs.toml missing in prod → all queries fail (detect: OptError::Unsupported at startup) → embed default constants in binary; compile-time default constant table. Budget exceeded on legit query → single query fails (detect: OptError::BudgetExceeded to caller) → caller retries relaxed budget/heuristic plan; optimization_budget_exceeded_total metric.
  • Hot-reload corrupt toml → zero impact (detect: parse error, retain old constants) → ERROR cost_constants_reload_failed, keep snapshot; validate on load, reject if any constant ≤0.

7.10 Operational Notes

  • Tracing: trace_span!("cost_operator",expr_id,operator,total) per cost(), sampled 1% in production. Metrics: quasar_cost_computations_total{operator}, quasar_cost_nan_total, quasar_cost_budget_exceeded_total, quasar_cost_constants_reload_total.
  • Event log: quasar-obs records CostAssigned{expr_id,cost,required_props_hash} (Phase 13 replay), verifies identical cost given same stats + constants. costs.toml management: version-controlled, changes require cost-regression golden update (quasar opt-test --update-goldens), document each constant's units + calibration.
  • Deployment: library dep of quasar-search, no separate step; costs.toml shipped as embedded default, override via --cost-constants. Rollback: redeploy previous costs.toml, no schema migration.
  • Compatibility: Cost not serialized externally, internal to memo, safe to extend (add mem:f64 later).

20. Phase 8 — Exploration Rules (logical) — join reordering + GenerateIndexScans

8.1 Objectives

Cost-based logical→logical exploration rules firing in search (Phase 10 activates search; Phase 8 defines rules). Tagged [Explore] in .opt; generated by quasar-optgen; dispatched via quasar-rules. Each rule adds new MExpr members to a Group without removing existing (memo monotonicity I1). Must preserve logical equivalence (same out_cols, same LogicalProps cardinality bounds). Join-order enumeration uses commutativity + associativity rules (transformation-based, NOT a separate DP pass — ADR-A07: Cascades already memoizes DP implicitly; a second DP pass would duplicate logic and break MEMO invariants). Reorder limit: join-subtree group count > JOIN_REORDER_LIMIT=8 → log reorder_limit_reached, suppress further assoc/commute for that group. Deliverable: opt-test explore directive shows added members. Acceptance: explore goldens (join orders enumerated, IndexScan alternatives); equivalence invariant holds; reorder-limit fallback logged + observable.

Rule inventory (all Explore kind):

  • CommuteJoin: A⋈B → B⋈A (inner/semi/anti); swaps children. AssociateJoinLeft: (A⋈B)⋈C → A⋈(B⋈C) left rotation. AssociateJoinRight: A⋈(B⋈C) → (A⋈B)⋈C right rotation.
  • GenerateIndexScans: Select(Scan(t),pred) → IndexScan(t,idx,spans,remaining) per applicable index via Phase 6 solver. PushLimitIntoScan: Limit(Scan(t),n) → Scan(t,hard_limit=n). PushLimitIntoIndexScan: Limit(IndexScan(t,idx,..),n) → IndexScan(..,hard_limit=n).
  • GeneratePartialDistinctAgg: GroupBy(Join(A,B),g,agg) → GroupBy(GroupBy(A,g,partial),g,final) when decomposable. PushAggThroughJoin: push partial agg below join when agg cols ⊆ join key cols.
  • DistinctToGroupBy: Distinct(input,cols) → GroupBy(input,cols,[]). GenerateOrToUnion: Select(input,a OR b) → Union(Select(a),Select(b)) when each branch uses a different index.

8.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: Graefe 1995 Cascades (Explore rule kind — logical→logical, ExploreGroup/ExploreExpr tasks), Selinger 1979 (join commutativity + associativity as the original interesting-order rewrite basis), Moerkotte & Neumann 2008 DPhyp (hypergraph join ordering — the alternative to transformation rules studied in ADR-A07), ADR-A04 (optgen [Explore] tag), ADR-A07 (transformation rules vs DPccp design decision), I3 (normalization confluent — exploration rules NOT required to be confluent), P3 (Explore fires only in ExploreExpr, not factory).

  • Crates touched — quasar-rules (.opt + dispatch), quasar-optgen (build.rs DSL compiler, [Explore] tag), quasar-constraint (GenerateIndexScans), quasar-cat (index enumeration), quasar-memo (MExpr insertion), quasar-props (equivalence check), quasar-obs (rule fire events).
  • Files/modules — crates/quasar-rules/src/explore/ (one .opt per family: join_reorder.opt, index_scans.opt, limit_pushdown.opt, agg_rewrite.opt, or_to_union.opt); crates/quasar-rules/src/explore/dispatch.rs (generated: apply_explore(rule:RuleId,expr:ExprId,memo:&mut Memo,cat:&dyn Catalog)->Vec<MExpr>); crates/quasar-rules/build.rs (compiles [Explore] rules, emits EXPLORE_RULES:&[RuleDef]); crates/quasar-optgen/src/explore_codegen.rs (emitter: pattern → transform → memo.add_expr).
  • Algorithm GenerateIndexScans — 1. match Select{input:Scan(t),filters:pred}. 2. enumerate all indexes on t (primary+secondary+partial+inverted+computed-col). 3. per index, quasar-constraint::solve(pred,index,memo,cat)→SolveResult{constraint,remaining}. 4. constraint.spans empty → skip (unsatisfiable).
    1. build IndexScanOp{table:t,index:idx,constraint,remaining:remaining.unwrap_or(TRUE),reverse:false,cols:scan.cols}. 6. add MExpr(IndexScan(op),children=[],scalar=Some(remaining_id)) to the Scan+Select group. 7. also reverse=true if index supports DESC scan (ordering props Phase 9). 8. debug! event index_scan_generated{table,index,span_count,tight}.
  • Algorithm join commute/associate — 1. CommuteJoin: InnerJoin(A,B,pred)→InnerJoin(B,A,pred) (no column remap — on uses GroupId refs, columns are global ColumnId). 2. AssociateJoinLeft: InnerJoin(InnerJoin(A,B,p1),C,p2)→InnerJoin(A,InnerJoin(B,C,p2'),p1') with predicate redistribution: B+C-only conjuncts go inner, rest outer. 3. redistribution: decompose AND conjuncts, push to side iff cols_referenced⊆left_out_cols∪right_out_cols.
    1. reorder guard: before emit, join_subtree_group_count(memo,group) > JOIN_REORDER_LIMIT → skip + obs::Event::ReorderLimitReached{group}. 5. rules_applied:RuleMask per MExpr tracks fired rules, prevents re-application in same iteration.
  • GenerateOrToUnion — 1. match Select(input,Or(a,b)) where each branch references different index-applicable cols. 2. emit UnionAll(Select(input,a),Select(input,b)), each child independently generates IndexScan alternatives. 3. fire only if both branches single-table predicates (multi-table OR unsupported; falls through).

8.3 Deliverable

  • All .opt explore files under crates/quasar-rules/src/explore/ compile via build.rs.
  • opt-test conformance/testdata/explore/<fixture>.opt-test explore directive renders added members.
  • quasar memo "<sql>" shows multiple MExpr members per group (join alternatives, IndexScan variants).
  • quasar explain --opt "<sql>" selects best join order + index; annotations show generating rule per node.
  • JOIN_REORDER_LIMIT hit: quasar opt --verbose shows reorder_limit_reached.

8.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • explore goldens: 100%. Commutativity: both orders in memo for A⋈B (unit, 2-table). Associativity: 3-table join → ≥3 order alternatives (golden).
  • GenerateIndexScans: each applicable index → one IndexScan MExpr (multi-index fixture). rules_applied prevents duplicate application (property: fire twice → same memo size).
  • Reorder limit: 9-table join logs reorder_limit_reached, memo bounded (fixture + metric). Equivalence (I1): new MExprs same out_cols as group (assert in memo.add_expr).
  • GenerateOrToUnion: a=1 OR b=2 → Union(IndexScan a, IndexScan b) (golden). Partial-agg pushdown: SUM below join (golden).

8.5 Conformance Fixtures

Under conformance/testdata/explore/ (file → purpose: expected):

  • join_commute.opt-test → 2-table inner join both orders: both A⋈B,B⋈A in group. join_assoc_3way.opt-test → 3-table order space: ≥3 order MExprs.
  • join_reorder_limit.opt-test → 9-table hits limit: reorder_limit_reached emitted. generate_index_scans.opt-test → 3-index table, a=5 AND b>1: 3 IndexScan alternatives + tight/loose noted.
  • index_scan_partial.opt-test → partial index applicable: IndexScan with partial index. index_scan_inapplicable.opt-test → no predicate matches index cols: no IndexScan added.
  • or_to_union.opt-test → a=1 OR b=2 two-index: UnionAll(IndexScan(a),IndexScan(b)). limit_push_scan.opt-test → Limit(Scan(t),10): Scan(t,hard_limit=10).
  • partial_agg_push.opt-test → decomposable SUM below join: GroupBy(A,partial_sum) + GroupBy(join,final_sum). distinct_to_groupby.opt-test → Distinct rewritten: GroupBy(input,cols,[]).
  • regression_*.opt-test → bug regressions: vary.

8.6 Pitfalls

  • Infinite exploration loop → search never terminates, memo unbounded (rules_applied not set before recursion, rule fires on own output; signal: memo.exprs.len() monotonic in trace, ExploreGroup task spike) → set rules_applied bit before emit, assert no growth beyond budget. Wrong predicate redistribution → wrong results, cross-product (conjunct pushed to wrong side, refs both; signal: golden diff, non-deterministic result) → push to inner only if cols⊆inner_left∪inner_right, test multi-col ON.
  • Duplicate IndexScan MExprs → memo inflated, wasted cost (same index added twice; signal: rules_applied not checked) → guard RuleMask::has(expr.rules_applied,rule_id). Join commute on outer joins → semantically wrong plan (Left/Right commute NOT symmetric, only inner/anti/semi valid; signal: extra rows) → restrict CommuteJoin to JoinKind::Inner, outer needs separate analysis.
  • Reorder limit too low for star schema → TPC-H star misses optimal order (=8 excludes some queries; signal: TPC-H plan regression) → raise to 12 for star (single large central table heuristic, ADR). GenerateOrToUnion on correlated subquery OR → invalid plan, dangling outer cols (outer_cols not propagated; signal: assert outer_cols in memo.add_expr) → check outer_cols.is_empty() before firing, skip if correlated.
  • Assoc + commute explosion → memo OOM for 7+ tables (n! alternatives before limit; signal: RSS spike) → reorder limit primary guard, also Group count ceiling.

8.7 Performance Concerns

  • Memo growth: each rule adds O(1) MExprs/Group; n-table join generates O(n!) before limit. Limit caps 8 tables → ≤40,320, bounded.
  • GenerateIndexScans/group: k indexes → k MExprs; each solve ≤50 µs (§6.7); total ≤k×50 µs/group — acceptable.
  • rules_applied RuleMask: 128-bit bitfield (≤128 rules); integer comparison; zero alloc.
  • Predicate redistribution: decompose AND → Vec<ScalarId> (reuses arena refs, no clones); O(conjunct_count)/application.
  • GenerateOrToUnion guard: fire only if each branch selectivity <0.1 (stats); avoids Union for low-selectivity OR better as filter.
  • Memo intern: memo.add_expr structural-hash lookup before insert; duplicate check O(1) amortized.

8.8 Edge Cases

  • Single-table (no joins) → commute/associate don't match, harmless. Cross join (ON=TRUE) → CommuteJoin applies, skip associate (non-useful); guard on=TRUE → skip AssociateJoin.
  • Self-join (t1 a JOIN t1 b) → distinct TableId per alias, per-alias ColumnId, no collision. View/CTE expanded by bind → WithScan{id} nodes; don't push rules through WithScan boundary (CTE semantics).
  • OR with 3+ branches → binary split (a OR b) OR c → Union(Union(a,b),c), depth bounded by OR nesting. Empty IN after normalization → IN () → FALSE → no MExpr (Phase 4).
  • Budget exhaustion mid-exploration → OptError::BudgetExceeded, partial memo valid, best-so-far extracted. Inverted-index scan in OR branch → GenerateOrToUnion emits IndexScan child, inverted loose, remaining filter applied.

8.9 Failure Analysis

  • Exploration loop (unbounded memo) → single query, search killed (detect: RSS>2 GB, optimize() timeout) → MAX_GROUPS=50_000 ceiling → BudgetExceeded; fix rules_applied bit, regression fuzz target. Incorrect join order (predicate moved) → all queries hitting join pattern (detect: wrong result set) → golden diff, disable AssociateJoin via feature flag; fix redistribution, fixture.
  • Rule MExpr not equivalent → single optimize() panic (detect: out_cols mismatch assert) → assert new_props.out_cols == group.logical.out_cols; fix transform, equivalence property test. GenerateOrToUnion invalid Union → exec type error (children different column sets) → quasar-bind Union schema type check; guard: emit only if both branches identical out_cols.
  • Missing IndexScan for applicable index → performance, suboptimal full scan (detect: EXPLAIN shows Scan where IndexScan expected) → verify quasar-cat returns all indexes, fixture, check solve non-empty.

8.10 Operational Notes

  • Tracing: debug_span!("apply_explore_rule",rule,expr_id) per application, child spans for GenerateIndexScans include index_id+span_count. Metrics: quasar_explore_rules_applied_total{rule_name}, quasar_memo_exprs_added_by_exploration_total, quasar_reorder_limit_reached_total, quasar_index_scans_generated_total{index_type}.
  • Event log: quasar-obs records ExploreRuleApplied{rule_id,expr_id,new_exprs_count}, replay verifies same MExprs (Phase 13). JOIN_REORDER_LIMIT tuning: --join-reorder-limit flag or QUASAR_JOIN_REORDER_LIMIT env, default 8, warn if >12.
  • Feature flags: disable individual rule via --disable-rule <RuleName> for A/B/bug isolation, RuleDef checked at dispatch. Deployment: pure library rules, no migration; new rule needs (1) .opt file, (2) golden fixture, (3) quasar opt-test --update-goldens.
  • Rollback: disable via feature flag, no data migration, memo shape internal only. Fuzz target: fuzz_explore_rules generates random bound RelExpr trees, asserts no panic + out_cols invariant, 60 s in CI.

21. Phase 9 — Physical Properties + Enforcers + Implementation Rules

9.1 Objectives

Logical→physical bridge: implementation rules mapping logical operators to physical alternatives; physical-property framework (top-down requirement propagation + bottom-up provision); enforcer insertion when required props unmet. Acceptance: quasar explain --opt renders physical operators; enforcer fixtures pass (ORDER BY without useful index → Sort node); merge join requests sorted children; ordering derivation goldens green.

  • Implementation rules (.opt [Implement]): InnerJoin→HashJoin/MergeJoin/LookupJoin; LeftJoin variants; GroupBy→HashAgg/StreamAgg; Scan→TableReader. Physical property framework: PhysProps (ordering + distribution + limit_hint), required/provided reconciliation.
  • Enforcer insertion: Sort enforcer when ordering unmet, Distribute enforcer when distribution unmet. Interesting orderings: collect orderings useful for merge join/stream agg/ORDER BY, request from children.
  • EXPLAIN (OPT): physical plan tree with per-node provided/required props + inserted enforcers.

9.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: Graefe 1995 Cascades (Implement rule kind — logical→physical, EnforceProps task), Selinger 1979 (interesting orders concept grounds OrderingChoice design), Zhou et al. 2010 SCOPE (distribution property as first-class PhysProp), ADR-A14 (PhysProps value-type design), ADR-A15 (Distribution enum design), I5 (winner satisfies required props or NoPlanFound — EnforceProps ensures completeness), I6 (OrderingChoice FD-simplified before use as winner-map key), P5 (physical properties drive plan choice), P8 (distribution is first-class).

  • quasar-props: PhysProps + derivation — satisfied_by(required:&PhysProps,provided:&PhysProps,fds:&FuncDepSet)->bool FD-simplifies both orderings before comparison (Distribution::Any satisfied by any provided). derive_provided(op:&Operator,child_props:&[PhysProps],memo:&Memo)->PhysProps dispatch: HashJoin→Distribution::Any (scrambles order), no ordering; MergeJoin→merge-key ordering (join-key ordering consumed); IndexScan→index col ordering (reverse:true flips direction); Sort enforcer→exactly its required ordering; Distribute enforcer→exactly its required distribution; HashAgg→no ordering; StreamAgg→grouping-key ordering if input does; Limit→passes through child ordering.
  • interesting_orderings(memo:&Memo,group:GroupId)->Vec<OrderingChoice>: walk consumer operators in parent groups (reverse parent-child index or search propagation), return orderings MergeJoin/StreamAgg/ORDER BY can exploit at each level. OrderingChoice::fd_simplify(fds:&FuncDepSet): drop FD-implied constants/equivalents, enables (a,b) satisfies (a,b,c) when b→c.
  • quasar-rules: implementation rules (crates/quasar-rules/src/rules/implement.opt, .opt syntax, no bodies) — ImplementHashJoin: [InnerJoin $l $r $on]→(HashJoin $l $r $on). ImplementMergeJoin: same match→(MergeJoin $l $r $on), requires join-key ordering requestable. ImplementLookupJoin: same match→(LookupJoin $l tbl idx key_cols $on), only when $r scannable group.
  • ImplementHashAgg: [GroupBy $in $gcols $aggs]→(HashAgg ...). ImplementStreamAgg: same match→(StreamAgg ...), requires input ordered by $gcols. ImplementTableReader: [Scan $tbl $cols ...]→(TableReader spans:full ...). ImplementIndexScan: [IndexScan $tbl $idx $constraint ...]→(IndexScan ...) physical.
  • ImplementLeftHashJoin: [LeftJoin ...]→(HashJoin kind:Left ...). ImplementLeftMergeJoin: same match→(MergeJoin kind:Left ...). quasar-optgen generates dispatch: per [Implement] rule, generated code called from quasar-search::apply_rule on ApplyRule task.
  • quasar-search: enforcer insertion (EnforceProps handler, crates/quasar-search/src/tasks.rs) — 1. provided=derive_provided(expr,child_provided_props,memo). 2. ordering unmet → create/intern SortEnforcer{input:group,ordering:required.ordering}, enqueue OptimizeExpr. 3. distribution unmet → create/intern Distribute{input:group,distribution:required.distribution}, enqueue OptimizeExpr. 4. enforcers interned as physical MExprs normally, provided props = exactly what they enforce. 5. guard: never insert Sort on top of Sort for same ordering (inspect MExpr.op on winner).
  • Required-props push for children (OptimizeExpr derives per child from parent required + operator semantics, passed as req field in OptimizeGroup tasks) — MergeJoin: join-key ordering on both children. StreamAgg: grouping-key ordering on input. LookupJoin: no ordering input side, right looked up by key. HashJoin: no specific ordering/distribution. Sort child: no ordering (Sort imposes its own). Limit: passes ordering req to child if exploitable (limit_hint propagation).
  • Interesting orderings integration — interesting_orderings_for_group(g:GroupId,memo:&Memo)->SmallVec<[OrderingChoice;4]> called by ExploreGroup, stored in Group.interesting_orderings (added field); OptimizeGroup iterates interesting orderings, enqueues OptimizeGroup{g,req:PhysProps{ordering,..}} per ordering plus originally-required props, pre-computes winners for orderings parents will request.
  • EXPLAIN (OPT) renderer (crates/quasar-cli/src/explain.rs) — walks winner tree from root; per physical MExpr emits operator name, provided props (ordering+distribution), parent required props, cost; enforcers rendered [Sort]/[Distribute] with ordering/distribution arg; indented text tree, machine-parseable prefix for golden diff.

9.3 Deliverable

  • quasar explain --opt "<sql>" → physical plan with props. quasar memo "<sql>" → raw memo inspection, groups show winners per PhysProps.
  • quasar opt-test conformance/testdata/enforcers/*.opt-test → enforcer trace. quasar memo --interesting "<sql>" (debug flag) → interesting orderings dump.
  • On completion: all joins produce ≥1 physical alternative; enforcer nodes appear in plans lacking required ordering/distribution; MergeJoin requests sorted children; stream-agg requests grouping-key ordering.

9.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • Implementation rule fixtures: opt-test conformance/testdata/implement/ zero diff. Enforcer insertion: ORDER BY on heap table → single Sort at root.
  • MergeJoin child ordering: both children required ordering = join key. StreamAgg ordering: child required = grouping cols; StreamAgg preferred when input ordered.
  • LookupJoin eligibility: generated only when right-side table+index in catalog. PhysProps satisfied_by FD simplify (I6): (a,b) satisfied by (a,b,c) when b→c (unit).
  • No double-Sort enforcer: Sort on already-sorted winner suppressed, test merge join + ORDER BY. EXPLAIN (OPT) renders props: provided/required for every node.
  • Latency regression: quasar bench tpch-lite plan time <150 ms p99 on 22-query suite. No memo bloat from enforcers: enforcer MExprs dedup'd via intern, member count ≤2× pre-enforcer.
  • Invariants: I3 (normalize-on-construct fires for enforcer exprs too), I6 (FD-simplify orderings), I7 (B&B prunes inferior physical alts), I10 (deterministic plan for same seed).

9.5 Conformance Fixtures

File → purpose: expected —

  • implement/inner-join-alts.opt-test → all three join alts: memo shows HashJoin, MergeJoin, LookupJoin members. implement/merge-join-ordering.opt-test → MergeJoin child required props: child groups required ordering = join key cols.
  • implement/stream-agg-ordering.opt-test → StreamAgg vs HashAgg: StreamAgg wins when input from index with grouping-key ordering. enforce/sort-enforcer-order-by.opt-test → ORDER BY on heap scan: root = Sort, child = TableReader.
  • enforce/no-double-sort.opt-test → MergeJoin + ORDER BY matching merge ordering: Sort suppressed (merge ordering satisfies). enforce/distribute-enforcer.opt-test → distribution mismatch: Distribute node between scan and join.
  • props/fd-ordering-simplify.opt-test → FD-simplify ordering: (a,b,c)→(a) when b→c and c→b, winner counts stable. implement/lookup-join-eligibility.opt-test → LookupJoin only when index present: table with no secondary index → no LookupJoin.
  • implement/left-join-variants.opt-test → LeftJoin physical alts: HashJoin(Left) and MergeJoin(Left) in group. implement/regression-sort-on-index-scan.opt-test → index scan provides ordering, no redundant Sort: Sort absent when index ordering satisfies ORDER BY.
  • Malformed/regression: implement/unknown-table-lookup.opt-test → LookupJoin on non-existent table: OptError::CatalogMissing. enforce/enforcer-budget-exceeded.opt-test → enforcer cost exceeds remaining budget: B&B prunes, plan from best-so-far.

9.6 Pitfalls

  • Ordering satisfiability ignores FDs → MergeJoin + ORDER BY → redundant Sort (satisfied_by compares raw column lists; signal: --trace-props, log pre/post FD simplify) → apply fd_simplify before comparison in satisfied_by. Double enforcer insertion → Sort→Sort chain (EnforceProps not checking existing enforcer winner; signal: quasar memo shows duplicate Sort MExprs) → guard: check Group.winners[req] before inserting.
  • LookupJoin on wrong side → generated with left as lookup side (rule pattern permissive; signal: EXPLAIN shows reversed LookupJoin) → anchor pattern: match only when $r is named-table scan group. Interesting orderings explosion → search O(n²) with column count (too many orderings/group; signal: phase-9 timing, interesting_orderings count metric) → cap 8/group, drop orderings no parent references.
  • Enforcer infinite loop → stack overflow/budget exhausted (EnforceProps pushes OptimizeGroup re-triggering EnforceProps; signal: stack depth counter, BudgetExceeded) → insert enforcer MExpr once, mark enforcer_inserted bitfield per PhysProps. StreamAgg without ordered input → wrong plan, executor panic (ordering registered but child not providing; signal: executor failure → trace provided_props) → derive_provided for StreamAgg verifies child winner actually provides ordering.
  • PhysProps HashMap collision → wrong winner (Hash/Eq not accounting for FD equivalences; signal: non-deterministic plan changes) → normalize PhysProps (FD-simplify) before insert/lookup in winners map.

9.7 Performance Concerns

  • derive_provided per OptimizeExpr, O(members×alts×orderings) → cache in MExpr, invalidate only on child winner update. Winner HashMap<PhysProps,Winner> hashing → PhysProps small (≤8 ordering cols + dist tag), FxHashMap.
  • Interesting orderings collection (reverse traversal) → compute during ExploreGroup, store in Group.interesting_orderings, one-time. Implementation rule fan-out (3 alts/join × exp join order) → B&B prunes post-Phase-10, Phase 9 limited search.
  • FD simplification per comparison → memoize OrderingChoice.simplified (lazy, once). Enforcer MExpr interning → key = (op-type,child-group,ordering/distribution), dedup O(1) amortized.

9.8 Edge Cases

  • No required ordering at root (no ORDER BY) → PhysProps{ordering:cols[],distribution:Any}, satisfied by all. Scan on table with no indexes → only TableReader, no LookupJoin/IndexScan alts, enforcer may add Sort.
  • MergeJoin on non-prefix index ordering → request may match no index, Sort enforcer on input. LookupJoin composite key + partial predicate → constraint solver partial spans, tight:false → remaining filter not nil.
  • Cascaded enforcers: Sort inside Distribute → insert inner (ordering) first, outer (distribution) second. Placeholder in join-key columns → PlaceholderIdx, rule defers if unbound → PhaseDeferred("FINAL_BLUEPRINT §25") (Deferred Features Register: same Phase 13 / §25 anchor as the plan-cache-family rows — resolved once quasar-cache substitutes concrete placeholder values).
  • GroupBy empty grouping cols (scalar aggregate) → ScalarGroupBy → scalar StreamAgg (single group), no input ordering. limit_hint through Sort → Sort with limit → partial sort (TopK), propagate limit_hint in required props.
  • FD set empty → no simplification, full ordering comparison, correct but less aggressive pruning. Enforcer on group already with winner → winner updated only if new enforcer path cheaper, not replaced blindly.

9.9 Failure Analysis

  • derive_provided wrong ordering → wrong results merge join/stream agg (detect: golden diff in CI) → fix derivation, golden update. Implementation rule not firing → query fails, no physical alt/NoPlanFound (detect: quasar memo shows only logical members) → check .opt pattern, re-run optgen.
  • Enforcer cost not computed → sub-optimal plan (cost=0 for Sort/Distribute, wrong winner) → Coster::cost handles SortEnforcer/Distribute via quasar-cost. Interesting ordering winner cache miss → high latency complex queries (detect: recomputation storm, quasar-obs task-count spike) → cap interesting orderings, reduce via FD simplification.
  • BudgetExceeded before any physical winner → query fails entirely (detect: OptError::BudgetExceeded(cost)) → caller fallback to planner, increase budget for query shape, log trigger rule. Hash/FxHashMap collision in winners → wrong plan, non-deterministic (detect: silent wrong winner, determinism check run twice same seed) → audit PhysProps Eq/Hash.

9.10 Operational Notes

Tracing/metrics (quasar-obs): quasar.phase9.impl_rule_fired{rule} (counter, implementation rule applications per rule); quasar.phase9.enforcer_inserted{type} (counter, Sort vs Distribute enforcer count); quasar.phase9.interesting_orderings{group} (histogram, interesting orderings per group); quasar.phase9.provided_props_cache_hit (counter, derive_provided memoization hits); TRACE quasar::props::reconcile (span, per-expr required/provided reconciliation, attrs satisfied/enforcer_type).

  • EXPLAIN (OPT) stability: provided/required props are golden-fixture-stable across versions; any derivation change updates conformance files + bumps a fixture-version comment.
  • Deployment: purely extends quasar-search task dispatch + quasar-rules .opt, no Memo/catalog schema change; rolling upgrade safe, optimizer stateless per query.
  • Rule versioning: .opt tracked in git, RuleMask=u128 keyed by RuleId; new rule increments count, shifts mask layout — MExpr.rules_applied is per-query-ephemeral (not persisted), no migration.
  • Regression gating: quasar bench tpch-lite plan quality (physical operator chosen) golden-diffed against stored plan tree; changes to rule priorities or PhysProps derivation trigger CI diff gate.

22. Phase 10 — Cascades Search Engine — quasar-search (B&B, tasks, winners)

10.1 Objectives

Full Cascades engine in quasar-search: task-stack driver, branch-and-bound pruning, goal memoization (winners keyed by (GroupId, PhysProps)), plan extraction. Integration phase: ties rules (P4/P8/P9), stats (P5), constraints (P6), cost (P7), PhysProps (P9) into one optimize(). Acceptance: quasar opt E2E on all conformance fixtures; B&B pruning measurable (I7); plan deterministic given seed; budget exhaustion → partial plan, no panic.

  • Task dispatch: all 6 Task variants from SPINE enum. B&B pruning: push upper-bound down, prune expr when lower-bound ≥ current best.
  • Goal memoization: Group.winners:HashMap<PhysProps,Winner>, skip re-opt when winner exists. Budget guard: Optimizer.budget:Cost, BudgetExceeded(cost) on exhaustion → best-so-far.
  • Plan extraction: walk winner chain root→leaves → physical RelExpr tree. EXPLAIN (OPT): full physical plan + per-node cost + provided props.
  • Determinism: Optimizer.seed:u64, rule/member iteration seeded (I10).

10.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: Graefe 1995 Cascades (PRIMARY — entire task-stack + B&B architecture), Graefe & McKenna 1993 Volcano (coroutine predecessor → explicit stack design justification), I7 (B&B soundness — partial_cost >= current_best prune is sound under I4 cost monotonicity), I10 (determinism — sorted iteration, ExprId tie-breaking, seeded RNG), I4 (cost monotonicity — B&B prune precondition), I9 (Memo immutability — extract() consumes Optimizer), ADR-A06 (Cascades over Volcano/System-R), ADR-A17 (determinism strategy), ADR-A18 (single-threaded per query), P6 (every search bounded — budget knobs).

  • Task stack (crates/quasar-search/src/tasks.rs) — loop while let Some(t)=self.stack.pop(){self.execute(t)}, push order = exploration priority (LIFO ≅ DFS). OptimizeGroup(g,req): winners[req] exists & cost≤upper_bound → return (goal hit); !explored → push ExploreGroup(g); each member ExprId → push OptimizeExpr(e,req); push EnforceProps(g,req) last (enforcers only if no member satisfies).
  • OptimizeExpr(e,req): lower-bound cost (quasar-cost::lower_bound, operator floor); lower_bound≥upper_bound_for(g,req) → prune (B&B); derive required props per child from operator+req; each child → push OptimizeGroup(child,child_req) with child_upper_bound; children done → full cost=Coster::cost(memo,e,req); cost<best → winners[req]=Winner{expr:e,cost,provided}.
  • ExploreGroup(g): explored→return, else each member → push ExploreExpr(e), set explored. ExploreExpr(e): each Explore rule ∉ rules_applied → push ApplyRule(e,r), mark applied.
  • ApplyRule(e,r): RuleId r∈rules_applied (RuleMask) → skip, else invoke quasar-optgen dispatch; each new expr → memo.add_expr() (normalize-on-construct fires), set r in rules_applied; intern → existing ExprId in other group → merge_groups (quasar-memo); Implement rules also enqueue OptimizeExpr(new_expr,current_req).
  • EnforceProps(g,req): any winner provided⊇req → skip; ordering unmet → insert Sort enforcer MExpr, distribution unmet → Distribute enforcer; each enforcer → push OptimizeExpr.
  • B&B upper-bound propagation — Optimizer.upper_bounds:HashMap<(GroupId,PhysProps),Cost>, updated each winner improvement; OptimizeExpr child upper bound = upper_bound − partial_cost_so_far. Per-operator lower bound (pruning floor): ScanOp→io_cost(rows*avg_row_bytes); HashJoin→cpu(left.rows+right.rows); Sort→cpu(n log n).
  • Budget guard — Optimizer.budget:Cost (total across tasks), decrement per Coster::cost; depletion → exhausted=true, stop pushing, return extract_best_plan (per group: lowest-cost winner among PhysProps satisfying req; none → OptError::NoPlanFound).
  • Winner memoization — Group.winners:FxHashMap<PhysProps,Winner>, PhysProps FD-normalized before key use; update guard new_cost<existing.cost → winners.insert(req.normalized(),Winner{..}); cross-PhysProps reuse on extract: more-general req winner (e.g. ordering:[]) satisfies specific via satisfied_by.
  • Plan extraction (crates/quasar-search/src/extract.rs) — cycle guard HashSet<GroupId> on stack (cycle = bug, memo is DAG); named algorithm extract(memo,group,req): look up winner=memo.groups[group].winners[req.normalized()] (absent → bug, memo incomplete); recursively extract each child group at its recorded required props to build children; assemble PhysicalPlan{op:winner.expr.op, children, cost:winner.cost, provided:winner.provided}.
  • EXPLAIN (OPT) (crates/quasar-cli/src/explain.rs::render_opt(&PhysicalPlan)) — DFS per-node render, e.g. HashJoin [cost=12.4 cpu=10.1 io=0.0 net=2.3] (provided: order=[] dist=Any) with indented ├─/└─ children each showing [cost=..], provided, and required props.
  • quasar-obs — span quasar::search::optimize_group (attrs group_id,req_ordering_len,member_count); counter quasar.search.tasks_executed{kind} per Task variant; histogram quasar.search.prune_ratio = pruned/considered; event per winner update {group,req,old_cost,new_cost,expr_id} → replay harness.

10.3 Deliverable

  • quasar opt "<sql>" → physical plan tree, end-to-end optimize. quasar explain --opt "<sql>" → per-node cost breakdown.
  • quasar memo "<sql>" → memo state after search, groups show winners per PhysProps. quasar opt-test conformance/testdata/opt/*.opt-test → golden runner.
  • quasar opt --budget 1000 "<sql>" → budget control. quasar opt --seed 42 "<sql>" twice → identical, determinism check.
  • quasar bench tpch-lite → benchmark, 22 queries, plan time + prune ratio.

10.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • TPC-H-lite q1..q22 → physical plans, zero NoPlanFound on default budget. B&B prune ratio ≥ 0.30 on q8/q9 (multi-join).
  • Determinism (I10): --seed 42 twice → byte-identical EXPLAIN (OPT). Plan matches exhaustive (I7): ≤5 tables, B&B winner cost ≤ exhaustive × 1.01.
  • Budget exhaustion graceful: --budget 1 → best-so-far, not panic. Winner memoization hit rate: winner_hits/total_optimize_group_calls > 0.50 on TPC-H-lite.
  • Optimize latency: TPC-H-lite p99 < 200 ms, 16-core. EXPLAIN (OPT) stable: golden diff all fixtures, zero diff after non-rule change.
  • Task count finite: stack bounded by groups × rules × PhysProps, no regression. Phase 9 fixtures green: no enforcer/implementation rule regression.
  • Invariants: I7 (B&B within budget = exhaustive), I10 (seed determinism), I3 (normalize-on-construct fires in ApplyRule path).

10.5 Conformance Fixtures

Under conformance/testdata/opt/, directive opt unless noted (file → purpose: key expected):

  • single-table-scan.opt-test → heap scan, no index: TableReader, no enforcer. index-scan-wins.opt-test → predicate on indexed col: IndexScan < TableReader.
  • hash-join-small.opt-test → two-table join, no index on join col: HashJoin. merge-join-ordered.opt-test → join on PK of both: MergeJoin, Sort enforcers absent.
  • lookup-join.opt-test → large outer, small indexed inner: LookupJoin. stream-agg.opt-test → GROUP BY indexed leading col: StreamAgg over IndexScan, no Sort.
  • hash-agg.opt-test → GROUP BY non-indexed col: HashAgg over TableReader. order-by-enforcer.opt-test → ORDER BY non-indexed col: Sort enforcer at root.
  • order-by-index.opt-test → ORDER BY indexed col: no Sort, IndexScan provides ordering. budget-exceeded.opt-test → --budget 1: partial plan, no panic.
  • determinism.opt-test → two runs same seed: identical output (runner asserts). tpch-q1.opt-test → TPC-H Q1 golden: StreamAgg over sorted scan, cost.
  • tpch-q3.opt-test → TPC-H Q3 (3-table) golden: LookupJoin/MergeJoin, cost threshold. tpch-q9.opt-test → TPC-H Q9 (6-table) B&B: prune ratio logged, within 1% of exhaustive.
  • regression-winner-reuse.opt-test (directive memo) → winner reuse across shared subexpr: subgroup opt once, 1 winner. Malformed/regression: opt/no-plan-found.opt-test (requires unimplemented physical op → NoPlanFound); opt/budget-zero.opt-test (--budget 0 → BudgetExceeded(Cost{..}) immediately).

10.6 Pitfalls

  • Task stack unbounded growth → OOM/stack overflow (Explore rules outpace B&B pruning; signal: tasks_executed plateau, heap profiler) → cap explores/group (max_explores), B&B fire early. Winner key not normalized → redundant opt, dup winners (FD-simplify not applied pre-insert/lookup; signal: dup winner entries in quasar memo) → normalize in winner_key() at all call sites.
  • Non-determinism → plans differ across runs (rule order from HashMap iteration; signal: two-run diff in CI) → BTreeMap or sort member/rule by ExprId/RuleId. Group merge missed after Explore → dup costing of cross-group equivalents (add_expr returns existing group, no merge; signal: quasar memo 2 groups same logical props) → merge_groups(g1,g2) after intern collision.
  • B&B lower-bound too loose → no pruning on complex joins (lower-bound 0.0 for unknown ops; signal: prune_ratio≈0) → per-operator lower bound, min Coster::lower_bound=scan cost. Winner updated with higher cost → plan degrades across passes (mutable winners without guard; signal: non-monotone cost in event log) → update only if new_cost<existing_cost in update_winner.
  • Extraction finds no winner → NoPlanFound on extractable query (EnforceProps not pushed, enforcer uncosted; signal: empty winners for root in quasar memo) → ensure EnforceProps pushed for root pre-extraction.

10.7 Performance Concerns

  • FxHashMap<PhysProps,Winner> per group, hot → PhysProps ≤~64B, FxHashMap inline keys, with_capacity(4). Task stack allocation → Vec<Task> pre-alloc 1024, Task ≤32B enum.
  • Coster::cost per OptimizeExpr → memoize MExpr.cost:Option<Cost>, skip on re-visit. Lower-bound recomputed → cache MExpr.lower_bound:Option<Cost>, immutable.
  • Member iteration in OptimizeGroup → SmallVec<[ExprId;4]> sequential, no HashMap. Extraction stack depth → deep trees (12+ joins) hit OS stack, iterative extraction w/ explicit stack.
  • Event log writes → RingBuffer<Event> cap 65536, drop oldest.

10.8 Edge Cases

  • Single-row result (VALUES) → trivial plan, winner on first expr, zero pruning (expected). Correlated subquery child → ScalarExpr::Subquery(GroupId), opt with empty req, cost added to parent.
  • Cross-group equivalence after Explore → same canonical form, intern returns same GroupId, dedup. Budget exhausted mid-join-tree → some groups have winners → best-so-far, may emit hybrid plan.
  • Placeholder unbound at optimize time → ScalarExpr::Placeholder(idx) no binding → OptError::PlaceholderUnbound, deferred to Phase 13. Cyclic rules (A→B→A) → MExpr.rules_applied:RuleMask prevents re-applying same rule to same expr.
  • Winner ordering:[],dist:Any satisfies specific req → cross-req reuse: satisfied_by on extract avoids re-opt. Memo root changes after Normalize → Memo.root updated by add_expr if root group merges, extract from new root.
  • All explore rules disabled (test) → --no-explore: normalization + implementation only, faster unit tests. GroupState::Explored flag → single-threaded stack, no race, set atomically in single thread.

10.9 Failure Analysis

  • NoPlanFound on valid query → entire query errors, no plan (detect: emitted w/ group+req in quasar-cli) → audit implementation rule coverage, add missing [Implement]. BudgetExceeded, no partial winner → query fails, no fallback plan (detect: return error + best-cost-seen hint) → raise budget default, budget warning metric.
  • Non-termination (rare) → query hangs, optimize never returns (detect: timeout wrapper in quasar-cli ~10s wall) → event-log replay finds cycling rule pair. Wrong plan (suboptimal, correct) → correct results, degraded quality, silent (detect: TPC-H golden diff in CI) → fix B&B upper-bound prop/lower-bound floor.
  • Panic in extraction (missing winner) → single query, panic + backtrace (detect: catch_unwind in CLI) → NoPlanFound, file bug, add fixture. Cost underflow (negative) → wrong operator selected (total<0 wins spuriously) → debug_assert!(cost.total>=0.0) in update_winner, fix Coster for operator.

10.10 Operational Notes

Observability (quasar-obs): quasar.search.tasks_executed (counter, task_kind); quasar.search.prune_ratio (histogram, per-query); quasar.search.winner_hits (counter, group_id bucket); quasar.search.budget_remaining (gauge, at query end); quasar.search.optimize_duration_ms (histogram, p50/p99); TRACE quasar::search::optimize_group (span: group_id,req,member_count,winner_cost); EVENT winner_update (event log: {ts,group,req_hash,old_cost,new_cost,expr_id}).

  • Deterministic replay: quasar obs replay <event-log> re-runs optimizer with same rule firing order, verifies same winner sequence; postmortem for plan regressions.
  • Deployment: stateless per query (Optimizer per optimize()), no persistent state except plan cache (Phase 13), hot-reload safe; budget default via --budget or QUASAR_OPTIMIZER_BUDGET.
  • Tuning: QUASAR_OPTIMIZER_BUDGET (5000) raise for analytical, lower for OLTP; QUASAR_MAX_EXPLORES_PER_GROUP (64) caps exploration fan-out, reduce if slow; QUASAR_SEED (0) fix for deterministic test plans, vary in prod to avoid systematic bias; quasar bench tpch-lite is the regression gate after any rule/cost-model change.

23. Phase 11 — Distributed Cost + Multi-Region — locality, distribution, LOS

11.1 Objectives

Distribution-aware cost + search: net transfer cost differentiates local / cross-node / cross-region; Distribution property drives plan shape; LOS exploits gateway region for LIMIT queries; multi-region zone configs feed placement. Acceptance: locality fixtures pass; LOS fixture shows local-first scan; lookup-vs-hash crossover golden matches expected crossover row count.

  • Net cost tiers: local ≈ 0; cross-node-same-region small; cross-region dominant. Distribution property: Single(RegionId)/Set(...)/Any, derived from leaseholder localities of scanned ranges.
  • Distribute enforcer cost: bytes * region_distance_factor (from zone config). Distributed join planning: LookupJoin > HashJoin for remote indexed right; HashJoin needs co-location via Distribute.
  • LOS: LIMIT + globally distributed table → scan gateway region first, fan out only if under-filled. Multi-region fixtures: same SQL, different zone config → different plan; EXPLAIN shows distribution nodes.

11.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: Zhou et al. 2010 SCOPE (distribution property as first-class PhysProp — net-cost component), CockroachDB DistSQL RFC (leaseholder-based placement, LOS algorithm), Spanner 2012 (range-leaseholder model — quasar-cat RangeMeta origin), ADR-A15 (Distribution enum — Any/Single/Set), ADR-A08 (Cost{net} component for cross-region traffic), I12 (root winner distribution matches required — EnforceProps inserts Distribute enforcer), P8 (distribution as first-class property), Designing Data-Intensive Applications Ch. 6 (partitioning model grounding quasar-cat zone configs).

  • quasar-cat: zone configs + leaseholder metadata — Table += zone_config:ZoneConfig, ranges:Vec<RangeMeta>; ZoneConfig{constraints:Vec<RegionConstraint>,lease_preferences:Vec<RegionId>,num_replicas:u8}; RangeMeta{id:RangeId,leaseholder:NodeId,region:RegionId,span:Span}; RegionPair{a,b}→region_distance_matrix:HashMap<RegionPair,f64> (from zone latency annotations or static config); Catalog::leaseholder_region(table,&Span)->RegionId = majority leaseholder region for span.
  • quasar-props: Distribution derivation (derive_distribution(&Operator,&[Distribution],&Catalog)->Distribution) — TableReader{spans}: leaseholder_region per span, all same→Single(r) else Set(regions). IndexScan{constraint}: as TableReader on constraint spans. HashJoin post-Distribute both sides: Single(gateway) if both same region. LookupJoin: = left (input) distribution, right = remote round-trips not shipped. Distribute enforcer: exactly required.distribution. Sort enforcer/Limit: passthrough child. HashAgg/StreamAgg: = input (no redistribution). Distribution::gateway(&Catalog)->RegionId = query-issuing node region, in Optimizer.gateway_region.
  • quasar-cost: net cost (extend Cost) — child same node as parent: 0. Child same region, diff node: bytes*INTRA_REGION_COST (~0.01). Child different region: bytes*region_distance_factor(child_r,parent_r). Distribute (by-hash): bytes*region_distance_factor(src,dst), bytes=rows*avg_row_size. LookupJoin (remote index): probe_count*avg_row_size*region_distance_factor(left_r,right_r).
  • Coster in quasar-cost::distributed needs Catalog for distance matrix → CostContext<'a>{cat:&'a Catalog,gateway:RegionId,stats:&'a Statistics} threaded into Coster::cost. HashJoin distributed: Distribute on both children (by join-key hash), net=2*table_bytes*region_distance_factor. LookupJoin distributed: net=left_rows*(avg_lookup_latency+right_avg_row_size*region_distance). Crossover (lookup vs hash): LookupJoin wins when left_rows < bytes(right_table)/(avg_lookup_net+cpu_per_row).
  • quasar-search: distribution integration — Optimizer.gateway_region set at optimize() entry from catalog/caller; root req PhysProps{distribution:Single(gateway_region),ordering:<ORDER BY or []>}; EnforceProps distribution unmet → insert Distribute{input:group,distribution:required.distribution}, cost via distributed Coster (eligibility: only if best winner distribution ≠ required, skip if Any satisfies); distribution-aware costing may flip Phase-10 local-only winners → re-run OptimizeGroup for affected groups.
  • LOS (quasar-search::los) — 1. trigger: root has Limit AND scanned table ranges span multiple regions. 2. partition ranges by region, push gateway-region-only scan first (limit_hint=N). 3. gateway scan ≥ N rows → done, no cross-region fan-out; under-filled → fan out remaining regions, union, apply limit. 4. plan = UnionAll(Limit(scan gateway-spans),Limit(scan other-spans,remaining_n)) — no new RelExpr, reuses locked UnionAll/Limit/Scan. 5. emit both LOS UnionAll plan and full distributed scan into memo, B&B picks cheaper. try_locality_optimized_search(g:GroupId,req:&PhysProps,&mut Optimizer)->Option<ExprId> called from OptimizeGroup when limit_hint>0 AND multi-region detected.
  • quasar-rules: distributed implementation rules (implement.opt) — ImplementLookupJoinDistributed: [InnerJoin $l $r $on] when right remote+indexed → (LookupJoin ...) + net cost annotation. ImplementHashJoinColocated: same match when both same region or Distribute equalizes → (HashJoin) + (Distribute by-hash) both inputs. ImplementLocalScan: [Scan $t $cols] when gateway leaseholder → (TableReader spans:local), lower net. GenerateLocalityOptimizedScan: [Limit (Scan $t ..) $n] when multi-region table → (UnionAll (Limit (Scan $t gateway-spans) $n) (Limit (Scan $t other-spans) $n)), gateway-first, no new operator.
  • EXPLAIN (OPT) — each node shows dist=Single(us-east1)/dist=Set(us-east1,eu-west1); Distribute nodes as [Distribute by-hash(col1,col2) → Single(us-east1)].

11.3 Deliverable

  • quasar explain --opt "<sql>" → distribution-aware plan, dist annotation per node. quasar opt-test conformance/testdata/dist/ → locality fixture.
  • quasar opt-test --zone-config configs/multi-region.toml "<sql>" → multi-region catalog stub. quasar explain --opt "SELECT * FROM t LIMIT 10" on multi-region → LOS plan.
  • quasar bench dist-crossover → cost crossover, lookup vs hash crossover row count.

11.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • Same SQL, diff zone config → diff plan (single-region → no Distribute; multi-region → Distribute or LookupJoin). LOS fixture: LIMIT 10 multi-region → UnionAll(local-scan,remote-scan).
  • Lookup vs hash crossover: ≤1000 left rows → LookupJoin, ≥100k → HashJoin. Net cost non-zero cross-region: EXPLAIN shows net > 0 for cross-region joins.
  • Gateway-local preferred: single-region table → no Distribute inserted. Latency regression: quasar bench dist-suite p99 < 300 ms (incl. leaseholder lookup).
  • Phase 10 fixtures green, no regression. Distribution derivation units: multi-region spans → Set, single-region → Single.
  • B&B prunes distributed alts: prune_ratio on distributed ≥ 0.25. Enforcer cost in output: Distribute node shows net in EXPLAIN (OPT).
  • Invariants: I5 (net cost dominates distributed plan ranking), I7 (B&B picks lowest incl. net), I10 (LOS decision seed-independent, row-count-driven).

11.5 Conformance Fixtures

Under conformance/testdata/dist/ (file → purpose: key expected):

  • single-region-no-distribute.opt-test → all ranges one region: no Distribute, dist=Single(r1) throughout. multi-region-hash-join.opt-test → join across two regions: Distribute by-hash both inputs, HashJoin.
  • lookup-join-remote-index.opt-test → small left, remote indexed right: LookupJoin over HashJoin. lookup-join-large-left.opt-test → large left (>100k), remote right: HashJoin + Distribute over LookupJoin.
  • los-limit-local-first.opt-test → LIMIT 10 multi-region: UnionAll(Limit(gateway scan),Limit(remote scan)), gateway first. los-under-filled-fanout.opt-test → local 5 rows, need 10: UnionAll(local,remote), remote fetches 5 more.
  • gateway-scan-prefers-local.opt-test → mixed leaseholders, gateway=us-east1: us-east1 spans → local, others → remote. crossover-lookup-vs-hash.opt-test → parameterized by left_rows: 500 → LookupJoin, 200k → HashJoin.
  • distribute-enforcer-cost.opt-test → Sort + Distribute at root: both enforcers visible, net > 0. multi-region-agg.opt-test → GROUP BY multi-region: Distribute-to-gateway + HashAgg at gateway.
  • regression-no-net-on-local.opt-test → single-region scan net = 0: net=0.0 in EXPLAIN. malformed-zone-config.opt-test → invalid zone config: OptError::CatalogMissing or fallback dist=Any.

11.6 Pitfalls

  • Region distance unconfigured → cross-region net = 0, wrong plan (region_distance_matrix missing pair; signal: EXPLAIN net=0 on cross-region join) → sentinel (~1000.0) for unknown pairs, log warning. LOS infinite fan-out → UnionAll over all regions despite limit met (row-count tracking not applied; signal: all regions in UnionAll children) → track running count, stop once limit reached.
  • Distribution ignores index scans → IndexScan → Any not Single(r) (missing case in derive_distribution; signal: wrong distribution on multi-region index scan) → add IndexScan span→region lookup. Spurious Distribute enforcer → local-only query has Distribute node (satisfied_by not recognizing Any⊨Single(gateway); signal: Distribute with zero transfer bytes) → fix satisfied_by: Any⊨Single(r) only if data already at r.
  • Gateway region not set → all plans route to region 0 (gateway_region defaults to 0, caller of Optimizer::new omits gateway) → assert non-zero in Optimizer::new, fail fast. LookupJoin N round trips for large N → high net cost uncaptured (per-row lookup not × left_rows; signal: LookupJoin wins for large tables) → net=left_rows*per_lookup_net_cost in Coster.
  • Multi-col hash Distribute key mismatch → co-location fails, wrong partitioning (Distribute hashes ≠ join key cols; signal: wrong join results in executor) → ImplementHashJoinColocated matches Distribute cols to join cols exactly.

11.7 Performance Concerns

  • leaseholder_region per span per OptimizeExpr → cache CostContext.span_region_cache:HashMap<RangeId,RegionId> per optimize(). Distance matrix HashMap per cost call → pre-flatten Vec<f64> indexed [a*num_regions+b].
  • LOS adds MExprs mid-search → may re-explore, call LOS once per group (Group flag). Distribute interning under multi-region → many (distribution,child_group) pairs, bounded by num_groups*num_regions.
  • CostContext borrowing → pass &CostContext not Arc<Catalog>, avoid ref-count overhead. Crossover computation → precompute per (table_pair,join_col_ndv) at rule application, store LookupJoinOp.crossover_hint.

11.8 Edge Cases

  • All ranges in gateway region → Single(gateway), no Distribute, LOS irrelevant. LIMIT 0 → LOS short-circuits to empty plan, no fan-out.
  • Zone config, no lease prefs → leaseholder unknown → Any, Distribute if Single required. Self-join on distributed table → both sides same distribution, same region → co-located, no Distribute.
  • LookupJoin on partitioned index → right-side spans may cross regions, per-row net w/ variable distance. Multi-region + ORDER BY + LIMIT → LOS + Sort enforcer; LOS rows arbitrary order, Sort at root after UnionAll.
  • Set subset matching required Single → satisfied_by false, Distribute inserted even for subset. Placeholder in join key (distributed hash) → unbound → defer distribution decision to Phase 13 cache, PhaseDeferred("FINAL_BLUEPRINT §25") (same Deferred Features Register anchor as above).
  • Region removed mid-query → catalog stale, leaseholder_region → OptError::CatalogMissing, query fails. LOS fan-out to 10+ regions → cap QUASAR_LOS_MAX_REGIONS (5), beyond → full distributed scan.

11.9 Failure Analysis

  • Wrong distribution → executor wrong partitioning, single query, correct on retry (detect: executor panic/wrong results, golden diff, executor error logged) → fix derive_distribution, add fixture, verify crossover test. CatalogMissing on leaseholder lookup → query fails (detect: OptError::CatalogMissing) → return error, no partial execute; retry after catalog refresh, stale-catalog metric.
  • LOS duplicate rows → wrong result count (local + remote scan overlap, UnionAll not deduplicating) → LOS: fetch local (limit N) then full → stop, no overlap. Distribute over-costed → never selected, HashJoin always loses to LookupJoin multi-region (region distance constant too high; signal: LookupJoin at 1M rows, wrong) → calibrate INTRA_REGION_COST + cross-region factors vs real latency.
  • Non-determinism in LOS fan-out order → UnionAll child order differs across runs (region iteration from HashMap; signal: EXPLAIN (OPT) diffs in CI) → sort regions by RegionId before building UnionAll children. Phase 10 B&B prune removes valid distributed alt → NoPlanFound on multi-region (upper-bound set too low, budget → 0 before distribution-aware pass) → raise budget for distributed path, --dist flag disables aggressive pruning.

11.10 Operational Notes

Observability (quasar-obs): quasar.dist.distribute_enforcer_count (counter, Distribute enforcers per query); quasar.dist.los_triggered (counter, LOS path activated); quasar.dist.los_local_sufficient (counter, LOS local filled limit, no fan-out); quasar.dist.lookup_vs_hash_decision{winner} (counter, lookup/hash per join); quasar.dist.net_cost_total (histogram, total net cost component per query); quasar.dist.region_distance_lookup_us (histogram, distance matrix lookup latency); TRACE quasar::dist::los (span: gateway_region,local_row_estimate,fan_out_regions); EVENT distribute_enforcer_inserted (event log: {group,distribution,net_cost_estimate}).

  • Zone config loading: loaded from quasar-cat at optimizer construction, cached for query lifetime, staleness via catalog version field, no live reload mid-query.
  • Multi-region test harness: quasar opt-test --zone-config <file> — TOML overriding catalog defaults, used by all conformance/testdata/dist/; format: [[region]] blocks with id, nodes, latency_ms_to_other_regions.
  • Deployment: additive to Phase 10, no Memo schema change; Any default for unknown tables → safe rollback (ignores distribution costs, no enforcer); region_distance_matrix loaded at startup, missing entries log warning + sentinel cost.
  • Calibration: QUASAR_INTRA_REGION_COST (0.01, bytes multiplier same-region cross-node); QUASAR_CROSS_REGION_BASE_COST (10.0, bytes multiplier when distance matrix has no entry); QUASAR_LOS_MAX_REGIONS (5, max fan-out regions, beyond → full distributed scan); QUASAR_LOOKUP_PROBE_NET_COST (0.001/row/unit-distance, per-lookup net for crossover).

24. Phase 12 — DistSQL Physical Planning — quasar-distsql (flows, processors, routers)

12.1 Objectives

Lower the physical plan from quasar-search (tree of physical MExprs with winners) into a FlowSpec of ProcessorSpec nodes, Stream edges, Router annotations placed across cluster nodes. Placement: TableReader → leaseholder node per span; join/agg procs → gateway unless inputs colocated; local collapse when estimated output rows < quasar_local_plan_rows_threshold (1000) AND all leaseholders same node → single-node FlowSpec. quasar explain --distsql renders flow diagram: node boxes, processor rectangles, stream arrows labeled with router kind.
