Acceptance: dist goldens verify processor count, node placement, router types; ordered fan-in fixture asserts output ordering preserved; threshold fixture shows single-node FlowSpec at boundary.

  • IndexScan/Scan → TableReader{spans,index}, one per contiguous span group, on leaseholder node. HashJoin → HashJoiner: probe ByHash(join_key_cols), build Mirror if small else ByHash.
  • MergeJoin → MergeJoiner, both inputs PassThrough ordered, fan-in merge preserves ordering. GroupBy (Hash) → two-stage: local partial Aggregator per reader node → ByHash(grouping_cols) → single final Aggregator.
  • GroupBy (Stream) → Aggregator on ordered stream, no repartition if distribution already satisfies grouping. Sort → Sorter per node + ordered-merge Sorter at fan-in (PassThrough streams).
  • Limit → Limit after fan-in merge, push soft_limit (LimitHint) to each TableReader. Distribute enforcer → ByHash/ByRange/Mirror routers between source and dest procs.

12.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: CockroachDB DistSQL RFC (ProcessorSpec/Stream/Router model, FlowSpec format — PRIMARY reference), Spanner 2012 (leaseholder locality for scan placement), ADR-A16 (Rust-native FlowSpec, no protobuf at internal boundary), ADR-A15 (Distribution enum drives Router selection: ByHash/ByRange/Mirror/PassThrough), I12 (root FlowSpec distribution matches required — final assert in plan()), I5 (required physical props satisfied — DistSQL ensures ordered fan-in respects MergeJoin ordering), P12 (no execution coupling — FlowSpec is pure output).

  • Crate scaffold (quasar-distsql) — flow_planner.rs: FlowPlanner, fn plan(memo:&Memo,winner:ExprId,catalog:&dyn Catalog,required:&PhysProps)->Result<FlowSpec,OptError>; walks physical MExpr tree top-down, per-node dispatch to per-operator planner; tracks NodeId->Vec<ProcessorSpec> accumulation, assigns monotone ProcId.
  • Span partitioning (spans → nodes) — SpanPartitioner: Constraint spans + catalog.range_leaseholders(table,spans)→Vec<(NodeId,Spans)>; group adjacent spans on same leaseholder → one TableReader per group; wire TableReader outputs via PassThrough if single node, else fan-in.
  • Router insertion — insert_router(source_procs,router_kind,dest_proc) → intermediate Router wiring in FlowSpec.streams. ByHash: dest=murmur3(tuple_key_cols)%num_dest_procs. Mirror: replicate each row to all dest procs (broadcast). ByRange: range metadata directs rows to owning node.
  • Ordered stream merge — fan-in from N sorted TableReaders into MergeJoiner/final Sorter → insert Sorter{ordering} at gateway, N PassThrough inputs, StreamKind::Ordered; invariant: required.ordering non-empty + source provides it → merge sorter declares identical ordering.
  • Local-vs-distributed — fn is_local(stats:&Statistics,leaseholders:&[NodeId])->bool = single distinct node AND row_count < threshold; local: all procs on gateway, all streams PassThrough, no routers; threshold via quasar_local_plan_rows_threshold env/config.
  • PostProcessSpec — each ProcessorSpec.post carries projection (output col subset), filter (scalar remaining after constraint extraction), rendering (col names for EXPLAIN).
  • EXPLAIN (DISTSQL) — distsql_render.rs walks FlowSpec, groups procs by NodeId, emits ASCII box diagram (node box = address + proc IDs; streams = arrows w/ router label); registered as quasar explain --distsql "<sql>" in quasar-cli.
  • quasar-obs — span distsql::plan wrapping FlowPlanner::plan; events processor_placed{proc_id,node_id,core_kind}, router_inserted{kind,fan_in,fan_out}, local_plan_chosen{estimated_rows}; metrics quasar_distsql_processors_total, quasar_distsql_streams_total, quasar_distsql_local_plans_total.
  • Test harness — opt-test dist directive runs FlowPlanner::plan on memo winner, serializes FlowSpec deterministically (sorted by proc_id), diffs golden; unit tests: span partitioner (split/colocated merge), router insertion, local threshold boundary.

12.3 Deliverable

  • quasar explain --distsql "<sql>" → ASCII flow: node boxes, processors, stream arrows, router labels. quasar opt-test conformance/testdata/distsql/ → all dist goldens pass.
  • quasar explain --opt "<sql>" → unchanged from Phase 10, physical plan tree. FlowSpec serializable to JSON for exec engine (project 318); quasar-distsql exposes pub fn plan_flow(...); gateway-local FlowSpec degrades correctly on single-node cluster.

12.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • dist goldens 100% pass (placement, router types, stream count). Ordered fan-in preserves ordering: output ordering col list == required.ordering.
  • Local-vs-distributed threshold: single-node ≤ threshold, multi-node above, fixture at boundary ±1 row. TableReader placement: each on correct leaseholder node per catalog.
  • HashJoiner symmetry: ByHash both sides same hash cols, probe/build assignment stable. Two-stage aggregation: local partial then final, golden shows two Aggregator procs.
  • MergeJoiner ordering: both inputs StreamKind::Ordered matching ordering, golden asserts. FlowSpec serialization determinism: byte-identical across two plan calls (I-DET).
  • Conformance suite: Phases 0–12 golden suites green. Planning latency: FlowPlanner::plan < 5 ms on TPC-H Q5 (10-table star) winner.
  • References: §4 I-DET, §7 I-PHYS, §7 I-COST.

12.5 Conformance Fixtures

Under conformance/testdata/distsql/ (file → purpose: key assertions):

  • basic_scan.txt → SELECT * FROM t WHERE a>1, two ranges two nodes: two TableReader on two nodes, PassThrough fan-in. hash_join.txt → SELECT * FROM t JOIN s ON t.k=s.k (large): ByHash(k) both inputs, HashJoiner on gateway.
  • merge_join.txt → ordered merge join (both sorted on join key): MergeJoiner, StreamKind::Ordered both, merge sorter. agg_two_stage.txt → SELECT region,count(*) FROM t GROUP BY region: local partial Aggregator per node, ByHash(region) → final Aggregator.
  • local_plan.txt → small table (< threshold), single-node: single-node FlowSpec, all procs on node 1, PassThrough only. limit_pushdown.txt → SELECT * FROM t LIMIT 10: Limit at gateway, soft_limit=10 on each TableReader.
  • broadcast_small.txt → hash join, build side < broadcast threshold: Mirror build side, ByHash probe side. multiregion_place.txt → three-region cluster, EU/US/AP ranges: procs on leaseholder nodes per region, net cost comment.
  • ordered_fanin.txt → Sort + fan-in from 4 reader nodes: Sorter at gateway, four PassThrough ordered streams, ordering matches. regression_router_nil.txt → zero-width join key (degenerate cross join): Mirror chosen, no panic.
  • Malformed/regression: missing leaseholder metadata (OptError::CatalogMissing); zero-span constraint (empty FlowSpec, ConstraintUnsat); fan-in conflicting orderings (planner emits Sorter to re-sort, asserted in golden).

12.6 Pitfalls

  • Leaseholder staleness → TableReader on offline node, exec fails (catalog cache not refreshed pre-plan; signal: processor_placed{node_id} vs live leaseholder map) → invalidate cache on CatalogMissing, retry plan. Hash col mismatch router vs joiner → HashJoiner misses matching rows, wrong results (ByHash col ordering ≠ HashJoiner eq cols; signal: diff ByHash(cols) vs HashJoiner.eq_cols in dump) → assert equality in planner, golden catches.
  • Ordered-merge without ordering guarantee → MergeJoiner unsorted output, missing downstream Sort (winner lacks ordering in provided PhysProps; signal: StreamKind::Ordered set per input vs required.ordering) → assert merge inputs carry StreamKind::Ordered in FlowPlanner. Threshold miscalibration → always-local, all plans single-node even for large tables (quasar_local_plan_rows_threshold too high; signal: quasar_distsql_local_plans_total spikes) → lower threshold, fixture at 1M-row asserting multi-node.
  • Two-stage agg omitted for stream-agg → no local partial, all rows to gateway, hot node (treated GroupBy stream-agg w/o ordering check on distributed input; signal: router_inserted missing, single Aggregator) → if input not ordered on grouping cols, force hash-agg two-stage. Mirror broadcast on large table → OOM/network amplification (build cardinality estimate wrong, stale stats; signal: Mirror-label streams, large net cost) → cap broadcast at row-count threshold, recheck stats freshness.
  • ProcId collision on retry → two procs share ProcId, routing fails (non-monotone ProcId on replanning; signal: duplicate proc_id in FlowSpec JSON) → atomic incrementing counter per FlowSpec.

12.7 Performance Concerns

  • Span partitioner catalog call per table → batch range_leaseholders RPC, cache for plan() duration. FlowSpec realloc for prepared stmts → cache alongside memo in quasar-cache, invalidate on leaseholder change.
  • ASCII rendering in hot path → lazy, only with --distsql, skip in opt-test unless dist directive. Stream list growth, wide fan-outs → SmallVec<[Stream;8]> proc output, heap only when fan-out > 8.
  • Placement sort (determinism) → sort by (node_id,proc_id) once at end, O(n log n), n < 1000. Golden serialization diff → stable topological order (DFS from root proc), avoid HashMap iteration order.

12.8 Edge Cases

  • All ranges single node → local FlowSpec, no routers, PassThrough only. Zero-row estimated input to HashJoin → HashJoiner still emitted (correctness), may log low_row_count_join.
  • Scan with empty Constraint (full scan) → single TableReader, unbounded span, first leaseholder. Join on zero columns (cross join) → Mirror on smaller side, HashJoiner empty eq_cols (Cartesian).
  • Limit 0 → Limit{n:0} emits no rows, soft_limit=0 on each TableReader. Schema change between plan and exec → CatalogMissing/range mismatch, exec surfaces error, planner unguarded (Phase 13 cache handles invalidation).
  • Locality skew: all leaseholders one node → multi-proc FlowSpec, procs colocated, PassThrough, no network cost. Window function operators → ProcessorCore::Noop + post-process expr, single-node (window not distributed this phase).
  • Subquery-as-processor → WithScan/Values proc → Noop, wiring follows correlated-scan pattern.

12.9 Failure Analysis

  • FlowPlanner::plan → OptError::CatalogMissing → single query, no memo corruption (detect: error to CLI, --distsql exits non-zero) → return error, no partial FlowSpec cached; refresh catalog, retry. Infinite loop in span partitioner → single query (detect: budget check → BudgetExceeded) → budget checked per iteration; raise budget or fix span degenerate loop.
  • Panic in ASCII renderer → loses CLI output (detect: process crash) → catch_unwind in CLI render path; fix bug, add regression golden. Incorrect ByHash col set → wrong node, all queries using this join shape (detect: silent wrong-result at exec) → dist golden diff at CI; fix hash-col derivation, add golden.
  • Placement to dead node → single query exec, exec connection refused → event processor_placed_dead_node, exec retries fresh leaseholder; catalog TTL refresh, plan retry. FlowSpec serialization non-determinism → CI reliability (detect: golden diff flakes in CI) → sort all collections before serialize; fix sort, I-DET enforced.

12.10 Operational Notes

  • Tracing: distsql::plan span {table_count,proc_count,stream_count,is_local}, child spans per operator (distsql::lower_hash_join, distsql::lower_agg, …).
  • Metrics: quasar_distsql_processors_total{core_kind} (histogram of procs per FlowSpec); quasar_distsql_local_plans_total (counter, alert if ratio drops); quasar_distsql_plan_duration_ms (histogram, P99 < 5 ms); quasar_distsql_router_insertions_total{kind} (PassThrough/ByHash/Mirror/ByRange breakdown).
  • Event log (quasar-obs): records FlowSpec summary (proc count, node set, local flag) per query; replay harness (Phase 13) re-runs planner and diffs FlowSpec.
  • Deployment: library crate, no binary; FlowSpec protobuf/JSON is compatibility surface with exec engine (project 318), field additions backward-compatible, removals require version gate.
  • Rollback: QUASAR_FORCE_LOCAL_PLAN=1 forces is_local always true → single-node execution if FlowPlanner broken in a release.
  • Compatibility: no memo wire format or cost model change; Phase 0–11 goldens unmodified, dist goldens additive.

25. Phase 13 — Plan Cache + Placeholders + Observability + Conformance + Hardening

13.1 Objectives

Production hardening across four subsystems.

  • quasar-cache — prepared-stmt lifecycle prepare(sql)->StmtKey, fingerprint = hash of bound IR structure (column IDs, operator shapes, placeholder positions/types); placeholder fast-path PlaceholderIdx(u16) in ScalarExpr::Placeholder, substitution swaps Datum in scalars without rebuilding memo; generic plan = optimize placeholders as unknown-but-typed (worst-case cardinality), cache physical plan + FlowSpec; custom plan = re-optimize with Datum bound, use if custom_cost < generic_cost*CUSTOM_PLAN_THRESHOLD else generic; staleness = fingerprint includes (schema_version,stats_version) per table, version bump → evict.
  • quasar-obs — per-task tracing spans (OptimizeGroup, OptimizeExpr, ExploreGroup, ExploreExpr, ApplyRule, EnforceProps); structured event log JSON-L {event,rule_id,group_id,expr_id,cost,timestamp}; metrics (rules fired by kind/name, memo groups/members, search time, prune count, cache hit/miss); deterministic replay = record (sql,catalog_snapshot,stats_snapshot,seed), replay re-runs Optimizer::optimize, diff physical plan tree + FlowSpec, byte-identical required (I-DET).
  • Conformance — quasar opt-test all directives (norm,explore,opt,memo,stats,index-constraints,dist,placeholder) across full conformance/testdata/; TPC-H Q1–Q22-lite + TPC-DS-lite plan goldens exact match; plan-quality gate per-query cost.total ≤ stored budget.
  • Fuzzing + Soak — structure-aware SQL fuzzer (random valid SQL → no panic, memo invariants I-MEMO members dedup / I-COST cost monotone, clean OptError); proptest idempotence norm(norm(e))==norm(e); proptest confluence (different rule orders → same normal form); 1-hour soak (random workload, zero panics, no leak trend).

13.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Anchors: CockroachDB optimizer RFC (generic-vs-custom plan invalidation via epoch counters), ADR-A19 (plan cache + placeholder design decision), ADR-A21 (tracing crate — quasar-obs uses tracing::Subscriber), ADR-A22 (metrics facade — quasar-obs wires Prometheus exporter), ADR-A23 (golden/conformance test runner wired here), I10 (determinism — replay harness must produce byte-identical plan), I8 (generic plan valid across placeholder bindings — proptest in quasar-cache), P10 (observability and replayability by default), GPORCA (standalone optimizer library model informs quasar-obs event log design).

  • quasar-cache types + API — CacheKey{ir_fingerprint:u64,schema_version:u64,stats_version:u64}, fingerprint = FNV-1a over bound IR skeleton (operator kinds + column IDs, placeholder positions). PlanCache: HashMap<CacheKey,CachedPlan> behind RwLock; CachedPlan={generic:Option<CachedEntry>,custom:HashMap<PlaceholderValues,CachedEntry>}; CachedEntry{physical_expr:ExprId,flow_spec:FlowSpec,cost:Cost,inserted_at:Instant}. lookup(key,placeholder_values) custom-first-then-generic; insert_generic/insert_custom. Staleness: catalog.schema_version(table) change → invalidate_by_table(table_id) scans keys via secondary HashMap<TableId,Vec<CacheKey>>. should_use_custom(generic,custom)=custom<generic*CUSTOM_PLAN_THRESHOLD (default 0.5, configurable). substitute_placeholders(memo,scalar_id,&[Datum])->ScalarId: walk ScalarExpr, Placeholder(idx)→Const(values[idx]), intern, no RelExpr rebuild.
  • quasar-obs — OptimizerTracer wraps tracing::Span (task_start(&Task)/task_end(&Task,Outcome)); EventLog append-only Vec<OptimizerEvent> per run, JSON-L via serde_json (struct OptimizerEvent{ts_ns:u64,kind:EventKind,rule_id:Option<RuleId>,group_id:Option<GroupId>,expr_id:Option<ExprId>,cost:Option<Cost>}; enum EventKind{TaskPush,TaskPop,RuleFired,RuleSkipped,WinnerUpdated,GroupPruned,BudgetExceeded}); ReplayHarness record(...)->ReplayRecord/replay(record)->PhysicalPlan/diff(a,b)->DiffResult; metrics via metrics crate: counter!("quasar.rules.fired","kind"=>k), histogram!("quasar.search.duration_ms"), gauge!("quasar.memo.groups"), gauge!("quasar.memo.members"), counter!("quasar.prune.count"), counter!("quasar.cache.hits"), counter!("quasar.cache.misses").
  • Conformance runner (opt-test) — reads line-by-line, accumulates SQL block, on ---- directive runs subsystem and captures output; diff output != golden → unified diff, exit 1. Directives → subsystem → output: norm→quasar-rules normalize pass→memo after normalization text; explore→quasar-search explore phase only→memo after exploration; opt→full optimize→physical plan tree text; memo→memo dump→group list+members+winners; stats→quasar-stats cardinality→per-group statistics text; index-constraints→quasar-constraint span solver→spans+remaining filter text; dist→quasar-distsql flow planner→FlowSpec text diagram; placeholder→cache+substitution→generic+custom plan side-by-side.
  • Fuzzing (fuzz/, cargo fuzz) — fuzz_sql_optimize: random &str SQL → full optimize, no panic, Ok or OptError. fuzz_rule_application: random MExpr + rule order → memo invariants (dedup, no dangling GroupId). fuzz_cost_monotone: random physical tree → parent.cost >= sum(children.cost). proptest: norm_idempotent(expr in arb_scalar_expr()) → assert_eq!(normalize(e),normalize(normalize(e))).
  • Soak — benches/soak.rs (criterion disabled, integration test --ignored): loop 1 hour, random corpus SQL, track peak RSS (/proc/self/status or sysinfo), assert no growth > 10 MB after warmup, zero panics.

13.3 Deliverable

  • quasar opt-test conformance/testdata/ → full conformance across all directives, exit 0 on pass. quasar opt "<sql>" (cache) → cache hit on repeat, [cache:generic]/[cache:custom] annotation.
  • quasar explain --opt "<sql>" → unchanged, physical plan tree with costs. quasar bench tpch → TPC-H Q1–Q22-lite plan + cost report, cost regression gate.
  • quasar bench tpcds → TPC-DS-lite plan + cost report. Event log: JSON-L to stderr or QUASAR_EVENT_LOG=path per run.
  • quasar replay <record.json> → re-runs, diffs plan, exit 0 if byte-identical. Library: quasar-cache::PlanCache exposes prepare/lookup/invalidate_by_table; quasar-obs::ReplayHarness exposes record/replay/diff.

13.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • placeholder goldens: generic + custom both pass, custom chosen when cost < 0.5*generic. Staleness invalidation: schema_version bump for T → entry evicted, cache_invalidation.txt asserts miss.
  • Conformance suite: 100% of conformance/testdata/ goldens pass, zero diffs. TPC-H plan goldens: Q1–Q22-lite plan text exact match.
  • TPC-H cost regression gate: per-query cost.total ≤ stored budget, CI fails on regression. Replay byte-identical: quasar replay diff empty for all conformance queries (I-DET).
  • Fuzz: 1-hour zero panics (cargo fuzz run fuzz_sql_optimize -- -max_total_time=3600, no crash corpus). proptest norm idempotent: 10000 samples pass norm(norm(e))==norm(e).
  • proptest confluence: 1000 samples pass multi-order convergence. Cache hit latency: generic lookup < 50 µs (no re-opt), quasar.cache.lookup_duration_us.
  • Soak RSS stability: peak RSS growth < 10 MB over 1-hour soak. Memo invariants under fuzz: I-MEMO (dedup) + I-COST (monotone) for all fuzz inputs.
  • References: §4 I-DET; §7 I-MEMO, I-COST, I-PHYS; §4 perf (optimize < 100 ms P99 TPC-H).

13.5 Conformance Fixtures

Under conformance/testdata/ (file → purpose: key assertions):

  • cache/generic_plan.txt → SELECT * FROM t WHERE a=$1, placeholder: generic plan, unknown-but-typed cardinality. cache/custom_plan.txt → same SQL, $1=42 (low-selectivity), custom cheaper: custom chosen, [cache:custom].
  • cache/generic_plan_chosen.txt → $1 high-selectivity but generic still cheaper: generic kept, [cache:generic]. cache/staleness_invalidation.txt → optimize, bump schema_version, re-lookup: cache.misses increments, re-optimized.
  • cache/stats_staleness.txt → same, triggered by stats_version bump: miss on stats refresh. obs/event_log_shape.txt → single SELECT, event log fields present: JSON-L has TaskPush,RuleFired,WinnerUpdated.
  • obs/replay_identical.txt → optimize, serialize record, replay, diff: byte-identical plan, exit 0. obs/replay_seed_stable.txt → same seed → same rule order → same plan: seeded randomness determinism.
  • tpch/q1.txt–q22.txt → TPC-H Q1–Q22-lite full plan goldens: plan tree exact match, cost ≤ budget. regression/cache_nil_placeholder.txt → no-placeholder query cached generic, lookup w/ non-empty values: returns generic, no panic.
  • regression/double_invalidation.txt → two consecutive schema_version bumps: single eviction, no double-free/corruption in secondary index. Malformed: PlaceholderUnbound (placeholder in IR, no value for custom plan → OptError::PlaceholderUnbound); replay corrupted stats snapshot (hash mismatch → OptError::MemoStale).

13.6 Pitfalls

  • Generic reused after stats change w/o staleness → stale cardinality, wrong join order (stats_version not in CacheKey; signal: cache.hits high, quality degrades after ANALYZE) → include stats_version in CacheKey, invalidate on stats refresh. Substitution rebuilds too much → cache miss every custom-plan lookup (substitute_placeholders interns new scalar even when structurally identical; signal: cache.misses high for prepared stmts) → normalize substituted scalar pre-intern, structural hash check before insert.
  • Replay non-determinism from HashMap iteration → replay diff non-empty, CI flake (rule order from HashMap iteration in dispatch; signal: obs/replay_identical.txt intermittent) → IndexMap (insertion-order) or BTreeMap in dispatch table. Event log unbounded growth → OOM in multi-query session (event log Vec never cleared; signal: RSS grows in soak, memo.members stable) → drain after each query, ring buffer or file.
  • Confluence failure → norm(norm(e)) != norm(e) in proptest (non-confluent, ordering-sensitive rules; signal: proptest shrinks to minimal failing expr) → fix rule precedence, ordering constraint in dispatch. Custom threshold too aggressive → no reuse, optimize time dominates (CUSTOM_PLAN_THRESHOLD=1.0 or stats always show custom cheaper; signal: cache.hits near zero) → lower threshold, fixture asserting generic when costs equal.
  • TPC-H golden drift after cost fix → CI fails on q*.txt mismatch (cost fix changes optimal plan; signal: quasar bench tpch cost delta) → re-baseline goldens intentionally, update stored budget, review quality.

13.7 Performance Concerns

  • Cache RwLock contention, high-concurrency → shard by CacheKey % N_SHARDS, per-shard RwLock. Generic cardinality (unknown-but-typed) over-broad search → cap exploration budget for generic opt, store budget alongside generic CachedEntry.
  • Substitution allocates new ScalarId per call → precompute per (scalar_id,placeholder_values_hash), cache in PlanCache. JSON-L serialization in hot path → batch writes, serialize async via bounded channel to background writer.
  • cargo fuzz corpus explosion → limit 10000 entries, prune duplicates by coverage map hash. TPC-H conformance run time → cache TPC-H catalog + stats as fixture snapshots, skip real catalog RPC in opt-test.
  • proptest slow on large trees → max_shrink_iters=1000, arb_scalar_expr depth ≤ 6.

13.8 Edge Cases

  • Zero-placeholder query cached generic, later run w/ non-empty values → generic returned, extra values ignored (no PlaceholderUnbound). Schema version wraps (u64 overflow) → treat wrap as always-stale, invalidate all entries.
  • Replay record from older version (extra JSON fields) → no deny_unknown_fields, forward-compat: ignore extra fields, warn. Soak: optimize returns BudgetExceeded → cache does NOT store partial plan, return error, no entry.
  • Fuzz: SQL producing empty FlowSpec → no panic, clean OptError::NoPlanFound. Secondary index has stale CacheKey after GC → points to evicted key, invalidate_by_table skips missing keys silently.
  • Stats snapshot hash collision in replay → two snapshots same hash → wrong stats, plan differs → store full snapshot content, not just hash. Concurrent invalidate_by_table + lookup → RwLock write during invalidation, lookup blocks until release, no torn reads.

13.9 Failure Analysis

  • PlanCache corruption (wrong shard insert) → all queries on affected shard (detect: placeholder golden mismatch, spurious hits) → disable shard via feature flag, no-cache fallback; fix shard hash, regression golden. Replay different plan (I-DET violation) → signals non-determinism in optimizer/rules (detect: quasar replay non-zero in CI) → block merge, investigate dispatch ordering; fix ordering, add replay fixture to permanent suite.
  • Fuzz crash: panic in rule application → single fuzz input, no prod impact (detect: cargo fuzz crash input in fuzz/artifacts/) → fix panic, add crash input as regression test; fix rule, re-run fuzz to confirm clean. Conformance golden regression → CI gate fails (detect: opt-test exit 1, diff shown) → do not merge until updated/fixed; verify plan change correct, re-baseline or fix.
  • TPC-H cost regression → plan quality degraded (detect: quasar bench tpch cost > budget) → diff vs previous cost report; identify phase that changed cost model, revert or adjust budget. Event log fill-up on slow disk → single-query latency spike (detect: writer blocks optimize loop, quasar.event_log.write_lag_ms spikes) → async bounded channel, drop on full buffer, quasar.event_log.drops.

13.10 Operational Notes

Tracing spans: cache::lookup{key_fingerprint,hit:bool,plan_kind:generic|custom}; cache::invalidate{table_id,evicted_count}; obs::replay{sql_hash,identical:bool}; fuzz::run (test only) {seed,input_len,outcome:ok|error|panic}.

Metrics summary (all phases, as of Phase 13): quasar.rules.fired{kind} (counter, phases 4/8/9); quasar.memo.groups/quasar.memo.members (gauge, phase 3); quasar.search.duration_ms (histogram, phase 10); quasar.prune.count (counter, phase 10); quasar.distsql.plan_duration_ms (histogram, phase 12); quasar.cache.hits/.misses (counter, phase 13); quasar.cache.lookup_duration_us (histogram, phase 13); quasar.event_log.drops (counter, phase 13).

  • Deployment/compatibility: quasar-cache, quasar-obs library crates, no separate binary; CacheKey internal, no wire commitment, cache in-process (no external store); event log JSON-L additive-only (new EventKind variants) backward-compatible; ReplayRecord JSON "v":1 version field, consumers check before deserialize; fuzz corpora in fuzz/corpus/ (VCS), CI cargo fuzz run -- -max_total_time=300 (5 min), nightly soak 1 hour.
  • Rollback: cache off via QUASAR_PLAN_CACHE=0 (optimize-every-query); event log off via QUASAR_EVENT_LOG=off; replay harness test-only, not in prod path.
  • Done state (Phase 13 and all Quasar): all 14 phases delivered; full conformance green; TPC-H/TPC-DS-lite goldens baselined; fuzz canary clean; replay byte-identical; soak stable. No ✅ tag until code built from this blueprint.

Threat Model

Quasar is a library; OS-level isolation (process sandbox, network ACLs, cgroups) is host responsibility. Trust boundary: untrusted SQL text + placeholder values from any client; catalog + statistics semi-trusted (operator-controlled, possibly stale/tampered). Required behavior: degrade to safe suboptimal plan — never panic, allocate unbounded memory, or expose row data.

Attack Surface

Surface Trust Entry point
SQL text Untrusted quasar-sql lexer
Placeholder values ($1, $2 …) Untrusted quasar-bind substitution
Catalog (schemas, indexes, zone configs) Semi-trusted quasar-cat reader
Statistics (row counts, histograms) Semi-trusted quasar-stats reader
optgen-generated Rust Build-time trusted quasar-optgen build.rs
Cargo deps Build-time trusted Cargo.lock pin
Operator zone configs Operator-trusted quasar-cat zone validation

Threats (T-1 … T-9)

Per-threat columns: Mitigations / Detection (telemetry) / Fallback+Recovery.

T-1 — Adversarial SQL → combinatorial blowup

Vector: deep-nested subqueries, huge IN-lists (IN (v1 … v10000)), many-way joins (n ≥ 12), chained CTEs → exponential memo growth / unbounded B&B task-stack → runaway latency / OOM. Severity High.

  • Mitigations:
    • Memo.budget: Cost — hard cap total memo expressions; constructor → BudgetExceeded on breach.
    • Optimizer.budget: Cost — B&B prunes sub-trees when running_cost >= budget (Phase 10).
    • reorder limit: ReorderJoins gated by reorder_limit (default 8); excess joins kept in input order (bushy fallback off).
    • IN-list cap in_list_cap (default 1000): larger lists → single full-scan span + remaining filter (tight=false), no disjunction spans.
    • recursive CTE depth: bind-time check; > cte_depth_limit → OptError::Unsupported.
    • memo_group_limit hard cap: GroupState::Saturated past limit; exploration stops.
    • subquery flatten depth: correlation rewrite capped at subquery_depth_limit.
  • Detection/telemetry: budget_exceeded_total counter (alert on rate > threshold); optimize_latency histogram (p99 spike ↔ near-budget); event log records BudgetExceeded + GroupId + task-stack depth at abort.
  • Fallback: on BudgetExceeded, extract best-known winner from memo root (may be None → NoPlanFound); return suboptimal plan + OptError::BudgetExceeded(cost); caller rejects or runs degraded.
  • Recovery: operator raises reorder_limit/in_list_cap only on controlled workloads; reduce join complexity at app layer.

T-2 — Pathological cardinality (poisoned statistics)

Vector: wrong stats (row_count=1 on billion-row table, distinct_count > row_count, zero-width buckets) → catastrophic plans (NL join on large table, no index, sort of 10^9 rows). Severity Medium.

  • Mitigations (clamps in Statistics derivation): distinct_count ≤ row_count; null_count ≤ row_count; selectivity ∈ [1e-9, 1.0]; row_count ≥ 0.0; max_row_count_estimate cap (default 10^12, cardinality advisory only — never drives allocation); histogram invariant num_eq + num_range ≥ 0, malformed → uniform; multi-col distinct_count > product of per-col → clamp + warn; is_estimated flag propagated, planner WARNs when set on any plan relation.
  • Detection: q_error histogram (estimated/actual ratio via EXPLAIN ANALYZE); alert p95 > 100.
  • Recovery: ANALYZE refresh (quasar-stats harness); selectivity-pin hint (Phase 5); SET optimizer_use_histograms = false → uniform estimates.

T-3 — Resource exhaustion (memo memory, span explosion)

Vector: unconstrained memo growth (Arena<Group>+Arena<MExpr>) or span explosion (OR of many range predicates) exhausts heap. Severity High.

Resource Cap Mechanism
Memo groups memo_group_limit (50 000) Constructor → BudgetExceeded
Memo expressions memo_expr_limit (200 000) Same
Spans/constraint span_limit (10 000) Solver clips + tight=false
ScalarExpr depth scalar_depth_limit (1 000) bind-time reject
Arena store arena_byte_limit jemalloc slab; abort if full
Task stack depth task_depth_limit depth counter; abort + BudgetExceeded
  • Caps fixed at OptimizerConfig construction (not runtime-mutable → no TOCTOU); arena alloc pre-validated (checked arithmetic, no unwrap).
  • Detection: memo_bytes gauge; memo_groups + memo_members counters; span_count histogram.
  • Recovery: restart with simpler query / lower join count; tune memo_group_limit to fleet p99.

T-4 — Placeholder type confusion

Vector: $1 bound INT at prepare, supplied TEXT at execute → wrong span (string key vs int index key), bad coercion, panic in Datum compare. Severity Medium.

  • Mitigations: strict bind-time typing in quasar-bind — each Placeholder(idx) assigned DataType at prepare, recorded in PlaceholderMap; execute-time validate vs PlaceholderMap[idx].typ, mismatch → OptError::PlaceholderUnbound (returned, not panicked); quasar-constraint coercion explicit only (no implicit widening from Datum); cardinality Datum arithmetic type-checks before dispatch, unknown → uniform selectivity.
  • Detection: placeholder_type_error_total counter; logged with index + expected vs actual type.
  • Recovery: application fix; return error, no optimizer-side recovery.

T-5 — Catalog/statistics tampering (stale or malicious)

Vector: compromised catalog injects bad index descriptors (wrong key cols), fake zone configs (non-existent region), inflated stats → adversarially cheap plans. Severity Medium.

  • Mitigations: catalog version token — monotonic per quasar-cat snapshot, memos keyed to version (Phase 13); stats staleness — Statistics.collected_at, WARN stats_stale when age > stats_max_age; zone validation — every RegionId must exist in region registry, else Distribution::Any; index descriptor bind-time check — key cols ⊆ table cols, types match schema, else reject; catalog read-only from optimizer (no writes); Spans sanity — start ≤ end, inverted spans dropped.
  • Detection: catalog_version_mismatch_total; stats_stale_total; zone_config_invalid_total.
  • Recovery: re-fetch catalog from authoritative source; invalidate cached memos (MemoStale); alert operator.

T-6 — Determinism attacks (HashMap iteration order)

Vector: non-deterministic HashMap order → identical input yields different physical plan → non-reproducible plans, replay harness useless. Severity Medium.

  • Mitigations: all plan-affecting iteration (rule order, winner tie-break, processor placement) uses BTreeMap/IndexMap (deterministic insertion order), never HashMap; HashMap only for order-irrelevant lookups (intern table, rules-applied bitmask); Optimizer.seed: u64 sole tie-break randomness, recorded in event log; Invariant I10: optimize(sql, seed=S) == optimize(sql, seed=S) for identical memo state; rustc-hash/ahash allowed for intern tables only — std::collections::HashMap forbidden in quasar-search + quasar-distsql (Clippy lint).
  • Detection: replay harness records session, replays same seed, diffs physical plans; non-identity diff = I10 violation → CI fail.
  • Recovery: bisect offending rule/task path; convert map to BTreeMap.

T-7 — Supply chain (optgen codegen, dependencies)

Vector: compromised Cargo dep or optgen codegen bug → silent mis-optimization, panic, or RCE. Severity Medium.

  • Mitigations: Cargo.lock pinned (CI fails on drift); cargo audit per push (HIGH/CRITICAL block merge); optgen output deterministic given .opt sources — build.rs writes to OUT_DIR, hash of .opt inputs embedded as compile-time assertion; generated code committed under crates/quasar-rules/src/generated/ for review/diff; cargo deny license allowlist (MIT/Apache-2.0/BSD); minimal deps (no serde_json in hot path, no proc-macro in generated path).
  • Detection: cargo audit CI artifact; generated-code diff in PR review.
  • Recovery: pin previous known-good version; open advisory; patch or replace.

T-8 — EXPLAIN information disclosure

Vector: EXPLAIN (OPT)/(DISTSQL) leaks row counts, cardinality, spans → reveals data distribution (e.g. histogram upper-bound exposes column values). Severity Low.

  • Mitigations: EXPLAIN (OPT) debug-only, documented unstable, gate behind operator privilege in prod; histogram upper_bound: Datum shown only when explain_include_stats = true (off by default); default EXPLAIN renders operator names + estimated cost, no raw Datum; quasar-obs cardinality span fields tagged sensitive=true (exporters may filter).
  • Scope: Quasar reads stats metadata, not stored row data — disclosure limited to distribution metadata; mitigation operational (privilege gate), not cryptographic.

T-9 — Operator misconfiguration (bad zone configs)

Vector: table assigned to region with no leaseholders, or LOS gateway region absent → queries routed Distribution::Single to non-local node (correct but slow). Severity Low.

  • Mitigations: zone validation at catalog load — every RegionId ∈ RegionRegistry, else rejected + logged; LOS gateway region must be in RegionRegistry, else fall back to standard distributed plan; EXPLAIN (DISTSQL) shows node=UNKNOWN for unresolvable nodes (misconfig visible).
  • Detection: unresolvable_node_total counter.
  • Recovery: operator corrects zone config; cache invalidation re-plans next query; no data loss.

T-10 — Cross-Tenant Plan Cache Aliasing (embedder misconfiguration)

Vector: CacheKey{sql_fingerprint, schema_version, stats_version} (ADR-A19) is scoped to one Catalog + one PlanCache instance — Quasar provides no tenant field or cross-tenant isolation primitive of its own. If an embedding SQL layer shares a single PlanCache across tenants without a tenant-namespaced schema_version (e.g. two tenants' catalogs both report schema_version=7 under one shared cache), identical-fingerprint queries from different tenants alias to the same cached generic plan, and one tenant's index/cardinality shape becomes visible to another via EXPLAIN (OPT). Severity Medium.

  • Mitigations: documented hard operational requirement (Repository Layout quasar-cache/, ADR-A19) — one Catalog + PlanCache pair per tenant, or a schema_version namespaced per tenant, is the embedder's responsibility; Quasar's own contract is "optimizer scope = whatever Catalog/PlanCache instance you construct it with," so a correctly-scoped deployment has no shared state to alias across in the first place.
  • Detection: deployment-topology audit (not a runtime Quasar metric) — verify the embedder's tenant-to-Catalog/PlanCache mapping is 1:1 or that schema_version is tenant-namespaced; cache_hit_total split by (tenant, table) at the embedder layer surfaces an unexpected hit if the precondition is violated.
  • Recovery: if audit finds a shared, un-namespaced cache, the fix is topological (split instances or namespace schema_version) — not a Quasar code change; until fixed, disabling the plan cache (optimizer.cache_enabled=false) removes the aliasing surface at the cost of optimize() running every time (no plan-cache speedup, M6 target not met, but no leakage).

Threat Matrix

ID Threat Severity Mitigated by Fallback
T-1 Combinatorial blowup High Budget caps, reorder_limit, IN-list cap BudgetExceeded + best-so-far
T-2 Poisoned stats Medium Sanity clamps, is_estimated flag Uniform selectivity
T-3 Resource exhaustion High arena_byte_limit, span_limit, task_depth_limit BudgetExceeded
T-4 Placeholder type confusion Medium Strict bind-time type check OptError::PlaceholderUnbound
T-5 Catalog/stats tampering Medium Version tokens, staleness checks, schema validation MemoStale invalidation
T-6 Determinism attacks Medium BTreeMap in plan paths, seed tie-break, replay harness I10 CI gate
T-7 Supply chain Medium Cargo.lock pin, cargo-audit, cargo-deny, generated-code diff Dep pin/patch
T-8 EXPLAIN disclosure Low Privilege gate, Datum redaction default Operational
T-9 Operator misconfiguration Low Zone validation, unresolvable_node metric LOS fallback
T-10 Cross-tenant plan cache aliasing Medium Per-tenant Catalog/PlanCache instance or namespaced schema_version (embedder contract) Disable plan cache (cache_enabled=false)

Failure Modes & Recovery

Failure Taxonomy

Class Name Error signal Automatic recovery Severity
A No plan found OptError::NoPlanFound Naive fallback plan High
B Budget exceeded OptError::BudgetExceeded(cost) Best-so-far extraction Medium
C Bad cardinality estimate Plan regression (no error) Plan-quality gate (CI) Medium
D Stale / missing stats Degraded plans + is_estimated Re-plan after ANALYZE Medium
E Memo staleness OptError::MemoStale Cache invalidation + re-optimize Medium
F Non-deterministic plan Replay divergence I10 CI gate blocks merge High
G Constraint span explosion Loose constraint fallback Span cap + tight=false Low
H optgen codegen drift Build-time assertion failure Build fails; diff reviewed High
I Numeric issues in cost NaN/Inf in Cost fields Clamp + assertion High
J Leaseholder/locality placement drift FlowSpec routes to a stale leaseholder Executor-side re-route; plan unaffected Medium

Per-Class Detail

Columns: Symptoms / Root causes / Detection / Auto recovery / Manual + Operator / Forensics / Long-term.

Class A — No Plan Found

  • Symptoms: optimize() → Err(NoPlanFound); EXPLAIN aborts "no plan for required properties".
  • Root causes: required PhysProps (ordering+distribution) unsatisfiable by any enforcer chain (e.g. ordering on non-projected column); all impl rules rejected (no IndexScan, no HashJoin for cross-product); all enforcers (Sort, Distribute) cost above budget.
  • Detection: no_plan_found_total counter; event log records final goal (GroupId, PhysProps).
  • Auto recovery: naive fallback TableReader → Sort (if ordering req) → Distribute (if distribution req), marked suboptimal=true; always valid (never NoPlanFound on simple scan).
  • Manual/operator: inspect EXPLAIN (OPT); relax ORDER BY / shorten projection; ensure projected cols include ordering cols; verify zone configs; add missing index.
  • Forensics: event log goal + all losing candidates; memo dump.
  • Long-term: enforcer-completeness test — every RelExpr + arbitrary PhysProps yields ≥ naive fallback (Phase 9 conformance).

Class B — Budget Exceeded

  • Symptoms: Err(BudgetExceeded(cost)); plan may be suboptimal; budget_exceeded_total spikes.
  • Root causes: joins beyond reorder_limit, huge IN-list, deep subqueries; memo hits memo_group_limit/memo_expr_limit before convergence.
  • Detection: budget_exceeded_total; optimize_latency p99 spike; memo_groups near cap.
  • Auto recovery: extractor walks memo-root winner map for PhysProps::Any, returns best cost so far (may be None → Class A naive fallback).
  • Manual/operator: raise reorder_limit for known-complex queries; add hint; simplify join graph; tune OptimizerConfig.memo_group_limit; watch budget_exceeded_total / total_queries.
  • Forensics: event log BudgetExceeded + task-stack snapshot; memo_groups at abort.
  • Long-term: per-query-class budget tiers (OLTP vs OLAP); adaptive budget scaling with complexity estimate.

Class C — Bad Cardinality Estimate

  • Symptoms: no error; plan slow; EXPLAIN ANALYZE q_error (actual/estimated) far from 1.
  • Root causes: correlated columns without multi-col stats; non-uniform distributions with too few buckets; skew after large batch insert.
  • Detection: q_error histogram, p95 > 10 → WARN; plan-quality gate (CI) — TPC-H/TPC-DS-lite plans vs known-good cost baseline, regression > 10% fails; EXPLAIN ANALYZE feedback (project 318 actual rows) logged as delta by quasar-obs.
  • Auto recovery: none at optimize time (optimizer cannot know actual rows); with feedback integrated, future re-plans pick up corrected stats.
  • Manual/operator: ANALYZE refresh; add multi-col stats for correlated sets; cardinality hint (Phase 5); tune histogram bucket count.
  • Forensics: EXPLAIN ANALYZE output; q_error export; stats snapshot at optimization time.
  • Long-term: auto is_estimated escalation when q_error exceeds threshold; adaptive histogram bucket growth.

Class D — Stale / Missing Statistics

  • Symptoms: Statistics.is_estimated = true; stats_stale_total rising; plans regress after bulk load.
  • Root causes: stats collected at T but insert/delete at T+Δ without re-ANALYZE; new table has no stats.
  • Detection: is_estimated propagated via LogicalProps.stats; WARN stats_stale when collected_at age > stats_max_age; stats_stale_total per table, alert on rate.
  • Auto recovery: uniform-distribution estimates when histogram absent; emits is_estimated=true.
  • Manual/operator: trigger stats refresh; optimizer_use_histograms=false if permanently unavailable; schedule periodic ANALYZE; set stats_max_age to churn rate.
  • Forensics: Statistics.collected_at; quasar-obs log per stale relation.
  • Long-term: auto incremental stats on row-count delta threshold (Phase 13); per-table stats TTL.

Class E — Memo Staleness (schema change under cached plan)

  • Symptoms: MemoStale on cache lookup; cache_invalidation_total spike; cold-cache latency rise.
  • Root causes: DDL (ALTER TABLE, CREATE/DROP INDEX, ANALYZE) changes catalog version or stats fingerprint after caching; fingerprint mismatch at execution.
  • Detection: lookup compares memo_fingerprint (hash of catalog version + stats version + query text) to stored entry; mismatch → MemoStale.
  • Auto recovery: evict stale entry, re-optimize, store new winner; transparent to caller.
  • Manual/operator: force flush via quasar-cache invalidate_all() / invalidate_table(TableId); pre-warm cache after large DDL; watch cache_invalidation_total for churn.
  • Forensics: cache_invalidation_total reason=fingerprint_mismatch; quasar-obs event log.
  • Long-term: fine-grained invalidation (only plans referencing changed table); lazy invalidation for non-structural stat changes.

Class F — Non-Deterministic Plan (nondeterminism leak)

  • Symptoms: replay harness plan_diff != empty; CI gate replay_determinism fails.
  • Root causes: HashMap order in plan-affecting path (rule app, winner tie-break, processor order); non-seeded randomness; pointer value as tie-break.
  • Detection: replay harness (Phase 13, quasar-obs) records (sql, seed, stats_snapshot, catalog_snapshot) → replays → diffs physical plan; any diff = CI fail.
  • Auto recovery: none — correctness violation, must fix before merge.
  • Manual/operator: bisect rule-by-rule via replay bundle, replace HashMap → BTreeMap/IndexMap; do not deploy build; roll back to last deterministic version.
  • Forensics: replay bundle (sql, seed, catalog snapshot, stats snapshot, recorded plan, replayed plan, diff).
  • Long-term: Clippy lint banning std::collections::HashMap in quasar-search + quasar-distsql (Phase 10).

Class G — Constraint Solver Span Explosion

  • Symptoms: span_count near span_limit; tight=false on many-OR queries; plan falls back to full scan.
  • Root causes: disjunctive predicates (IN (v1 … vN) → OR, or explicit OR chain) → N spans per index column; multi-col indexes multiply.
  • Detection: span_count histogram; span_limit_hit_total counter; tight=false in EXPLAIN index-constraints.
  • Auto recovery: clip spans at span_limit, set tight=false, re-apply remaining predicate as post-scan filter; correct (no false negatives), scans more rows.
  • Manual/operator: raise span_limit if hardware allows; restructure query to cut OR fan-out.
  • Forensics: EXPLAIN (OPT) index-constraints showing tight=false + remaining filter.
  • Long-term: span merging (coalesce adjacent/overlapping before limit check); partial-index pre-filter.

Class H — optgen Codegen Drift

  • Symptoms: cargo build fails — assertion "optgen hash mismatch: .opt sources changed but generated code not regenerated".
  • Root causes: .opt source under crates/quasar-rules/src/ edited but build.rs not re-run (generated code committed manually with wrong content).
  • Detection: build.rs computes sha256(concat(.opt files)) vs hash in generated file header; mismatch → compile error.
  • Auto recovery: re-run cargo build → build.rs regenerates.
  • Manual/operator: delete stale generated files; cargo clean -p quasar-rules && cargo build.
  • Forensics: diff between .opt source and src/generated/dispatch.rs.
  • Long-term: generated files committed (PR diff catches hand-edits); CI cargo build --frozen verifies.

Class I — Numeric Issues in Cost (NaN / Inf)

  • Symptoms: Cost.total is NaN/Inf; total.partial_cmp() → None; panic in assert! or subtly wrong plan.
  • Root causes: divide-by-zero in cardinality (row_count=0 to cost model); f64::INFINITY * 0.0 = NaN in net cost; log(0) in selectivity.
  • Detection: cost_numeric_error_total counter; debug assert!(!total.is_nan() && !total.is_infinite()) in Cost::new().
  • Auto recovery: constructor clamps cpu/io/net NaN/Inf → f64::MAX / 4, total recomputed; clamped-cost plan heavily penalized, loses to any finite-cost plan.
  • Manual/operator: inspect expression cardinality; trace back to zero-row_count group.
  • Forensics: event log cost_numeric_error + ExprId + raw unclamped values.
  • Long-term: cardinality lower-bound clamp row_count ≥ 1.0 (ghost row, no divide-by-zero); proptest property — cost formula never NaN/Inf for any non-negative cardinality.

Class J — Leaseholder/Locality Placement Drift

  • Symptoms: FlowSpec places a TableReader/processor at a node that is no longer the range's leaseholder by the time the executor (project 318) consumes the plan; unnecessary cross-region hop; distsql_misplacement_total rises.
  • Root causes: quasar-cat RangeMeta (leaseholder-per-range) snapshot taken at bind time goes stale between optimization and execution — a lease transfer or rebalance (host cluster's Raft layer) happens in the gap; zone-config edit changes region affinity mid-query for a long-running plan-cache hit.
  • Detection: executor-side comparison of planned node vs actual lease holder at dispatch (outside Quasar; surfaced back via quasar-obs distsql_misplacement_total counter and remote_hop_ratio gauge, §23 Phase 11 / §24 Phase 12 telemetry hooks).
  • Auto recovery: none inside optimize() (Quasar is stateless per call and does not re-plan mid-flight); the executor performs the redirect at the KV layer (out of Quasar's scope) — the physical plan (join order, algorithm choice) stays valid, only node placement is suboptimal for one query.
  • Manual/operator: shorten stats.ttl_secs/plan-cache TTL for tables with active rebalancing; invalidate quasar-cache after a known large rebalance event; monitor remote_hop_ratio per table and re-ANALYZE/re-plan hot queries.
  • Forensics: RangeMeta snapshot timestamp vs actual lease-transfer timestamp (from host cluster's range descriptor log); distsql_misplacement_total by table/region pair.
  • Long-term: subscribe to push-based leaseholder-change notifications instead of point-in-time RangeMeta snapshots — the same live-push-channel infrastructure as the "Online stats auto-collection daemon" row in the Deferred Features Register would carry leaseholder-change events too, so this rides that feature's existing un-defer trigger rather than opening a second one.

Forensic Artifact Reference

Artifact Location Produced by Consumed by
Optimizer event log quasar-obs structured log All phases Incident response, replay
Memo dump quasar memo "<sql>" CLI quasar-memo Class A/B/F debugging
Replay bundle quasar-obs record session quasar-obs Class F determinism analysis
EXPLAIN (OPT) quasar explain --opt quasar-cli Class A/B/C/G analysis
EXPLAIN (DISTSQL) quasar explain --distsql quasar-distsql Class A node-placement
q_error histogram Prometheus export quasar-obs Class C plan-quality monitoring
Stats snapshot quasar-stats serialized snapshot quasar-stats Class D/F forensics
optgen diff git diff src/generated/ quasar-optgen Class H
RangeMeta snapshot + remote_hop_ratio quasar-obs / quasar-cat quasar-cat, quasar-obs Class J placement-drift forensics

Operational Readiness

Quasar is an embedded, stateless optimizer library co-located in SQL gateway nodes. "Operating" it means observing optimize() behavior, gating plan quality, and rolling optimizer/rule changes safely. All telemetry is emitted by quasar-obs (§25).

Metrics strategy

Metric Type Labels Alert use
quasar_optimize_seconds histogram query_class, cache_hit latency SLO, regression detection
quasar_rules_fired_total counter kind(normalize/explore/implement) search-cost drift
quasar_memo_groups / quasar_memo_members histogram query_class memo-size growth
quasar_memo_bytes histogram — budget pressure
quasar_tasks_executed / quasar_prune_count counter — B&B effectiveness
quasar_fallback_total counter reason(NoPlanFound/BudgetExceeded) health (primary alert)
quasar_est_q_error histogram — estimator drift (when EXPLAIN ANALYZE feedback available)
quasar_cache_hit_ratio / quasar_cache_evictions gauge/counter — cache health
quasar_plan_cost histogram query_fingerprint plan-quality drift gate

Cardinality control: query_fingerprint is bucketed (top-N + "other"); never emit raw SQL as a label.

Tracing strategy

One root span per optimize() (trace_id = request id from the SQL layer); child spans per Task kind (OptimizeGroup/Expr, ExploreGroup/Expr, ApplyRule, EnforceProps). Span attributes: group id, rule id/name, cost-so-far, prune decision. Sampling: head-based 1% in steady state, 100% for queries that fall back or exceed a latency threshold (tail-based escalation).

Structured logging — optimizer event log

OptimizerEvent enum (JSON lines): {ts, trace_id, kind, group, rule, cost, detail}. Kinds: RuleFired, GroupExplored, WinnerSet, EnforcerInserted, Pruned, Fallback, BudgetExceeded. The event log is the forensic backbone for plan-regression postmortems and the input to deterministic replay (§25). Default level: Fallback/BudgetExceeded always; full event stream behind a per-query debug flag.

Dashboard design

  • Health row: fallback rate, p50/p95/p99 optimize latency by query_class, cache hit ratio.
  • Quality row: plan-cost distribution vs the per-fingerprint baseline (drift = regression signal), est_q_error histogram.
  • Search row: rules_fired by kind, memo_groups/members, prune_count, memo_bytes.
  • Cache row: size, hit ratio, eviction rate, staleness invalidations/min.

Alerting philosophy

Alert on aggregate health, never on a single slow plan. Page on: fallback-rate spike (>1% of queries), p99 optimize-latency regression vs trailing baseline, plan-cost-drift on a high-traffic fingerprint, cache-staleness storm (mass invalidation). Ticket (no page): rising est_q_error, slow cache hit-ratio decline.

SLO definitions

Query class optimize() p95 optimize() p99 Plan-quality
simple (1–2 tables) 3 ms 8 ms cost ≤ 1.1× baseline
medium (3–10 joins) 30 ms 80 ms cost ≤ 1.25× baseline
complex (11–32 joins) 150 ms 400 ms best-effort under budget

Cite §4 constraints. Plan-quality SLO enforced in CI via TPC-H/TPC-DS cost_ceilings.toml and in prod via the drift gate.

Incident response + runbooks

docs/runbooks/: debugging_plan_regression.md (EXPLAIN(OPT) → memo dump → replay bundle → diff vs last-good), plan_cache_ops.md (invalidation, monitoring, manual flush), stats_collection.md (refresh stale stats), adding_a_rule.md. Standard regression flow: pull the replay bundle for the offending fingerprint, re-run under the current and prior optimizer build, diff winners, bisect the responsible rule, disable it via the rule-disable list pending a fix.

Backup / restore

Quasar holds no durable state. What must be snapshotted for reproducibility: the stat catalog and schema/catalog versions (epoch-stamped). Replay bundles (sql + catalog snapshot + stats snapshot + seed) are the restore unit — restoring a bundle reproduces a plan byte-identically (I10).

Deployment topology

Optimizer linked into every SQL gateway node; fully stateless and horizontally scalable (add gateways). Plan cache is per-node (default) or a shared tier (optional). No leader, no quorum, no shared mutable state across queries (the catalog/stats are Arc-shared read-only).

Canary + rolling upgrade — shadow-optimize

New optimizer/rule-set ships dark first: run it in parallel with the incumbent on a sampled fraction of live queries, diff the produced plans + costs, and surface regressions before cutover. Promote only when shadow plan-cost is within the quality SLO across the fingerprint distribution. Mixed-version clusters are safe because FlowSpec is the stable contract (Compatibility & Versioning).

Rollback

Two levers, both hot: (1) pin the optimizer crate version (revert the gateway binary); (2) rule-disable list — disable a specific rule by name at runtime without redeploy (turns one .opt rule off, forcing the search to route around it). Disabling a rule only ever widens the plan space minus that transform; it cannot produce an invalid plan (Normalize-confluence + enforcer-completeness hold).

Migration + compatibility

Optimizer upgrades invalidate the plan cache (fingerprint includes the optimizer rule-set hash, Compatibility & Versioning). Stat-format and catalog-format upgrades are versioned and additive (Compatibility & Versioning). No data migration — Quasar owns no persistent data.

Compatibility & Versioning

Quasar exposes exactly one stable external contract — the FlowSpec it hands to the execution engine (project 318). Everything internal (Memo, Task, PhysProps, rule numbering, EXPLAIN(OPT) text) is explicitly unstable and may change between minor versions. Stability tiers below.

Stability tiers

Surface Tier Guarantee
FlowSpec / ProcessorSpec / Router / Stream Stable wire Additive-only: new ProcessorCore/Router variants are feature-gated; existing fields never change meaning. Old executors reject unknown variants explicitly.
optimize(req) -> Result<PhysicalPlan,_> entrypoint signature Stable API SemVer-governed; breaking changes ⇒ major bump.
stat-catalog on-disk format, Histogram/Bucket Stable on-disk Versioned header; reader supports current + previous format.
catalog descriptors (TableDesc/IndexDesc/ZoneConfig) Stable on-disk Additive fields; epoch-stamped.
EXPLAIN (OPT), memo dump, rule-firing trace Unstable/debug May change any release; never parse programmatically.
RuleId numbering, GroupId/ExprId Internal Meaningless across builds/memos.
rule names (rule-disable list) Stable operator surface Two-release deprecation window before rename/removal.

Wire compatibility (the FlowSpec contract)

FlowSpec is the only thing that crosses the Quasar→executor trust boundary. Rules:

  • Additive variants only. A new physical capability (e.g. a new join processor) is a new ProcessorCore variant guarded by a cluster feature flag. The optimizer emits it only when every target executor advertises support.
  • Explicit version field. FlowSpec carries a format_version; the executor refuses (clear error) rather than mis-executes an unknown version.
  • Mixed-version clusters. During a rolling upgrade the optimizer plans to the minimum advertised executor version in the flow's target node set — never emits a processor an older executor on the path cannot run.

Storage compatibility

Stat and catalog formats carry a version byte. Reader supports N and N−1; the writer emits N. A format bump triggers a one-time stat re-collection (out-of-band) and a plan-cache flush. Histograms are versioned independently so equi-depth → future histogram types is a clean migration.

Schema evolution

Catalog changes (add/drop column, add/drop index, zone-config change) bump the schema epoch; any cached plan whose fingerprint references the changed object is invalidated (§25). Additive changes (new nullable column, new index) never break an existing plan — they only unlock new alternatives on the next optimize().

API stability + SemVer

Optimizer crate follows SemVer. A plan change is NOT a breaking change under SemVer — better/different plans ship in minor/patch releases. But every optimizer release changes the rule-set hash, so it is plan-cache-invalidating (caches keyed on that hash auto-flush). Breaking changes are reserved for: optimize() signature changes, FlowSpec non-additive changes (avoided), and stat-format reader drops.

Migration guarantees

  • Stat-format upgrade: reader handles previous version; collection daemon re-emits current. No downtime.
  • Optimizer upgrade: plan cache flushes on rule-set-hash change; first optimize() per fingerprint pays a cold cost, then warms.
  • Rolling upgrade: shadow-optimize (Operational Readiness) validates plan quality before fleet cutover.

Deprecation policy

A rule is deprecated by marking it in the registry (still fires, logs a deprecation counter) for a two-release window, then removed. Removing a rule only narrows the explored plan space; correctness is preserved by Normalize-confluence + enforcer-completeness. Operator-facing rule names follow the same two-release window.

Rolling-upgrade guarantee (summary)

A cluster running optimizer vN and executor v(N−1) together is safe because (1) FlowSpec is additive + version-gated, (2) the optimizer plans to the minimum executor version on each flow path, and (3) the plan cache is keyed on the optimizer rule-set hash so the two versions never share a cached plan.

Testing & Verification Strategy

Correctness first (per §6). The optimizer is tested as a deterministic function (sql, catalog, stats, seed) → plan, so the backbone is datadriven golden testing (CockroachDB opt-tester style) plus property tests that assert the §7 invariants directly.

Datadriven golden suite (the backbone)

quasar opt-test conformance/testdata/ runs every .opt fixture. Each fixture is a directive + SQL + expected rendered output; a mismatch prints a unified diff; --rewrite regenerates goldens (reviewed in PR). Directives:

Directive Asserts Example fixture
norm normalized memo + rule-firing trace norm/predicates.opt
explore memo members added by exploration explore/join_order.opt
opt final chosen physical plan opt/multi_join.opt
memo full memo dump (groups/members/props) memo/memo_structure.opt
stats row-count + selectivity estimates stats/cardinality.opt
index-constraints spans + remaining filter index-constraints/range.opt
dist FlowSpec processors/streams/routers dist/multi_region.opt
placeholder generic vs custom plan choice placeholder/generic_vs_custom.opt

Unit testing (per crate)

quasar-props (FD closure, key derivation, OrderingChoice::simplify), quasar-stats (per-predicate selectivity, join cardinality, missing-stats degradation), quasar-constraint (span intersect/union/invert, tightness), quasar-cost (each cost formula, monotonicity), quasar-memo (intern dedup, group props), quasar-distsql (placement, router selection).

Integration testing

Full optimize() over the opt/ and tpch/ fixtures; assert chosen plan + that the produced FlowSpec round-trips through the executor's decoder stub.

Property testing (proptest) — asserts invariants directly

Property Invariant
normalize(normalize(e)) == normalize(e); order-independent I3 (idempotent + confluent)
parent.cost.total ≥ Σ chosen-child.cost.total I4 (monotonicity)
identical (op,children,scalar) ⇒ one ExprId I1 (dedup)
same (sql,catalog,stats,seed) ⇒ byte-identical plan I10 (determinism)
root winner.provided.satisfies(required) OR NoPlanFound I5 (props satisfied)
B&B winner == exhaustive winner under same budget I7 (optimality)

Fuzzing (cargo-fuzz, structure-aware)

Targets in fuzz/fuzz_targets/: fuzz_memo (arbitrary RelExpr → build memo → check I1/I2), fuzz_rules (arbitrary MExpr → apply all rules → check idempotency + no panic), fuzz_constraint (arbitrary predicates → solver → soundness: spans never exclude a satisfying row), fuzz_cost (arbitrary MExpr+stats → cost → finite + monotone), fuzz_optimizer (arbitrary bound RelExpr → optimize → I5/I7/I12 + no panic). Arbitrary impls generate type-correct trees (structure-aware) so fuzzing reaches deep into search, not just the parser.

Differential testing (advisory)

Diff Quasar's cardinality estimates and plan shapes against PostgreSQL EXPLAIN and/or CockroachDB on a shared schema + dataset. Used to find estimator blind spots, not as a pass/fail gate (plan choices legitimately differ). Results feed the cost-model calibration doc.

Chaos / fault injection

Inject: missing stats (force is_estimated), budget = 0 (force BudgetExceeded fallback), unsatisfiable required props (force NoPlanFound), poisoned stats (NDV = 0, row_count = 1e18). Assert graceful degradation + no panic + bounded memory.

Deterministic replay

Every optimization can emit a replay bundle (sql + catalog snapshot + stats snapshot + seed). The replay test re-runs a bundle and asserts a byte-identical plan (I10). Bundles from prod incidents become regression fixtures.

Soak

quasar bench driver runs randomized type-correct queries continuously for N hours; watches for panics, monotonic memory growth (leak), and latency drift. Acceptance gate: 1-hour fuzz/soak with zero panics (§25).

Conformance validation + plan-quality gate

CI runs the full datadriven suite (must be green) plus the TPC-H/TPC-DS plan-cost gate: each query's chosen plan cost must be ≤ the ceiling in cost_ceilings.toml. A regression that raises a plan's cost above its ceiling fails CI — this is how plan quality is held across changes.

Compatibility + performance-regression testing

FlowSpec round-trips across format versions (decode old, encode new). Optimize-latency and plan-cost are tracked per commit (criterion + the cost gate); a statistically significant regression fails CI. The full ladder: unit → datadriven golden → property → integration → fuzz → differential (advisory) → soak → CI gates.

Build & Release Engineering

Build Graph

Cargo workspace root
  +-- build.rs (quasar-rules): runs quasar-optgen over crates/quasar-rules/rules/*.opt
  |     -> crates/quasar-rules/src/generated/*.rs   (same .opt set -> byte-identical)
  |     (quasar-optgen pinned as build-dependency in Cargo.toml)
  +-- compile all 16 crates (cargo dependency order)
  +-- quasar-cli links all crates -> `quasar` binary

optgen codegen is the only non-trivial build-time generation. Sandboxed: build.rs reads rules/*.opt, calls quasar-optgen::compile(), writes OUT_DIR/generated/*.rs. Output checked in to crates/quasar-rules/src/generated/; build.rs asserts byte-identity with checked-in copy (CI fails on drift).

Reproducible Builds

Mechanism File/Tool Guarantee
Toolchain pin rust-toolchain.toml (channel, components, targets) Identical rustc/cargo
Dependency lock Cargo.lock committed Transitive versions frozen
optgen determinism hash(sorted .opt) in generated header build.rs asserts; CI checks
Linker flags .cargo/config.toml (lto, codegen-units=1 release) Deterministic LLVM codegen
SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH release CI workflow Reproducible timestamps
RUSTFLAGS .cargo/config.toml No per-engineer drift

MSRV Policy

  • MSRV in Cargo.toml (rust-version) + rust-toolchain.toml; CI matrix stable + MSRV; break requires ADR.
  • Current: stable-6-months-prior to release; re-evaluated per minor release.

CI/CD Topology (GitHub Actions)

ci.yml (push to main, PR):
  fmt          cargo fmt --check (nightly fmt; stable compile)
  clippy       cargo clippy --all-targets --all-features -- -D warnings
  test         cargo test --workspace (stable + MSRV matrix)
  opt-test     quasar opt-test conformance/ (golden diffs; mismatch = fail)
  fuzz-smoke   cargo fuzz run <target> -- -max_total_time=60 (smoke)
  bench-check  cargo bench --no-run (compile-only; avoids flaky perf CI)
  bench-regress criterion vs main baseline (wall-clock; ±10% threshold)
  audit        cargo audit (fail on WARN+)
  deny         cargo deny check (licenses + bans + sources)
  vet          cargo vet (exemptions committed)
  sbom         cargo-sbom generate (archived; not pass/fail)

Matrix: os: [ubuntu-latest, macos-latest]; rust: [stable, $MSRV]. bench-regress on ubuntu-latest/stable only (avoid OS/rustc perf noise).

Release Channels

Channel Trigger Cadence Version
nightly Push to main Daily / on merge 0.x.0-nightly.<date>
beta Tag v0.x.0-beta.N Pre-release 0.x.0-beta.N
stable Tag v0.x.0 Feature milestone 0.x.0

Stable tags trigger full release pipeline; nightly/beta skip signing + SBOM upload, run full CI.

Version Stamping

quasar-cli/build.rs embeds at compile time: GIT_SHA (short), OPTGEN_RULESET_HASH (SHA-256 of sorted .opt), BUILD_DATE (ISO-8601 from SOURCE_DATE_EPOCH), RUSTC_VERSION. Surfaced via quasar version and quasar explain --opt header.

Artifact Signing and Provenance

Mechanism Tool Applied to
Binary signing cosign (sigstore, keyless OIDC) Release binaries (all platforms)
Crate signing cargo publish (crates.io token, CI secret) Published crates
SLSA attestation slsa-github-generator (L3) Release binaries
Provenance JSON .intoto.jsonl on GitHub Release Per binary

Verify: cosign verify-blob --certificate-oidc-issuer=... quasar-linux-x86_64.

SBOM

cargo-sbom (CycloneDX JSON 1.4+); release-time → GitHub Release quasar-<version>-sbom.cdx.json; also CI workflow artifact per main build; includes all transitive deps with SPDX license IDs.

Dependency Auditing

Tool Config Failure mode
cargo-audit .cargo/audit.toml; ignore list for accepted advisories Fail on unignored WARN
cargo-deny deny.toml; licenses MIT/Apache-2.0/BSD-3; sources crates.io only Fail on denied license / unknown source
cargo-vet supply-chain/audits.toml, imports.lock Fail if dep lacks audit/exemption

cargo-vet: all direct deps need audit entry (first-party or trusted peer via imports); exemptions require justification comment.

Release Automation

cargo-release with release.toml. Steps: bump version across all Cargo.toml (workspace inheritance) → update CHANGELOG.md (keep-a-changelog) → commit → tag → cargo publish per crate in dependency order → GitHub Release via gh. Publish order topological (leaf-first): quasar-ir → quasar-props → … → quasar-cli.

Cross-Compilation Targets

Target Platform Notes
x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu Linux amd64 Primary release
aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu Linux arm64 (cross) Graviton/ARM cloud
x86_64-apple-darwin macOS Intel Dev + release
aarch64-apple-darwin macOS Apple Silicon Dev + release
x86_64-pc-windows-msvc Windows Best-effort; not in soak

Build Caching

  • GitHub Actions actions/cache on ~/.cargo/registry, ~/.cargo/git, target/ (key = Cargo.lock hash + rustc version).
  • Incremental: CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0 in CI release (avoid cache poisoning); enabled for dev.
  • sccache: optional via .cargo/config.toml for distributed builds.

Pitfalls

  • build.rs must not read outside crates/quasar-rules/rules/; cargo:rerun-if-changed must cover all .opt — missing one → silent stale generated code.
  • Non-deterministic HashMap order in optgen codegen → non-deterministic output; use BTreeMap for sorted output.
  • cosign keyless needs GitHub Actions OIDC token; local release fails — stable releases must go through CI.
  • cargo-vet imports.lock must be committed and updated per new dep; forgetting breaks CI for all contributors.

Performance Engineering

Throughput and Latency Targets (from §4)

Query class p50 p99 Memo budget
Single-table point / small range < 1 ms < 5 ms ≤ 500 MExpr
Multi-join OLTP (≤ 8 tables) < 5 ms < 20 ms ≤ 5 k MExpr
Analytical (8-16 tables, TPC-H single) < 50 ms < 200 ms ≤ 50 k MExpr
TPC-H full suite (q1-q22) batch < 2 s total — —

Latency measured from bound RelExpr input to FlowSpec output (excludes SQL parse + catalog I/O).

Every row derived from §4 System Constraints or an existing target table above (no invented numbers); each gated by a specific phase's conformance/bench suite in CI.
